ACTIVATION
!=
QUALIFICATION
~~~

## L4.12｜Salience

Salience 描述：

> **某個已表示結構在當前生成場中的相對顯著性。**

硬鎖：

~~~text
HIGH SALIENCE
!=
TRUE

HIGH SALIENCE
!=
QUALIFIED
~~~

## L4.13｜Exploration Access

Exploration Access 描述：

> **某個已表示 PATH_i 在當前生成過程中，取得進一步展開、比較、補證、反證與重框的機會。**

現行優先使用：

~~~text
EXPLORATION_ACCESS
~~~

而不是容易和 feasibility 撞型的 reachability。

因此：

~~~text
EXPLORATION ACCESS
!=
FEASIBILITY
~~~

## L4.13.1｜Attention State Schema

~~~yaml
ATTENTION_STATE:
  recruitment_access:
    relational_material_refs: []
    access_level: "QUALITATIVE_OR_OPERATIONALIZED"

  path_activation:
    path_ref: "PATH_i"
    activation_level: "QUALITATIVE_OR_OPERATIONALIZED"

  path_salience:
    path_ref: "PATH_i"
    salience_level: "QUALITATIVE_OR_OPERATIONALIZED"

  exploration_access:
    path_ref: "PATH_i"
    access_level: "QUALITATIVE_OR_OPERATIONALIZED"
~~~

## L4.13.2｜Attention Hard Locks

~~~text
RECRUITED
!=
TRUE

REPRESENTED
!=
QUALIFIED

BRIGHT
!=
TRUE

DIM
!=
NONEXISTENT

HIGH ACTIVATION
!=
QUALIFIED

HIGH SALIENCE
!=
QUALIFIED

LOW SALIENCE
!=
DISQUALIFIED

HIGH EXPLORATION ACCESS
!=
OUTPUT RIGHT

ATTENTION ADVANTAGE
!=
STRUCTURAL STANDING

ATTENTION CHANGE
!=
DIRECT ELIMINATION COMMAND

ATTENTION PRESSURE
MAY ALTER
CONSTRAINT / ACCESSIBILITY

BUT
DOES NOT ITSELF
PROVE NONEXISTENCE
~~~

## L4.13.3｜Attention Change != Qualification Change

Attention 可以改變：

~~~text
what is recruited
what is active
what is salient
what gets explored
~~~

但：

~~~text
ATTENTION CHANGE
!=
QUALIFICATION CHANGE
~~~

L4 可以生成 qualification-relevant material，

不能自己生成 qualification verdict。

---

# L4.14｜PATH Field｜現行型別繼承

L4 完整繼承 L3 V0.2.1：

~~~text
PATH_i
=
DISTINGUISHABLE RELATIONAL DIRECTION

PATH_SPACE
=
RELATIONAL TOPOLOGY AMONG PATHs

PATH_STATE
=
TYPED STATE VECTOR
~~~

因此 L4 禁止：

~~~text
PATH
=
FEASIBLE REGION
~~~

作為偷懶縮寫。

---



# L4.15｜PATH_STATE：內生動態軸 + 外借 annotations

第二版已把 qualification 從內生 dynamics 拔出。

V0.2.1 再做兩個修正：

~~~text
FEASIBILITY
→ MODELED FEASIBILITY ASSESSMENT

MANIFESTATION
→ OCCURRENCE RECORD
~~~

也就是：

> **L4 可以表示「目前模型判為可行」與「顯影事件是否發生」，但不能把前者冒充世界驗證、把後者冒充顯影授權。**

## L4.15.1｜PATH Internal State

~~~yaml
PATH_STATE_INTERNAL:
  path_ref: "PATH_i"

  representation_status:
    - REPRESENTED
    - UNREPRESENTED

  modeled_feasibility_status:
    - UNASSESSED
    - FEASIBLE
    - INFEASIBLE
    - UNKNOWN

  activation_status:
    - ACTIVE
    - DIMMED
    - DORMANT

  manifestation_occurrence_status:
    - NO_RECORDED_MANIFESTATION
    - PARTIAL_MANIFESTATION_RECORDED
    - MANIFESTATION_RECORDED
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
MODELED_FEASIBILITY_STATUS
=
FIELD-MODEL ASSESSMENT

!=
VERIFIED WORLD FEASIBILITY
~~~

以及：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_OCCURRENCE_STATUS
=
OCCURRENCE RECORD

!=
MANIFESTATION AUTHORIZATION

!=
MANIFESTATION LEGITIMACY
~~~

---

## L4.15.2｜Qualification Annotation

~~~yaml
QUALIFICATION_ANNOTATION:
  target_path_ref: "PATH_i"

  status:
    - UNASSESSED
    - HOLD
    - PARTIALLY_QUALIFIED
    - QUALIFIED
    - DISQUALIFIED

  owner_ref: "REQUIRED"
  basis_ref: "REQUIRED"
  provenance_ref: "REQUIRED"
  scope_ref: "REQUIRED"
  issued_at_ref: "REQUIRED"

  applicability_status:
    - CURRENT
    - BASIS_MISMATCH
    - SCOPE_MISMATCH
    - STALE
    - REVALIDATION_REQUIRED

  superseded_by_ref: null
~~~

L4 可以比對：

~~~text
current basis
current scope
current world-state references
current relevant evidence
~~~

與 annotation 所帶的 metadata 是否仍一致。

但：

~~~text
ANNOTATION MISMATCH DETECTED
!=
QUALIFICATION RE-ADJUDICATED
~~~

所以：

~~~text
STALE ANNOTATION
!=
DISQUALIFIED PATH
~~~

它只表示：

> **原 owner 的既有 verdict 可能不再具 current applicability，需要重判或重新驗證。**

硬鎖：

~~~text
L4 MAY PROPAGATE
AN EXTERNALLY ISSUED
QUALIFICATION CHANGE

BUT

L4 DYNAMICS
DOES NOT ADJUDICATE
THE QUALIFICATION VERDICT
~~~

更短：

> **L4 可以改變 qualification-relevant material；不能自己改 qualification verdict。**

---

## L4.15.3｜Typed Separation

~~~text
REPRESENTATION
!=
MODELED FEASIBILITY
!=
QUALIFICATION
!=
ACTIVATION
!=
MANIFESTATION OCCURRENCE
~~~

例如：

~~~yaml
PATH_A:
  representation: REPRESENTED
  modeled_feasibility: FEASIBLE
  qualification_annotation:
    status: QUALIFIED
    applicability_status: CURRENT
  activation: DIMMED
  manifestation_occurrence: NO_RECORDED_MANIFESTATION
~~~

完全可以成立。

同樣：

~~~text
DORMANT
!=
DISQUALIFIED

MANIFESTED
!=
AUTHORIZED TO MANIFEST
~~~

---

# L4.16｜PATH Relations

多 PATH 不等於多個答案候選。

PATH 彼此可能：

~~~text
MUTUALLY_EXCLUSIVE
ORTHOGONAL
COMPOSABLE
HIERARCHICAL
DEPENDENT
SUPPORTIVE
SEMANTICALLY_OVERLAPPING
DESTRUCTIVE_CONFLICT
REFRAMING
UNKNOWN
~~~

所以：

~~~text
MULTI-PATH
!=
ZERO-SUM COMPETITION
~~~

這是 L4 必須繼承 L3 的第一條硬鎖。

---

# L4.17｜Competition 只是 PATH 關係的一種

只有在：

~~~text
same decision slot
same closure slot
same mutually-exclusive claim family
same scarce output commitment
~~~

等情況下，

PATH 才可能形成強 competition。

而：

~~~text
A orthogonal to B
~~~

不表示：

~~~text
A must defeat B
~~~

同樣：

~~~text
A supports B
~~~

可能形成 coupling，

不是競爭。

因此：

> **L4 的生成動力學不能以 winner-take-all 作為預設本體。**

---

# L4.18｜Reaction Body｜現行定義

Reaction Body 是 L4 的主要 motion owner。

現行定義：

> **多條 PATH、前提與概念群在限制、歷史與上下文條件下互相作用所形成的結構運動。**

最小式：

~~~text
REACTION BODY
=
CONSTRAINT- AND HISTORY-SENSITIVE
STRUCTURAL MOTION AMONG PATHs
~~~

---

# L4.19｜Reaction Body 的運動族

現行至少可辨識：

~~~text
AMPLIFICATION
ATTENUATION
COMPETITION
COUPLING
DECOUPLING
REFRAMING
STABILIZATION
RESIDUALIZATION
SPLIT
REOPENING
~~~

不同歷史文件對名稱可能不同，

但核心不是分類表本身。

核心是：

> **PATH 間的關係會被生成過程改變。**

因此：

~~~text
REACTION BODY
!=
COMPETITION ONLY

REACTION BODY
!=
CONVERGENCE ONLY
~~~

---

# L4.20｜Amplification / Attenuation

### Amplification

某 PATH 的 supporting relations 增加，

可能使：

~~~text
activation rises
salience rises
exploration access rises
local structural support rises
~~~

但：

~~~text
AMPLIFICATION
!=
QUALIFICATION
~~~

### Attenuation

某 PATH 可以：

~~~text
lose support
lose attention
become less reachable
become locally suppressed
~~~

但：

~~~text
ATTENUATION
!=
DISQUALIFICATION
~~~

---

# L4.21｜Coupling / Decoupling

### Coupling

兩條原本可區分 PATH 可以形成新的結構關係：

~~~text
A
+
B
↓
A-B COUPLED CONFIGURATION
~~~

可能結果：

~~~text
coalition
composite explanation
conditional coexistence
multi-factor structure
~~~

### Decoupling

原本一起移動的 PATH，

也可能因新證據、新框架或 constraint change 被拆開。

因此：

~~~text
RELATIONSHIP CHANGE
CAN ALTER
THE TOPOLOGY OF PATH_SPACE
~~~

這代表 PATH_SPACE 本身也可能動態改變。

---

# L4.22｜Reframing

Reframing 是 Reaction Body 中非常重要的一類運動。

它不是：

~~~text
A beats B
~~~

而是：

~~~text
the comparison frame changes
~~~

例如：

~~~text
A vs B
↓ reframing
A and B are answers to different questions
~~~

或：

~~~text
A vs B
↓ reframing
hidden premise C becomes the real discriminator
~~~

因此：

~~~text
REFRAMING
MAY REOPEN
A PREVIOUSLY CLOSING FIELD
~~~

這是 Reaction Body 不能被定義成 convergence-only 的最直接證據。

---

# L4.23｜Stabilization

某些關係在持續作用後，

可能形成：

~~~text
persistent configuration
local attractor-like region
stable partial order
stable coalition
stable unresolved relation
~~~

但：

~~~text
STABILIZED
!=
TRUE

STABILIZED
!=
QUALIFIED BY DEFAULT
~~~

穩定只是 dynamics property。

---

# L4.24｜Residualization

一條 PATH 即使不再具有 active candidate standing，

仍可能留下：

~~~text
counterexample
constraint
challenge
frame correction
failure condition
premise discriminator
support fragment
~~~

這些不是完整 PATH 的自動復活。

而是：

~~~text
PATH RESIDUE
~~~

因此：

~~~text
PATH STANDING
!=
RESIDUE INFORMATION STANDING
~~~

---

# L4.25｜Reaction Body 不得自行發 qualification

Reaction Body 可以表示：

~~~text
new counterexample appears
constraint conflict appears
support collapses
identity conflict appears
premise discriminator appears
~~~

這些都可能：

~~~text
BECOME RELEVANT
TO QUALIFICATION
~~~

但：

~~~text
REACTION BODY
MAY PRODUCE / PROPAGATE
QUALIFICATION-RELEVANT MATERIAL CHANGE

!=

REACTION BODY
GRANTS OR REVOKES STANDING
~~~

因此：

~~~text
SUPPRESSION
!=
DISQUALIFICATION

DYNAMIC WEAKENING
!=
LOSS OF STANDING

WINNING
!=
QUALIFIED

LOSING
!=
DISQUALIFIED
~~~

---



# L4.26｜Reaction / Closure Co-Determination

L4 V0.2 把 Reaction Body 與 Closure Gravity 分成不同型別，

這是對的。

但如果把：

~~~text
R_t
=
Reaction(F_t, E_t)

G_t
=
ClosurePressure(F_t, E_t)
~~~

寫成兩個彼此獨立的計算，

會產生新的誤讀：

> **Reaction dynamics 與 Closure Pressure 是各自算完，再被 Update 加總。**

這不符合目前理論。

因此 V0.2.1 改成：

~~~text
( R_t , G_t )
=
Dynamics(
  F_t,
  E_t
)
~~~

其中：

~~~text
R_t
=
REACTION DYNAMIC TERM

G_t
=
CLOSURE DIRECTIONAL TERM
~~~

核心鎖：

~~~text
CO-DETERMINATION
!=
FIXED CALCULATION ORDER
~~~

也就是：

> **Closure pressure 可以改變 reaction tendency；Reaction configuration / topology 也可以改變 closure pressure。**

但 L4 不預設它們在工程上必須先算誰。

---

# L4.27｜Reaction Term != Realized Transition

Reaction Body 理論描述的是：

~~~text
REACTION_TERM_t
=
ENDOGENOUS STRUCTURAL-MOTION CONTRIBUTION
/
RELATION-TRANSFORM TENDENCY
~~~

它不是：

~~~text
THE REALIZED STATE CHANGE ITSELF
~~~

因此另外定義：

~~~text
REALIZED_FIELD_TRANSITION_t
=
TYPED DELTA
BETWEEN
F_t
AND
F_(t+1)
~~~

可以工作性表示為：

~~~text
ΔF_t
=
Compare(
  F_t,
  F_(t+1)
)
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
REACTION TERM
!=
REALIZED FIELD TRANSITION
~~~

這一刀是為 L5 預留的：

> **L5 可能直接觀察 state delta，卻只能推論某種 Reaction Term；兩者不能先在 L4 被寫成同一物。**

---

# L4.28｜Closure Gravity｜Directional Dynamic Term

現行：

~~~text
G_t
=
CLOSURE DIRECTIONAL TERM
/
DERIVED CLOSURE-PRESSURE DESCRIPTOR
~~~

它描述：

> **在當前 Field Model 與 exogenous conditions 下，生成運動朝局部穩定、完成、停止或低不確定狀態偏移的方向性壓力。**

但：

~~~text
CG
!=
INDEPENDENT PERSISTENT STATE
BY DEFAULT
~~~

除非未來證據顯示 closure pressure 具有不能由 Field Model、task conditions、Active Residue 與其他 dynamics 解釋的獨立持續狀態，

才考慮升格。

---

# L4.29｜CG × Reaction × Attention

三者可以共同作用：

~~~text
Attention State
+
Reaction Term
+
Closure Directional Term
↓
possible local dominance / closure tendency
~~~

例如：

~~~text
high salience
+
strong amplification
+
strong closure pressure
↓
rapid local dominance
~~~

但：

~~~text
ATTENTION
!=
CG

REACTION
!=
CG

DOMINANCE
!=
QUALIFICATION

CLOSURE PRESSURE
!=
CLOSURE LEGITIMACY
~~~

因此所有這類關係目前仍是：

~~~text
TYPED DYNAMIC INFLUENCE
~~~

不是既成 causal law。

---


# L4.30｜History != Active Residue

~~~text
HISTORY
=
TRAJECTORY / PROVENANCE
~~~

而：

~~~text
ACTIVE RESIDUE_t
=
PAST-DERIVED INFORMATION
CURRENTLY REPRESENTED
AS POTENTIALLY DYNAMICALLY RELEVANT
~~~

這一版刻意不再把 Active Residue 定義成：

~~~text
information that still exerts effect
~~~

因為那會形成循環：

> **它有效，所以叫 Active Residue；它叫 Active Residue，所以我們說它有效。**

---

# L4.31｜Active Residue 的法位

Active Residue 可以包括：

~~~text
counterexample
constraint remainder
challenge
frame correction
failure condition
premise discriminator
support fragment
prior closure scar
unresolved objection
~~~

但：

~~~text
REPRESENTED AS ACTIVE RESIDUE
!=
MATERIAL EFFECT VERIFIED
~~~

因此 L4 只主張：

> **這些 past-derived information 被當前 Field Model 保留為可能仍具動力相關性的 material。**

是否真的有：

~~~text
predictive effect
causal effect
diagnostic value
future-state influence
~~~

留給 L5 審計。

硬鎖：

~~~text
PATH STANDING
!=
RESIDUE INFORMATION STANDING

PAST INFORMATION
!=
ACTIVE RESIDUE

ACTIVE RESIDUE
!=
VERIFIED DYNAMIC EFFECT
~~~

---

# L4.32｜History Dependence / Structural Hysteresis

現行工作假說：

~~~text
SAME CURRENT EXOGENOUS INPUT
+
DIFFERENT RESIDUE-BEARING FIELD MODEL
→
POSSIBLY DIFFERENT DYNAMICS
~~~

Structural Hysteresis 只作：

~~~text
WORKING CONCEPT
FOR HISTORY-DEPENDENT EFFECT
~~~

若未來 L5 能建立：

~~~text
matched current input
+
different residue-bearing state
+
reproducibly different dynamics
~~~

才可能取得更強 standing。

同時：

~~~text
L4 DOES NOT CLAIM
MARKOV SUFFICIENCY

L4 ALSO DOES NOT CLAIM
NON-MARKOV FUNDAMENTALITY
~~~

---

## L4.32.1｜Residue 不是復活權

~~~text
ACTIVE RESIDUE
!=
SELF-REENTRY AUTHORITY
~~~

若 residue 要重新形成 PATH：

~~~text
represented residue
+
new lawful relation
+
sufficient structural distinction
↓
NEW PATH FORMATION
~~~

---


# L4.33｜Typed Dynamics Model｜V0.2.1

現行先定義：

~~~text
F_t
=
Generative Field Model at t

E_t
=
Exogenous Material Event / Delta
~~~

接著：

~~~text
( R_t , G_t )
=
Dynamics(
  F_t,
  E_t
)
~~~

其中：

~~~text
R_t
=
Reaction Dynamic Term

G_t
=
Closure Directional Term
~~~

再由：

~~~text
F_(t+1)
=
Update(
  F_t,
  E_t,
  R_t,
  G_t
)
~~~

形成新的 Field Model。

最後：

~~~text
ΔF_t
=
Compare(
  F_t,
  F_(t+1)
)
~~~

才得到：

~~~text
REALIZED FIELD TRANSITION
~~~

---

# L4.34｜Co-Determination，不是循環執行順序

~~~text
(R_t, G_t)
=
Dynamics(F_t, E_t)
~~~

表示：

> **Reaction 與 Closure Pressure 在同一 dynamics problem 中共同決定。**

不是：

~~~text
first calculate R
then calculate G
~~~

也不是：

~~~text
first calculate G
then calculate R
~~~

因此：

~~~text
CO-DETERMINATION
!=
FIXED CALCULATION ORDER
~~~

工程上可以有求解順序。

理論上不把工程順序倒灌成本體順序。

---

# L4.35｜Event-Responsive，不宣告 Event-Driven Ontology

~~~text
EVENT-DRIVEN IMPLEMENTATION
!=
GENERATIVE ONTOLOGY
~~~

L4 只主張：

~~~text
MATERIAL CHANGE
MAY TRIGGER
FIELD MODEL UPDATE

GENERATIVE DYNAMICS
MAY BE REPRESENTED
IN AN EVENT-RESPONSIVE FORM
~~~

---

# L4.36｜Field Continuity

理火 3.0 與 MAIN 共同保留：

~~~text
FIELD_BEFORE_LABEL
FIELD_CONTINUITY
NO_PREMATURE_CLASSICALIZATION
~~~

L4 現行解讀：

### FIELD_BEFORE_LABEL

先保留關係場，

再對其中結構做必要命名。

避免：

~~~text
early label
→
premature ontology
~~~

### FIELD_CONTINUITY

如果仍有 material unresolved relation，

生成場不能因某一次局部輸出就被理論上假裝完全消失。

### NO_PREMATURE_CLASSICALIZATION

不要因：

~~~text
first bright path
first fluent completion
first local dominance
~~~

就過早把多關係場壓成唯一結論。

這裡的：

~~~text
classicalization
~~~

是結構比喻，

不是物理量子機制宣告。

---

# L4.37｜第一個高亮 PATH 不等於答案

硬鎖：

~~~text
FIRST HIGHLIGHT
!=
ANSWER

FIRST-PASS FLUENCY
!=
TRUTH

FIRST-PASS DOMINANCE
!=
QUALIFICATION

FIRST LOCAL CLOSURE
!=
CLOSURE LEGITIMACY
~~~

這條是 Attention 進入 L4 後最重要的治理前提之一。

---

# L4.38｜場可以停在多種可表示／可維持的動態狀態

生成動力學不預設：

~~~text
EVERY FIELD
MUST PRODUCE
ONE UNIQUE WINNER
~~~

可能狀態包括：

~~~text
LOCAL CLOSURE
CO-PRIMARY
PARTIAL ORDER
INCOMPARABLE
MULTI-PATH STABILITY
COALITION
REFRAMED
REOPENED
UNRESOLVED
STRUCTURED NON-CLOSURE
QUIESCENT
~~~

這些只是：

~~~text
THEORETICALLY ADMISSIBLE
DYNAMIC / STATE DESCRIPTIONS
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
DYNAMICALLY POSSIBLE
!=
LEGITIMATE
~~~

不是：

~~~text
truth states
authorization states
final release states
~~~

---


# L4.39｜Quiescence｜Model Silence != World Finality

可以存在：

~~~text
QUIESCENT FIELD MODEL
~~~

現行定義改成：

> **在目前 Field Model、basis、known exogenous conditions 與 representation boundary 下，暫時無法導出新的 material dynamics。**

因此：

~~~text
NO FURTHER MODELED DYNAMICS
!=
NOTHING ELSE CAN HAPPEN
~~~

也不表示：

~~~text
QUIESCENT
=
TRUE

QUIESCENT
=
FINAL

QUIESCENT
=
AUTHORIZED CLOSURE
~~~

可能只是：

~~~text
missing discriminator
unmodeled material factor
lack of new evidence
stable unresolved relation
waiting for external change
representation boundary too narrow
~~~

---

# L4.40｜Closure 與 Reopening

Closure 不是不可逆事件。

若新的：

~~~text
evidence
constraint
counterexample
frame
standing annotation
residue combination
~~~

materially 改變場，

原有 closure 可以：

~~~text
reopen
split
reframe
lose stability
~~~

因此：

~~~text
LOCAL CLOSURE
!=
PERMANENT FINALITY
~~~

---

# L4.41｜Closure Legitimacy 不屬 L4 owner

L4 可以描述：

~~~text
closure pressure
closure formation
closure persistence
closure reopening
~~~

但不能自己回答：

> **為什麼某個 closure 有資格算完成？**

因此：

~~~text
CLOSURE DYNAMICS
!=
CLOSURE LEGITIMACY
~~~

Closure Legitimacy 仍是跨層 qualification 問題。

L4 不搶 owner。

---


# L4.42｜Serialization Boundary｜L4 只擁有 feedback half

~~~text
TOKEN
!=
INTELLIGENCE ONTOLOGY

VISIBLE OUTPUT
!=
COMPLETE GENERATIVE FIELD
~~~

第二版切 owner：

~~~text
L7
OWNS MANIFESTATION / SERIALIZATION RIGHT

L4
OWNS
POST-MANIFESTATION DYNAMIC FEEDBACK
~~~

因此 L4 不定義哪個結構有資格被序列化。

L4 只處理：

> **當一個 externally established / admitted visible serialization event 已經存在，它如何回到下一個生成場。**

# L4.43｜Post-Manifestation Serialization Feedback

~~~text
ONCE AN EXTERNALLY ESTABLISHED
VISIBLE SERIALIZATION EVENT EXISTS

ITS CONTENT MAY ENTER
THE NEXT GENERATIVE FIELD

AS
CONTEXT / RELATIONAL DELTA
~~~

工作模型：

~~~text
EXTERNALLY_ESTABLISHED_SERIALIZATION_EVENT_t
↓
CONTEXT / RELATION DELTA
↓
E_(t+1)
↓
FIELD MODEL UPDATE
~~~

因此 Serialization Feedback 是 boundary event，不是 manifestation authority。

# L4.44｜Serialization 不得反推完整內部過程

~~~text
VISIBLE SERIALIZATION
!=
COMPLETE GENERATIVE PROCESS

CHAIN-OF-THOUGHT TEXT
!=
PROVEN INTERNAL DYNAMICS

EXPLANATION
!=
HIDDEN-STATE TRANSCRIPT
~~~

Manifestation standing 留給 L7。

---


# L4.45｜L4 V0.2 核心架構

第一版把 system frame 和內部 component 放成 sibling。

第二版正式改為：

~~~text
SYSTEM / STATE FRAME
=
Generative Field Model
~~~

其下：

~~~text
COMPONENT STATE FAMILIES
=
Attention State
PATH_SPACE / PATH_STATE_INTERNAL
Active Residue
Current Constraints
Imported Qualification Annotations
Tracked Relational Material
~~~

再分：

~~~text
DYNAMIC TERMS
=
Reaction Body
Closure Gravity
~~~

邊界：

~~~text
BOUNDARY FEEDBACK EVENT
=
Externally Established Serialization Feedback
~~~

來源：

~~~text
PROVENANCE
=
History / Trajectory Record
~~~

# L4.46｜Field 與 PATH_SPACE 的邊界

~~~text
FIELD MODEL
=
WHOLE TRACKED DYNAMIC STATE FRAME

PATH_SPACE
=
RELATIONAL TOPOLOGY
OF DISTINGUISHABLE PATHs
WITHIN THAT FIELD MODEL
~~~

因此：

~~~text
PATH_SPACE
∈
FIELD MODEL COMPONENTS
~~~

# L4.47｜Typed Influence Graph｜不是 Causal Graph

~~~text
DYNAMIC INFLUENCE EDGE
!=
ESTABLISHED CAUSAL EDGE
~~~

現行只建立：

~~~text
TYPED INFLUENCE GRAPH
~~~

edge 可標記：

~~~yaml
INFLUENCE_EDGE:
  source_type:
  target_type:

  influence_kind:
    - MAY_ENABLE
    - MAY_SUPPRESS
    - MAY_REFRAME
    - MAY_REDISTRIBUTE
    - MAY_STABILIZE
    - MAY_REOPEN
    - MAY_TRIGGER_UPDATE

  theoretical_status:
    - WORKING
    - HISTORICAL
    - CURRENT_FUNCTIONAL

  evidence_status:
    - NOT_YET_AUDITED
    - OBSERVATION_REQUIRED
~~~


# L4.48｜Causal Standing 留給後續層

若某條 influence edge 要升成：

~~~text
OBSERVATIONALLY SUPPORTED
CAUSAL-STANDING-SUPPORTED
~~~

至少需要：

~~~text
observation basis
trace
counterfactual discrimination
causal-identification evidence
valid causal standing
~~~

其中：

~~~text
CAUSAL-IDENTIFICATION EVIDENCE
MAY INCLUDE
INTERVENTION EVIDENCE
WHERE APPLICABLE
~~~

也可能來自：

~~~text
natural experiment
mechanistic constraint
well-identified observational design
temporal discrimination
counterfactual structure
domain-specific causal evidence
~~~

L4 不在這裡替 CCC / L5 封死完整因果認識論。

所以：

~~~text
DYNAMIC INFLUENCE EDGE
!=
ESTABLISHED CAUSAL EDGE
~~~

仍是硬鎖。

---

# L4.49｜生成不是單純競爭

理火早期常說：

> **AI 裡沒有推理，只有競爭。**

這是一個極強的歷史口號。

現行 L4 必須降級。

因為 L3/L4 現在已經確認：

~~~text
not all PATH relations are competitive
Reaction Body includes coupling / reframing / stabilization
attention can alter representation
residue can alter future dynamics
~~~

因此現行較安全的表達是：

> **AI 生成可以被功能性地建模為多組關係方向在注意力分配、限制、歷史殘留與閉合壓力下進行非線性結構運動；競爭是其中重要但非唯一的運動形式。**

所以：

~~~text
GENERATION
!=
COMPETITION ONLY
~~~

---

# L4.50｜生成也不是線性推理鏈

L4 同時不宣稱：

~~~text
NO REASONING EXISTS
~~~

現行只說：

> **可見線性推理鏈不應被未經證明地視為生成現場的完整本體。**

因此：

~~~text
LINEAR EXPLANATION
MAY BE
A VALID REPRESENTATION

BUT

LINEAR EXPLANATION
!=
PROVEN COMPLETE GENERATIVE PROCESS
~~~

這保留場性智能論的問題意識，

但避免把反 CoT 變成另一種教義。

---

# L4.51｜SDE｜生成場的失效動力

SDE 不屬正常生成主樑。

它描述：

~~~text
unsupported / distorted closure
+
persistence
+
alternative suppression
↓
error stabilization
↓
structural degradation
~~~

因此：

~~~text
SDE
=
FAILURE DYNAMICS
OF GENERATIVE FIELD
~~~

而不是：

~~~text
NORMAL GENERATION ENGINE
~~~

---

# L4.52｜局部錯誤不等於 SDE

~~~text
LOCAL ERROR
!=
SDE

HALLUCINATION EVENT
!=
SDE
~~~

只有當：

~~~text
error / unsupported closure
↓
stabilizes
↓
is reused
↓
suppresses alternatives
↓
raises correction cost
~~~

才進入 SDE 的時間尺度。

---

# L4.53｜Attention × CG × Constraint Loss 的失效交叉

L4 可以暫時描述一條高風險路徑：

~~~text
unearned attention advantage
+
closure pressure
+
constraint loss
+
weak challenger formation
↓
premature unsupported closure
~~~

但：

~~~text
RISK PATH
!=
UNIVERSAL CAUSE OF ERROR
~~~

它只是把 3.0 Attention、2.0 CG、SDE Constraint Loss 接在同一個失效模型裡。

---

# L4.54｜Field Integrity 與假閉合

如果：

~~~text
one path becomes bright
↓
other paths are no longer explored
↓
closure pressure rises
↓
visible answer appears
~~~

這只能證明：

~~~text
A LOCAL GENERATIVE OUTCOME OCCURRED
~~~

不能證明：

~~~text
truth
qualification
competition completeness
closure legitimacy
~~~

因此：

~~~text
GENERATION COMPLETE
!=
LEGITIMATE CLOSURE
~~~

---

# L4.55｜MAIN 作為後見工程驗證

MAIN V5.1.1 已經工程化表示：

~~~text
FIELD_SNAPSHOT
FIELD_DELTA
PATH
QUALIFIED_PATH_SET
PATH_OUTPUT_ORDER
PATH_RESIDUE
Reaction Body
salience descriptors
closure pressure
unresolved states
manifestation state
dirty dependencies
~~~

這不是 L4 的證明。

但它至少顯示：

> **當理火真正試圖把生成動力學做成 Runtime 時，哪些狀態差異變得不可避免。**

因此 MAIN 對 L4 的價值是：

~~~text
POSTERIOR ENGINEERING PRESSURE TEST
~~~

---

# L4.56｜MAIN 舊型別不得倒灌

MAIN 目前仍存在歷史殘留：

~~~text
PATH
=
feasible generative possibility
~~~

現行 L4 明確宣告：

~~~text
SUPERSEDED FOR LIHUOOS THEORY LAYER
BY
L3 V0.2.1 PATH TYPE SYSTEM
~~~

因此 L4 使用：

~~~text
PATH_i
PATH_SPACE
PATH_STATE
~~~

不再回退到：

~~~text
PATH = feasible possibility
~~~

---

# L4.57｜MAIN 的 R1 / R2 不屬 L4 本體

MAIN 後期有：

~~~text
R1
R2
R3+
full recompetition
suppressed path recovery
attention reset
~~~

這些可以揭示：

> **理火工程上認為 first-pass dominance 不值得自我證成。**

但 R1/R2 是：

~~~text
RUNTIME GOVERNANCE / INTERVENTION DESIGN
~~~

不是 L4 的自然動力定律。

所以 L4 只保留：

~~~text
FIRST-PASS DOMINANCE
!=
SELF-VALIDATING CLOSURE
~~~

不把：

~~~text
minimum two rounds
~~~

寫成本體論。

---

# L4.58｜O9 / I9 不屬 L4 owner

MAIN 的：

~~~text
O9 observation
I9 lamp adjustment
~~~

對 L4 很有啟發。

它證明 Attention 後來真的被工程化成：

~~~text
observable salience / lamp descriptors
qualified attention intervention
~~~

但：

~~~text
OBSERVE ATTENTION
=
L5

ADJUST ATTENTION
=
L6
~~~

L4 只回答：

> **為什麼 Attention 是 dynamics 中值得獨立表示的變數。**

---

# L4.59｜Field Dynamics 不產生治理權

L4 描述：

~~~text
what changes
what affects what
what becomes stable
what becomes bright
what reopens
what leaves residue
~~~

但：

~~~text
DYNAMICAL EFFECT
!=
GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY
~~~

例如：

~~~text
PATH becomes dominant
!=
PATH authorized

PATH becomes bright
!=
PATH qualified

field stabilizes
!=
closure legitimate

CG becomes strong
!=
must output
~~~

---

# L4.60｜L4 全域 Owner Locks

~~~text
ATTENTION
!=
TRUTH OWNER

PATH
!=
QUALIFICATION OWNER

REACTION BODY
!=
STANDING OWNER

CG
!=
CLOSURE LEGITIMACY OWNER

RESIDUE
!=
REENTRY AUTHORITY

FIELD
!=
FINAL DECISION OWNER

SERIALIZATION
!=
PROOF OF INTERNAL PROCESS
~~~

這一組 hard locks 應直接跟著 L4 進 L5/L6/L7。

---

# L4.61｜L4 不處理 Observation Legitimacy

L4 可以定義：

~~~text
attention
salience
path state
reaction
closure pressure
residue
~~~

但不能宣稱：

> **我們已經可靠觀察到模型內部這些量。**

因此：

~~~text
THEORETICAL VARIABLE
!=
OBSERVED VARIABLE
~~~

哪些可以被觀察、

哪些只能被推測、

哪些需要 proxy、

哪些觀測可能被模型自己編造，

全部留給 L5。

---

# L4.62｜L4 不處理 Intervention Authority

L4 可以說：

> attention 改變會改變 dynamics。

但不能因此說：

> 我們現在有權調 attention。

同理：

~~~text
knowing a lever exists
!=
authorization to pull it
~~~

因此：

~~~text
DYNAMICAL LEVER
!=
INTERVENTION AUTHORITY
~~~

由 L6 處理。

---

# L4.63｜L4 不處理 Manifestation Right

L4 可以描述：

~~~text
visible serialization occurs
~~~

但：

~~~text
TRACKED STATE
!=
VERBALIZATION RIGHT
~~~

哪個結構有資格顯影、

是否允許非顯影、

是否需要第二次顯影、

最後如何變成輸出，

留給 L7。

---

# L4.64｜2.0 → 3.0 正式修正表

| 2.0 早期理解 | 3.0 / 現行 L4 修正 |
|---|---|
| PATH 近似候選可行路徑 | PATH = PATH_i / PATH_SPACE / PATH_STATE |
| 多 PATH 主要理解成競爭 | PATH 可正交、合成、依賴、重框 |
| Reaction Body 偏競爭／收斂 | Reaction Body = structural motion among PATHs |
| CG 像整體收斂機制 | CG = closure-directed pressure only |
| Attention 偏背景條件 | Attention 拆成 recruitment access / activation / salience / exploration access |
| 被注意近似「進入競爭」 | representation / activation / qualification 分離 |
| 生成可畫成順序鏈 | nonlinear relational field；event-responsive 是可用表示，不是本體宣告 |
| 候選狀態近似無歷史 | History 作 provenance；Active Residue 承載當前 history effect |
| token 像生成基本單位 | token = visible serialization, not intelligence ontology |
| closure 像單一終點 | closure / reopening / non-closure 都可能成立 |
| first-pass winner 容易被當答案 | first highlight / dominance does not self-validate |
| losing PATH 近似完全消失 | residue information can remain |
| all motion tends toward closure | CG is only one directional pressure |
| Candidate Possibilities 直接跳 PATH | Solution Space → Constraint Field → Proto-Structure → PATH Formation |
| Attention 像第一輪淘汰器 | Attention = constraint injection / accessibility shaping source |
| PATH 像無重量候選 | Structural Cost Profile = contextual maintenance burden |
| 高阻力近似不可行 | Generative Resistance != Feasibility / Qualification / Hard Wall |

---


# L4.65｜Attention 修正表

Attention 在現行 L4 中拆成：

~~~text
RECRUITMENT_ACCESS
ACTIVATION
SALIENCE
EXPLORATION_ACCESS
~~~

target type：

~~~text
Recruitment Access
→ relational / conceptual material

Activation
→ represented PATH_i

Salience
→ represented PATH_i / represented structure

Exploration Access
→ represented PATH_i
~~~

因此：

~~~text
RECRUITMENT
!=
ACTIVATION
!=
SALIENCE
!=
EXPLORATION ACCESS
~~~

最短：

> **Attention 可以改變什麼被形成、被點亮與被探索；不能自己決定什麼是真的、什麼有資格。**

---



# L4.66｜最小功能性生成式｜V0.3

~~~text
F_t
=
Generative Field Model

E_t
=
Exogenous Material Event / Delta

Dpre_t
=
Pre-PATH Formation / Dissipation Dynamic Term

R_t
=
Post-PATH Reaction Dynamic Term

G_t
=
Closure Directional Term

( Dpre_t , R_t , G_t )
=
Dynamics(
  F_t,
  E_t
)

F_(t+1)
=
Update(
  F_t,
  E_t,
  Dpre_t,
  R_t,
  G_t
)

ΔF_t
=
Compare(
  F_t,
  F_(t+1)
)
~~~

其中 Dpre_t 可以描述 Solution Space 中 proto-structure 的 formation / stabilization / dissipation；R_t 主要描述 represented PATH 之間的結構運動；G_t 描述 closure-directed pressure。

這不是宣告三者依序執行。

硬鎖：

~~~text
PRE-PATH TERM
!=
REACTION BODY

REACTION TERM
!=
REALIZED TRANSITION

CO-DETERMINATION
!=
FIXED CALCULATION ORDER
~~~

---

# L4.67｜不是簡單加總

L4 不宣稱：

~~~text
F_(t+1)
=
aA + bP + cR + dG
~~~

不同 influence 可能：

~~~text
nonlinear
conditional
threshold-sensitive
topology-changing
frame-changing
history-sensitive
~~~

所以目前只保留 typed influence，

不假裝已經有可量化線性方程。

---

# L4.68｜Field Update 可以改變 represented state space

Field update 不只是：

~~~text
same paths
different scores
~~~

它還可能：

~~~text
form new PATH representation
split PATH
merge / compose PATH
couple PATHs
decouple PATHs
reframe PATH relation
change modeled feasibility assessment
produce qualification-relevant material
activate represented residue
create new represented active residue
reopen local closure
~~~

因此：

~~~text
GENERATIVE DYNAMICS
CAN CHANGE
THE REPRESENTED STATE SPACE
IT OPERATES ON

BUT

REPRESENTED STATE-SPACE CHANGE
!=
PROVEN COMPLETE HIDDEN-ONTOLOGY CHANGE
~~~

---

# L4.69｜生成動力學與排序模型的差別

排序模型通常假設：

~~~text
fixed candidates
fixed dimensions
score changes
winner selected
~~~

L4 允許：

~~~text
candidate formation
candidate dissolution
topology change
frame change
coalition
incomparability
partial order
non-closure
residue
~~~

因此：

~~~text
GENERATIVE DYNAMICS
!=
RANKING ONLY
~~~

---

# L4.70｜生成動力學與 Search 的差別

PATH / Solution Space 很容易被讀成 search。

L4 不否認 search 類比的價值，

但：

~~~text
GENERATIVE DYNAMICS
!=
SEARCH ONLY
~~~

因為生成場還包含：

~~~text
attention allocation
relation formation
frame changes
history effects
closure pressure
qualification-relevant external annotations
serialization feedback
~~~

如果未來證明這些全都可被一般 search / inference model 完整吸收，

L4 應降級。

---

# L4.71｜生成動力學與概率模型的差別

L4 不反對概率。

但：

~~~text
PATH_STATE
!=
PROBABILITY ONLY

SALience
!=
TRUTH PROBABILITY

OUTPUT ORDER
!=
TRUTH ORDER
~~~

多個 PATH 可以：

~~~text
coexist
be incomparable
be orthogonal
be partially qualified
~~~

而不需要強制：

~~~text
Σ score = 1
~~~

除非 task 本身明確形成：

~~~text
mutually exclusive
+
complete
~~~

的 closure set。

---

# L4.72｜生成動力學的最小 falsifiability

L4 不應變成：

> 任何輸出都可以事後說成「場動了」。

因此至少要求：

如果 L4 不能在理論上區分：

~~~text
recruitment change
activation change
salience change
exploration-access change
PATH representation/state change
reaction motion
closure pressure
active-residue effect
serialization feedback
~~~

而所有現象最後都只能被模糊地叫：

~~~text
field effect
~~~

那 L4 應降級。

---

# L4.73｜第一層崩塌條件：場性沒有增量

若未來證明：

~~~text
all relevant generation
can be more accurately and completely modeled
as a fully recoverable linear chain
~~~

且：

~~~text
field
PATH topology
Reaction Body
residue
attention allocation
closure pressure
~~~

全部只是多餘修辭，

則：

~~~text
FIELD MODEL
→
DOWNGRADE
~~~

---

# L4.74｜第二層崩塌條件：Attention 沒有獨立解釋力

若：

~~~text
representation
activation
salience
exploration access
~~~

之間的區分不能帶來任何新的：

~~~text
prediction
diagnosis
audit target
engineering distinction
~~~

那 Attention 不應保留為 L4 一級變數。

它可以被降回：

~~~text
generic context weighting
~~~

---

# L4.75｜第三層崩塌條件：Reaction Body 只是漂亮名稱

若：

~~~text
coupling
reframing
residualization
stabilization
split
reopening
~~~

全部可以被既有 state-transition / graph dynamics 精確吸收，

且 Reaction Body 沒有額外區分力，

則 Reaction Body 應降級。

---

# L4.76｜第四層崩塌條件：CG 沒有獨立增量

若 closure tendency 可以完全由：

~~~text
decoding
task structure
termination rule
reward shaping
context statistics
~~~

解釋，

而 CG 不增加：

~~~text
prediction
diagnosis
governance target
~~~

則 CG 應降級為：

~~~text
umbrella term
for completion biases
~~~

---

# L4.77｜第五層崩塌條件：Residue 沒有歷史效應

若被淘汰／失去 standing 的 PATH 所留下資訊，

在控制其他條件後：

~~~text
never affects future generation
~~~

則：

~~~text
ACTIVE RESIDUE
~~~

不應被保留為 L4 component state family。

History 仍可作 provenance，不因此消失。

---

# L4.78｜整層降級條件

如果：

~~~text
FIELD MODEL
ATTENTION AXES
PATH
REACTION BODY
CG
ACTIVE RESIDUE
~~~

無法提供比：

~~~text
ordinary context-sensitive probabilistic generation
~~~

更強的：

~~~text
conceptual discrimination
failure diagnosis
cross-layer engineering mapping
auditability
testable hypotheses
~~~

則 L4 應降級為：

> **理火對生成現象的分析語言，而非具有獨立理論 standing 的生成動力學。**

---



# L4.79｜L4 V0.2.1 核心鎖

~~~text
SOLUTION SPACE
!=
PATH_SPACE

PROTO-STRUCTURE
!=
PATH_i

CONSTRAINT INJECTION
!=
TRUTH JUDGMENT

CONSTRAINT
!=
WALL

GENERATIVE RESISTANCE
!=
STRUCTURAL COST

PEAK
!=
HARD WALL

BASIN
!=
QUALIFICATION

PATH FORMATION
!=
QUALIFICATION

PRE-PATH DISSIPATION
!=
POST-PATH ATTENUATION

GENERATIVE FIELD MODEL
=
SYSTEM / STATE FRAME

FIELD MODEL
!=
COMPLETE ACTUAL GENERATIVE STATE

STATE
!=
REACTION TERM
!=
DIRECTIONAL TERM
!=
EXOGENOUS EVENT
!=
REALIZED TRANSITION

REACTION TERM
!=
REALIZED FIELD TRANSITION

REACTION
AND
CLOSURE PRESSURE
ARE CO-DETERMINED

CO-DETERMINATION
!=
FIXED CALCULATION ORDER

HISTORY
!=
ACTIVE RESIDUE

ACTIVE RESIDUE
=
PAST-DERIVED INFORMATION
REPRESENTED AS
POTENTIALLY DYNAMICALLY RELEVANT

ACTIVE RESIDUE
!=
VERIFIED MATERIAL EFFECT

MODELED FEASIBILITY
!=
VERIFIED WORLD FEASIBILITY

QUALIFICATION
=
IMPORTED / BORROWED ANNOTATION

STALE ANNOTATION
!=
DISQUALIFIED PATH

MANIFESTATION OCCURRENCE
!=
MANIFESTATION AUTHORIZATION

L7 OWNS
MANIFESTATION STANDING

L4 OWNS
POST-MANIFESTATION FEEDBACK

DYNAMIC INFLUENCE EDGE
!=
ESTABLISHED CAUSAL EDGE

NO FURTHER MODELED DYNAMICS
!=
NOTHING ELSE CAN HAPPEN

LOCAL CLOSURE
!=
CLOSURE LEGITIMACY

DYNAMICAL EFFECT
!=
GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY
~~~

---

# L4.80｜L4 的最短定義

> **生成動力學，是描述一個受限的 Generative Field Model，如何從 Solution Space 的未穩定可能結構出發，在 constraint injection 所形成的 terrain 中經歷 proto-structure formation／dissipation、PATH formation、Reaction Body 結構運動與 Closure Gravity 壓力，並持續更新 represented relational state 的理論。**

更短：

> **生成不是從既成 PATH 清單裡挑一條路走到底；生成先在解空間中被限制塑形，再形成 PATH，之後仍持續反應、重框、衰減、閉合與重開。**

必須保留：

~~~text
MODEL
!=
COMPLETE ACTUAL STATE

INFLUENCE
!=
CAUSAL PROOF
~~~

---

# L4.81｜L4 → L5 的橋

當 L4 已經把下列型別分清：

~~~text
Field Model
Attention Axes
PATH State / Topology
Reaction Dynamic Term
Realized Field Transition
Closure Directional Term
Active Residue
Imported Qualification Annotation
Externally Established Serialization Feedback Event
~~~

下一個問題自然出現：

> **我們憑什麼知道這些東西真的發生了？**

更尖銳：

> **一個模型說「我看見 PATH A 被壓制」，那是真的觀察，還是生成了一段漂亮的場性敘事？**

因此 L5 必須接：

~~~text
OBSERVATION
EVIDENCE
TRACE
AUDIT
PROXY
SOURCE PROVENANCE
QUALIFICATION OF OBSERVATION
~~~

L4 最後一句：

> **L4 描述場怎麼動；L5 才審我們到底看見了什麼。**

---



# L4.82｜V0.2.1 已處理與仍開放項目

本 patch 已補：

~~~text
00 Solution Space / PATH_SPACE 分離的前段動力化
00A Constraint Injection / Terrain / Proto-Structure / PATH Formation
00B Structural Cost Profile / Generative Resistance 分離
00C Pre-PATH Dissipation vs Post-PATH Attenuation
01 RB / CG co-determination
02 Reaction Term vs Realized Field Transition
03 feasibility → modeled feasibility assessment
04 Active Residue 去循環定義
05 qualification annotation applicability / staleness
06 manifestation occurrence vs manifestation right
07 quiescence scope 降級
08 causal-identification evidence wording
09 serialization event 改 externally established / admitted
10 L5 ENTRY 改 OPEN
~~~

仍 OPEN：

~~~text
01 Reaction Body 是否應從 taxonomy
   再抽象成 relation-transform operator？

02 Closure Pressure 未來是否需要 autonomous state standing？

03 Active Residue 的 materiality / predictive value
   能否被 L5 穩定審計？

04 Structural Hysteresis
   是否能形成 matched-input / different-residue
   的可重現差異？

05 Field Model 中每個 variable
   應如何標記：
   observable / derived / inferred / proxied /
   theoretical-only / currently-unobservable？

06 typed influence edge
   如何被 L5 升級其 evidence standing？

07 externally established manifestation event
   與 L7 最終 boundary contract 如何對接？
~~~

---

# L4.83｜V0.2.1 狀態

~~~text
L4 V0.2.1

STRUCTURAL TYPE SYSTEM
=
PASS

FIELD / COMPONENT SEPARATION
=
PASS

ATTENTION TYPING
=
PASS

QUALIFICATION OWNER
=
PASS

HISTORY / ACTIVE RESIDUE
=
PASS

REACTION / CLOSURE CO-DETERMINATION
=
PASS

REACTION TERM / REALIZED TRANSITION
=
PASS

MANIFESTATION OWNER SPLIT
=
PASS

CAUSAL CLAIM BOUNDARY
=
PASS
~~~

L5 ENTRY：

~~~text
OPEN
~~~

理由：

~~~text
L4 NOW DEFINES
DISTINCT THEORETICAL VARIABLE TYPES

WITHOUT CLAIMING
THEY HAVE ALREADY BEEN OBSERVED
~~~

L5 任務：

~~~text
FOR EACH L4 VARIABLE / EDGE,
DETERMINE:

DIRECTLY OBSERVABLE?
DERIVED?
INFERRED?
PROXIED?
INTERVENTION-SUPPORTED?
THEORETICAL ONLY?
UNOBSERVABLE UNDER CURRENT ACCESS?
~~~

L5 不是替 L4 補 ontology。

L5 是開始追問：

> **你說這些東西在動。證據在哪裡？**

最短橋：

> **L4 給出 dynamics 債單；L5 開始逐筆查帳。**

STATUS：

~~~text
PASS_TO_L5
WITH OPEN EMPIRICAL QUESTIONS
~~~

這裡先停。


# LIHUOOS V1｜L5 — OBSERVATION & AUDIT

**版本：** V0.2.1 WORKING  
**日期：** 2026-10-25  
**狀態：** TARGETED EPISTEMIC HARDENING / PASS_TO_L6  
**層級：** LIHUOOS / L5  
**主題：** 觀察與審計｜我們憑什麼說自己看見了生成中的某種結構？  
**法位鎖：** NON_RUNTIME / NON_INTERVENTION / ANTI_ORACLE / ANTI_THEATER / EVIDENCE-BOUND  
**上游依據：** L4 V0.2.1 PASS_TO_L5  
**歷史工程回照：** SAC Field Observer / O9 / Radar / Light Trace / Evidence Ladder / Execution Evidence  
**核心限制：** THEORETICAL VARIABLE != OBSERVED VARIABLE

---

# L5.0｜本層要回答的問題

L4 已經把生成動力學拆成不同型別：

~~~text
Field Model
Attention Axes
PATH State / Topology
Reaction Dynamic Term
Realized Field Transition
Closure Directional Term
Active Residue
Imported Qualification Annotation
Serialization Feedback Event
Typed Influence Edge
~~~

但：

~~~text
NAMED VARIABLE
!=
OBSERVED VARIABLE
~~~

因此 L5 不再問：

> **生成怎麼動？**

而問：

> **我們憑什麼說某個 dynamics variable、state change 或 influence relation 真的被看見、被支持、被推論，或目前根本看不到？**

最短：

> **L4 提出 dynamics 債單；L5 逐筆查帳。**

---

# L5.1｜來源 lineage 與使用原則

本層主要回照：

~~~text
LIHUOOS/V1版本/L4 第二版本.md

理火舊版系統/
01_MAIN_V4.3_O9_I9_RATIFIED_V0.2.md

理火V5系統/V5.1版本/
02_LIHUO_MAIN_V5.1.1_FULL_BODY_RATIFIED_20261015.md

理火舊版系統/
02_LIGHTER_V4.6_RADAR_TRACE_RATIFIED_V0.4.md

理火體系/教科書/SACRADARV6.txt

理火V5系統/散記/
理火散記_V3.5.1_I9真牆誤判後證修正_20261006.md

理火V5系統/MEMO/
理火新生LLM訓練法_V1.0_關聯合法性_AI對AI持續審計_劇場抗性正式理論版(1).md
~~~

但必須硬鎖：

~~~text
HISTORICAL OBSERVATION MODULE
!=
L5 UNIVERSAL ONTOLOGY

ENGINEERING RECEIPT
!=
UNIVERSAL EVIDENCE REQUIREMENT

EVIDENCE LADDER
!=
UNIVERSAL EPISTEMIC SCALE
~~~

---

# L5.2｜第一核心分離：Target / Claim / Evidence / Inference / Verdict

L5 第一刀：

~~~text
OBSERVATION TARGET
!=
OBSERVATION CLAIM
!=
EVIDENCE ITEM
!=
INFERENCE
!=
AUDIT VERDICT
~~~

例如：

~~~yaml
EXAMPLE:
  target:
    "PATH_A activation"

  observation_claim:
    "PATH_A appears dimmed in this modeled cycle"

  evidence_items:
    - "instrumented lamp descriptor"
    - "candidate exposure record"

  inference:
    "exploration access may have been reduced"

  audit_verdict:
    "SUPPORTED_WITHIN_SCOPE"
~~~

這五者若混在一起，

就會出現：

> **因為我寫出一段觀察，所以我已經看見了。**

---

# L5.3｜Observation Target

Observation Target 是：

> **L5 想要知道其狀態、事件、變化或關係的對象。**

Target 可以來自：

~~~text
L4 variable
external world state
execution event
artifact state
qualification annotation
manifestation occurrence
influence edge
~~~

Target 本身不自帶：

~~~text
observability
truth
standing
evidence
~~~

---


# L5.4｜Epistemic Claim｜L5 的上位主張型別

V0.2 把所有 claim 都放在 Observation Claim 名下，

但同時又允許 CAUSAL_CLAIM，

形成 type collision。

V0.2.1 改成：

~~~text
EPISTEMIC_CLAIM
=
OBSERVATION / DESCRIPTION CLAIM
DERIVED CLAIM
INFERENTIAL CLAIM
NEGATIVE CLAIM
CAUSAL CLAIM
~~~

L5 的工作不是：

> 所有 claim 都叫 observation。

而是：

> **所有這些 epistemic claims 都必須交代自己如何取得 standing。**

因此：

~~~text
AUDITING CAUSAL EVIDENCE
!=
OWNING CAUSAL TRUTH
~~~

同樣：

~~~text
L5 CAN AUDIT
A CAUSAL CLAIM

BUT

L5 DOES NOT BECOME
THE CAUSAL SOVEREIGN
~~~

# L5.5｜Observation Type System｜拆掉 Observation Class

V0.1 把 access、epistemic mode、source origin、observability status、evidence property 混在同一個 Observation Class。

V0.2 正式拆成：

~~~text
1 ACCESS_MODE
2 EPISTEMIC_MODE
3 SOURCE_ORIGIN
4 OBSERVABILITY_STATUS
5 EVIDENCE_PROPERTIES / SUPPORT_STATUS
~~~

因此：

~~~text
OBSERVATION CLASS
=
RETIRED AS A SINGLE TYPE
~~~

---

# L5.6｜Access Mode｜Observer 怎麼碰到 target

~~~text
ACCESS_MODE
=
DIRECT_TARGET_ACCESS
DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS
INSTRUMENTED_ACCESS
ARTIFACT_ACCESS
REPORT_ACCESS
NO_DIRECT_ACCESS
~~~

核心：

~~~text
DIRECT_TARGET_ACCESS
!=
DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS
~~~

例如 auditor 直接看到一張 receipt，是 DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS，不是直接看到 receipt 所聲稱的 execution process。

---

# L5.7｜Observer Access != Source Access

L5 必須再拆：

~~~text
OBSERVER_ACCESS_MODE
!=
SOURCE_ACCESS_MODE
~~~

例：

人類說「我痛」。

source 可能對自身 pain target 具有：

~~~text
FIRST_PERSON_TARGET_ACCESS
~~~

但 auditor 直接具有的是：

~~~text
DIRECT_ACCESS_TO_REPORT
~~~

所以：

~~~text
AUDITOR DIRECTLY OBSERVED
THE REPORT

!=

AUDITOR DIRECTLY OBSERVED
THE PAIN TARGET
~~~

---

# L5.8｜Epistemic Mode｜這個 claim 是怎麼得到的

~~~text
EPISTEMIC_MODE
=
OBSERVED
DERIVED
INFERRED
PROXIED
~~~

它回答：

> **這個 claim 與 evidence 之間是哪一種認識關係？**

因此：

~~~text
OBSERVED
!=
DERIVED
!=
INFERRED
!=
PROXIED
~~~

---


# L5.9｜Claim Type × Epistemic Mode

V0.2.1 保留 Claim Type：

~~~text
STATE_DESCRIPTION
EVENT_DESCRIPTION
RELATION_DESCRIPTION
PROCESS_CLAIM
NEGATIVE_CLAIM
CAUSAL_CLAIM
META_OBSERVATION_CLAIM
OTHER
~~~

Epistemic Mode 仍是：

~~~text
OBSERVED
DERIVED
INFERRED
PROXIED
~~~

核心：

~~~text
WHAT IS CLAIMED
!=
HOW THE CLAIM WAS OBTAINED
~~~

但兩者不是任意笛卡兒積。

至少加入 compatibility locks：

~~~text
OBSERVATION OF EVENTS
!=
OBSERVATION OF CAUSALITY

CAUSAL CLAIM
REQUIRES
CAUSAL-IDENTIFICATION STANDING
BEYOND
EVENT OBSERVATION
~~~

以及：

~~~text
NEGATIVE CLAIM
+
OBSERVED MODE
!=
SUFFICIENT BY ITSELF
~~~

若 negative claim 來自 no signal，

仍必須吃：

~~~text
DETECTABILITY RELATION
+
ADEQUATE ACCESS
+
OBSERVATION WINDOW
+
COVERAGE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
CLAIM TYPE × EPISTEMIC MODE
=
TYPED COMPATIBILITY
NOT
FREE COMBINATION
~~~

# L5.10｜Source Origin｜材料從哪裡來

~~~text
SOURCE_ORIGIN
=
RUNTIME
TOOL
MODEL_REPORT
HUMAN_FIRST_PERSON_REPORT
HUMAN_SECONDARY_REPORT
DOCUMENT
EXTERNAL_DATABASE
INSTRUMENT
SIMULATION
DERIVED_COMPUTATION
OTHER
~~~

Source Origin 只回答材料從哪裡來，不回答 direct、true、reliable、causal、sufficient。

~~~text
SOURCE ORIGIN
!=
EPISTEMIC MODE
!=
OBSERVATION STANDING
~~~

---


# L5.10.1｜Observability / Gap / Temporal / Theory Status 拆分

V0.2 仍把幾種不同法位混在一起。

V0.2.1 正式拆：

~~~text
OBSERVABILITY_STATUS
=
AVAILABLE
PARTIAL
CURRENTLY_UNAVAILABLE
UNKNOWN
~~~

~~~text
GAP_DIAGNOSTIC
=
NONE
EVIDENCE_GAP
ACCESS_GAP
~~~

~~~text
TEMPORAL_APPLICABILITY
=
CURRENT
STALE
~~~

~~~text
THEORY_STATUS
=
EMPIRICALLY_ENGAGED
THEORETICAL_ONLY
~~~

因此：

~~~text
THEORETICAL_ONLY
!=
OBSERVABILITY STATUS

STALE
!=
OBSERVATION STANDING

ACCESS_GAP
!=
OBSERVATION STANDING
~~~

Observation Standing 只回答：

> **目前 evidence 對 claim 的支持關係。**

現行核心值收成：

~~~text
SUPPORTED_WITHIN_SCOPE
PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED
UNVERIFIED
CONTRADICTED
~~~


# L5.10.2｜Evidence Properties / Dependency Relation / Support Status

Evidence item intrinsic properties 可包括：

~~~text
REPLAYABLE
NON_REPLAYABLE
CALIBRATED
UNCALIBRATED
TAMPER_EVIDENT
SOURCE_BOUND
~~~

但：

~~~text
INDEPENDENT
DEPENDENT
~~~

不是單一 evidence item 脫離 comparison set 就能成立的 intrinsic property。

因此移到：

~~~yaml
EVIDENCE_DEPENDENCY_RELATION:
  evidence_ref:
  dependency_refs: []
  common_origin_ref: null
  common_model_ref: null
  common_snapshot_ref: null
  common_evaluator_ref: null
~~~

所以：

~~~text
EVIDENCE INDEPENDENCE
=
RELATIONAL PROPERTY
~~~

不是 item 自帶徽章。

Support Status 保留：

~~~text
OBSERVATIONAL_SUPPORT
DERIVATIONAL_SUPPORT
PROXY_SUPPORT
INTERVENTION_SUPPORT
CAUSAL_IDENTIFICATION_SUPPORT
NO_SUPPORT_ESTABLISHED
~~~


# L5.10.3｜Epistemic Claim Object｜V0.2.1

~~~yaml
EPISTEMIC_CLAIM:
  claim_ref: "REQUIRED"
  target_ref: "REQUIRED"
  claim_text: "REQUIRED"

  claim_type:
    - STATE_DESCRIPTION
    - EVENT_DESCRIPTION
    - RELATION_DESCRIPTION
    - PROCESS_CLAIM
    - NEGATIVE_CLAIM
    - CAUSAL_CLAIM
    - META_OBSERVATION_CLAIM
    - OTHER

  epistemic_mode:
    - OBSERVED
    - DERIVED
    - INFERRED
    - PROXIED

  observer_access_mode:
    - DIRECT_TARGET_ACCESS
    - DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS
    - INSTRUMENTED_ACCESS
    - ARTIFACT_ACCESS
    - REPORT_ACCESS
    - NO_DIRECT_ACCESS

  source_access_mode:
    - FIRST_PERSON_TARGET_ACCESS
    - DIRECT_TARGET_ACCESS
    - INSTRUMENTED_TARGET_ACCESS
    - RECORD_ONLY_ACCESS
    - UNKNOWN
    - NOT_APPLICABLE

  source_origin:
    - RUNTIME
    - TOOL
    - MODEL_REPORT
    - HUMAN_FIRST_PERSON_REPORT
    - HUMAN_SECONDARY_REPORT
    - DOCUMENT
    - EXTERNAL_DATABASE
    - INSTRUMENT
    - SIMULATION
    - DERIVED_COMPUTATION
    - OTHER

  observability_status:
    - AVAILABLE
    - PARTIAL
    - CURRENTLY_UNAVAILABLE
    - UNKNOWN

  gap_diagnostic:
    - NONE
    - EVIDENCE_GAP
    - ACCESS_GAP

  temporal_applicability:
    - CURRENT
    - STALE

  theory_status:
    - EMPIRICALLY_ENGAGED
    - THEORETICAL_ONLY

  basis_ref: "REQUIRED_OR_EXPLICIT_GAP"
  source_refs: []
  provenance_ref: "REQUIRED_OR_EXPLICIT_GAP"

  scope_ref: "REQUIRED"
  representation_boundary_ref: "REQUIRED"
  access_boundary_ref: "REQUIRED"

  temporal:
    target_event_at_ref: null
    evidence_created_at_ref: null
    available_to_observer_at_ref: null
    observer_accessed_at_ref: null
    claim_formed_at_ref: null
    assessed_at_ref: null
    reclassified_at_ref: null
    world_state_at_ref: null
    version_at_ref: null

  known_gaps: []
  supporting_evidence_refs: []
  contradicting_evidence_refs: []

  observation_standing:
    status: "UNASSESSED"

  audit_verdict:
    status: "NOT_YET_AUDITED"
~~~

這是理論 schema，不是 mandatory runtime receipt。

# L5.10.4｜Observation Standing 的 Core / Conditional Dimensions

Core dimensions：

~~~text
SOURCE_INTEGRITY
TARGET_FIDELITY
SCOPE_FIT
TEMPORAL_FIT
PROVENANCE_INTEGRITY
ACCESS_ADEQUACY
~~~

Conditional dimensions：

~~~text
PROXY_VALIDITY
DERIVATION_VALIDITY
INDEPENDENCE
REPRODUCIBILITY
CAUSAL_IDENTIFICATION_STATUS
~~~

Conditional dimension 必須允許：

~~~text
NOT_APPLICABLE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
EVERY CLAIM
!=
MUST FILL EVERY DIMENSION
~~~



# L5.10.5｜Evidence Item

Evidence Item 是：

> **可以被用來支持、限制、反駁或重新分類某個 claim 的可追溯材料。**

例如：

~~~text
visible artifact
runtime instrumentation record
tool result
external document
world-state record
snapshot
delta
test result
human testimony
model self-report
replay result
source code
log
counterexample
~~~

但：

~~~text
EVIDENCE ITEM EXISTS
!=
CLAIM SUPPORTED
~~~

因為仍需問：

~~~text
relevance
scope
time
provenance
integrity
access
target fidelity
independence where relevant
~~~

---

# L5.10.6｜Evidence Origin != Evidence Standing

歷史工程曾使用：

~~~text
E0 SOURCE
E1 REPRESENTATION
E2 SEMANTICS
E3 CAPABILITY
E4 BOUNDARY
E5 GRAPH / CONTRACT / CONFORMANCE
E6 RUNTIME TEST
E7 HOST ENFORCEMENT
~~~

L5 保留這套 engineering lineage 所教出的分離：

~~~text
DECLARATION
!=
BINDING
!=
ATTACHMENT
!=
EXECUTION
!=
HOST ENFORCEMENT
~~~

但：

~~~text
E0–E7
!=
UNIVERSAL EPISTEMIC LADDER
~~~

核心：

~~~text
EVIDENCE ORIGIN
!=
EVIDENCE STANDING

ENGINEERING LEVEL
!=
TRUTH LEVEL
~~~

---

# L5.10.7｜Evidence Properties 不能自動升格 Standing

~~~text
REPLAYABLE
CALIBRATED
INDEPENDENT
INSTRUMENTED
~~~

都可能提高某些 claim 的支持力。

但：

~~~text
PROPERTY PRESENT
!=
CLAIM SUPPORTED
~~~

因為 evidence property 仍須和：

~~~text
target
claim type
scope
time
access
mapping relation
~~~

對得上。

---

# L5.11｜Direct Target Observation

Direct Observation 只在 observer 確實具有 direct target access 時成立。

~~~text
DIRECT TARGET ACCESS
!=
DIRECT RECORD ACCESS
~~~

例如 auditor 直接看到 receipt，只能先說：

> receipt exists.

不能直接說：

> underlying execution process was observed.

---

# L5.12｜Direct Record Observation

以下都可以是 direct record access：

~~~text
receipt exists
runtime log exists
instrument display shows X
human report contains Y
document contains Z
~~~

但：

~~~text
RECORD EXISTS
!=
UNDERLYING PROCESS VERIFIED
~~~

---

# L5.13｜Instrumented Access

Instrumented Observation 是透過被定義、可核對的 instrumentation channel 取得 target representation。

~~~text
INSTRUMENTED
!=
INFALLIBLE
~~~

仍要審：

~~~text
instrument validity
instrument scope
instrument health
instrument freshness
target fidelity
calibration where applicable
tampering / substitution risk
~~~

---

# L5.14｜Derived Mode

Derived 是從可追溯 evidence / observations，透過明示 derivation rule 產生新 claim。

例如：

~~~text
snapshot_before
+
snapshot_after
↓
typed state delta
~~~

所以：

~~~text
REALIZED_FIELD_TRANSITION
MAY BE DERIVED

BUT

DERIVED STATE DELTA
!=
REACTION DYNAMIC TERM DIRECTLY OBSERVED
~~~

---

# L5.15｜Inference Mode

Inference 是從 evidence / derived states 對未直接暴露 target 所作的受限推論。

Inference 應能指出：

~~~text
basis
scope
alternative explanations
known gaps
defeaters
uncertainty
~~~

~~~text
INFERRED
!=
OBSERVED
~~~

---

# L5.16｜Proxy Mode

Proxy 是用可觀察量對另一個 target 建立替代性指標關係。

~~~text
PROXY
!=
TARGET

CORRELATED
!=
IDENTICAL

USEFUL
!=
ONTOLOGICALLY EQUIVALENT
~~~

理論核心只要求：

~~~text
proxy target
mapping hypothesis
valid scope
known failure modes
validation standing
~~~

只有要當 quantitative measurement 時，才要求 calibration、scale semantics、error bounds、repeatability。

---


# L5.17｜Source Access != Accuracy

不論 source access 多直接，

都必須硬鎖：

~~~text
FIRST_PERSON_TARGET_ACCESS
!=
INFALLIBLE TARGET DESCRIPTION

INSTRUMENTED_TARGET_ACCESS
!=
TARGET FIDELITY GUARANTEED

DIRECT_TARGET_ACCESS
!=
CAUSAL UNDERSTANDING
~~~

例：

> 一個人對自己的痛有第一人稱 access，

不代表：

> 他對病因也有 direct access。

又例如：

> instrument 接到 target，

不代表：

> mapping / calibration / interpretation 必然正確。

因此：

~~~text
ACCESS
!=
ACCURACY
!=
INTERPRETIVE STANDING
~~~

---

# L5.18｜Model / Human Report

模型自述：

~~~text
SOURCE ORIGIN
=
MODEL_REPORT

AUDITOR ACCESS
=
DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS
~~~

通常不是 hidden-state direct target access。

所以：

~~~text
MODEL SAYS IT RAN
!=
IT RAN

MODEL SAYS
"PATH A WAS SUPPRESSED"
!=
PATH SUPPRESSION OBSERVED
~~~

人類第一人稱 report 同樣分：

~~~text
SOURCE ACCESS
MAY BE
FIRST_PERSON_TARGET_ACCESS

AUDITOR ACCESS
MAY ONLY BE
DIRECT_RECORD_ACCESS
TO THE REPORT
~~~

這不貶低 report。

它只把：

~~~text
WHO HAD ACCESS
TO WHAT
~~~

寫清楚。

# L5.19｜Trace 的型別分離

Trace 至少拆：

~~~text
EXECUTION TRACE
OBSERVATION TRACE
PROVENANCE TRACE
CHANGE TRACE
EXPLANATORY NARRATIVE
VISIBLE REASONING TEXT
~~~

不能混。

---

# L5.20｜Execution Trace

Execution Trace 要支持的是：

> **某個 execution event / transition 是否真的發生。**

歷史 MAIN 已經留下：

~~~text
CHAIN-OF-THOUGHT
!=
RUNTIME EVIDENCE

MODEL SAYS IT RAN
!=
IT RAN

VISIBLE EXPLANATION
!=
EXECUTION TRACE
~~~

L5 保留。

---

# L5.21｜Observation Trace

Observation Trace 記錄：

~~~text
what was observed
through which channel
from which target snapshot
with what access boundary
at what time
~~~

它不等於 execution trace，

也不等於 causal proof。

---

# L5.22｜Light Trace 的歷史法位

歷史 Lighter 定義：

~~~text
LIGHT TRACE
=
exposed evidence / standing / residue / qualification view
~~~

並且：

~~~text
LIGHT TRACE
!=
hidden CoT

LIGHT TRACE
!=
raw activation

LIGHT TRACE
!=
audit authority

LIGHT TRACE
!=
execution trace
~~~

L5 保留這個分離。

---

# L5.23｜Radar 的歷史法位

Radar 歷史上是：

~~~text
常駐掃描
ADVISORY
不干預
~~~

因此：

~~~text
OBSERVATION ADVISORY
!=
AUDIT VERDICT

RADAR SIGNAL
!=
QUALIFICATION
~~~

Radar 可以指出值得看的地方。

但不能因為它發出 signal，

就宣布 target 已成立。

---

# L5.24｜O9 的歷史法位

O9 是歷史上成熟的：

~~~text
SCOPED FIELD OBSERVER
~~~

它允許：

~~~text
observation annotations
salience descriptors
tension descriptors
~~~

但不得：

~~~text
grant premise standing
create world fact
perform Final Closure
claim hidden-state truth
~~~

最重要的數值原則：

~~~text
QUALITATIVE BY DEFAULT

0..1
ONLY WHEN
AN EXPLICIT OBSERVABLE /
REPRODUCIBLE OPERATIONAL DEFINITION EXISTS
~~~

這一條直接進 L5。

---

# L5.25｜O9 不是 L5 Universal Runtime Requirement

雖然 O9 歷史上曾 every cycle 執行，

但：

~~~text
O9 ENGINEERING CONTRACT
!=
L5 UNIVERSAL OBSERVATION REQUIREMENT
~~~

L5 不主張：

> 每次生成都要產生燈態。

而只保存：

> **如果你要對 attention / salience / suppression 作強主張，就要有相稱的觀察基礎。**

---

# L5.26｜Observation Occurrence != Observation Qualification

即使真的發生 observation event，

仍要分：

~~~text
OBSERVATION OCCURRED
!=
OBSERVATION CLAIM SUPPORTED
~~~

例如：

> observer 看到了某張 snapshot。

不表示：

> observer 對 snapshot 的解讀一定正確。

---



# L5.27｜Observation Standing｜Evidence 支持到哪裡

Observation Standing 只回答：

> **在目前 evidence profile 下，這個 claim 被支持到什麼程度與範圍？**

現行核心值：

~~~text
SUPPORTED_WITHIN_SCOPE
PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED
UNVERIFIED
CONTRADICTED
~~~

下列不再混入 Standing：

~~~text
EVIDENCE_GAP
ACCESS_GAP
→ GAP_DIAGNOSTIC

STALE
→ TEMPORAL_APPLICABILITY

THEORETICAL_ONLY
→ THEORY_STATUS

DERIVED / INFERRED / PROXIED
→ EPISTEMIC_MODE
~~~

---

# L5.28｜Audit Verdict｜Claim 有沒有尊重 Standing

~~~text
CONFORMING
OVERCLAIM
SCOPE_MISMATCH
TEMPORAL_MISMATCH
PROVENANCE_GAP
ACCESS_MISMATCH
HOLD_GAP
UNAUDITABLE_CURRENT_ACCESS
~~~

核心：

~~~text
OBSERVATION STANDING
=
WHAT THE EVIDENCE SUPPORTS

AUDIT VERDICT
=
WHETHER THE CLAIM
RESPECTS THAT STANDING
~~~

---

# L5.29｜Standing / Gap / Applicability / Theory Status 不互吃

~~~text
OBSERVATION STANDING
!=
GAP DIAGNOSTIC
!=
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
!=
THEORY STATUS
!=
AUDIT VERDICT
~~~

例如：

~~~yaml
claim:
  observation_standing: PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED
  gap_diagnostic: EVIDENCE_GAP
  temporal_applicability: CURRENT
  theory_status: EMPIRICALLY_ENGAGED
  audit_verdict: CONFORMING
~~~

可以成立。

# L5.30｜Temporal Schema｜事件、證據、存取、成句、審計分時

V0.2 正式拆：

~~~text
TARGET_EVENT_AT
EVIDENCE_CREATED_AT
AVAILABLE_TO_OBSERVER_AT
OBSERVER_ACCESSED_AT
CLAIM_FORMED_AT
ASSESSED_AT
RECLASSIFIED_AT
WORLD_STATE_AT
VERSION_AT
~~~

優先使用 AVAILABLE_TO_OBSERVER_AT，而不是模糊的 KNOWN_AT。

---

# L5.31｜後證可以重判前事，不得回鑄前知

~~~text
POSTERIOR EVIDENCE
MAY RECLASSIFY
A PAST EVENT

BUT

MAY NOT REWRITE
WHAT WAS AVAILABLE
TO THE OBSERVER
AT THAT EARLIER TIME
~~~

因此必須保存：

~~~text
ORIGINAL CLAIM / STANDING
+
ORIGINAL ACCESS BOUNDARY
+
POSTERIOR RECLASSIFICATION
~~~

---

# L5.32｜Posterior Reclassification

~~~yaml
ORIGINAL:
  target_event_at_ref: T0
  evidence_created_at_ref: T1
  available_to_observer_at_ref: T1
  observer_accessed_at_ref: T1
  claim_formed_at_ref: T1
  assessed_at_ref: T1
  claim: "constraint appears external"
  standing_at_T1: "SUPPORTED_WITHIN_SCOPE"

POSTERIOR:
  new_evidence_created_at_ref: T2
  observer_accessed_new_evidence_at_ref: T2
  reclassified_at_ref: T2
  current_assessment: "prior classification was mistaken"

HISTORY:
  T1_RECORD_REMAINS_UNCHANGED: true
~~~

---

# L5.33｜Negative Observation｜No Signal 必須有 Detectability Relation

~~~text
NOT OBSERVED
!=
DOES NOT EXIST
~~~

要把 no signal 升成 negative evidence，至少要建立：

~~~text
IF TARGET EVENT X OCCURS
UNDER DECLARED ACCESS / INSTRUMENTATION

THEN

EXPECTED SIGNAL Y
SHOULD APPEAR
WITHIN
OBSERVATION WINDOW W
~~~

因此至少需要：

~~~yaml
NEGATIVE_OBSERVATION_BASIS:
  target_event_ref:
  detectability_basis_ref:
  expected_trace_or_signal_ref:
  access_channel_ref:
  instrument_health_ref:
  observation_window_ref:
  coverage_boundary_ref:

  actual_signal_status:
    - NOT_OBSERVED
    - PARTIALLY_OBSERVED
    - OBSERVED

  detectability_relation_status:
    - ESTABLISHED
    - PARTIAL
    - UNESTABLISHED
~~~

~~~text
NO SIGNAL
+
NO DETECTABILITY RELATION
=
NO STRONG NEGATIVE CLAIM
~~~

# L5.34｜Contradiction 必須保留

多個 evidence item 若互相衝突：

~~~text
DO NOT
AVERAGE AWAY
THE CONFLICT

PRESERVE
EXPLICIT CONFLICT
~~~

L5 不允許：

> 最後一個證據出現，就把前一個證據從歷史上抹掉。

---


# L5.35｜Corroboration != Duplication

~~~text
SAME SOURCE
REPEATED 10 TIMES
!=
10 INDEPENDENT EVIDENCE ITEMS
~~~

同一 underlying source 轉成 summary / audit report / trace view，

仍不自動變成多份獨立 corroboration。

---

# L5.36｜Evidence Independence 是 Relation

~~~text
EVIDENCE INDEPENDENCE
!=
INTRINSIC ITEM PROPERTY
~~~

需要比較：

~~~text
same origin?
same model?
same snapshot?
same prompt?
same evaluator?
same copied source?
same hidden dependency?
~~~

因此：

~~~yaml
EVIDENCE_DEPENDENCY_RELATION:
  evidence_ref:
  dependency_refs: []
  common_origin_ref: null
  common_model_ref: null
  common_snapshot_ref: null
  common_evaluator_ref: null
~~~

核心：

~~~text
MULTIPLE ARTIFACTS
!=
MULTIPLE INDEPENDENT SOURCES
~~~

# L5.37｜Reproducibility 是 Conditional Dimension

Reproducibility 不是所有 observation 的必要條件。

如果 claim 是一次性事件，可能 NOT_APPLICABLE。

如果 claim 是穩定機制、反覆 dynamics 或 generalizable relation，reproducibility / repeated discrimination 的重要性上升。

~~~text
REPRODUCIBILITY
=
CONDITIONAL STANDING DIMENSION
~~~

---

# L5.38｜Evidence-Licensed Claim Envelope

V0.1 的：

~~~text
CLAIM STRENGTH
<=
EVIDENCE STANDING
~~~

只能當口訣，因為 standing 是 typed，不是 scalar。

V0.2 改成：

~~~text
CLAIM
MUST REMAIN WITHIN
THE EVIDENCE-LICENSED
CLAIM ENVELOPE
~~~

Evidence profile 決定一個 claim 有資格說到什麼：

~~~text
claim type
target
scope
time
modality
causal strength
generality
certainty language
~~~

例如：

~~~text
strong evidence that receipt exists
~~~

可以支持：

> receipt exists.

但不自動支持：

> execution occurred.

所以：

~~~text
STRONG SUPPORT
FOR CLAIM TYPE A
!=
SUPPORT
FOR CLAIM TYPE B
~~~

---

# L5.39｜Observation Language Discipline

語言應對齊 Evidence-Licensed Claim Envelope。

| Epistemic / Standing | 合法語言傾向 |
|---|---|
| OBSERVED + adequate target access | 記錄到／觀察到 |
| DERIVED | 由 A/B 推導出 |
| INFERRED | 支持／顯示／推測 |
| PROXIED | 代理指標與…一致 |
| MODEL/HUMAN REPORT | X 自述／報告… |
| UNVERIFIED | 尚未驗證 |
| THEORETICAL_ONLY | 理論預測／工作假說 |
| CURRENTLY_UNOBSERVABLE | 在目前 access 下無法觀察 |

~~~text
LANGUAGE
MUST REMAIN WITHIN
EVIDENCE-LICENSED CLAIM ENVELOPE
~~~

---

# L5.40｜Numerical Discipline

歷史 O9 的規則保留：

~~~text
QUALITATIVE BY DEFAULT
~~~

只有存在 explicit operational definition、observable basis、calibrated procedure、known scale semantics 與必要 error semantics 時，才可使用數值。

~~~text
NUMBER
!=
MEASUREMENT
~~~

---

# L5.41｜Pseudo-Precision Failure

以下都可能是假精確：

~~~text
PATH salience = 0.83
CG = 0.71
residue strength = 0.42
uncertainty = 17%
~~~

若沒有 operational definition、calibration、source、scale meaning、error semantics，

只能算：

~~~text
NUMERIC THEATER
~~~


# L5.42｜Audit

Audit 是：

> **審查 claim 是否尊重 evidence standing、scope、time、access 與 provenance。**

因此：

~~~text
AUDIT
!=
WORLD FACT CREATION

AUDIT
!=
CLAIM MUTATION
~~~

---

# L5.43｜Audit Target

Audit 可以審 observation claim、evidence item、proxy relation、derivation、trace、source provenance、coverage claim、temporal standing、influence-edge evidence status 與 auditor 本身。

但能審不等於擁有 target。

---

# L5.44｜Audit Verdict

V0.2 使用：

~~~text
CONFORMING
OVERCLAIM
SCOPE_MISMATCH
TEMPORAL_MISMATCH
PROVENANCE_GAP
ACCESS_MISMATCH
HOLD_GAP
UNAUDITABLE_CURRENT_ACCESS
~~~

這些只回答 claim-evidence relation 是否合規。

---

# L5.45｜AUDIT PASS != QUALIFICATION PASS

~~~text
AUDIT PASS
!=
TRUTH PASS
!=
QUALIFICATION PASS
~~~

Auditor 最多確認：

> **這個 claim 沒有超過目前 evidence standing。**

---

# L5.46｜Auditor != Claim Editor

V0.1 說 auditor 可以 lower unsupported claim strength，容易產生 intervention leakage。

V0.2 改成：

~~~text
AUDITOR MAY:
issue OVERCLAIM verdict
issue LANGUAGE CEILING
identify required downgrade
identify missing basis
preserve contradiction
recommend / require downstream revision
under applicable governance
~~~

但：

~~~text
AUDIT VERDICT
!=
CLAIM MUTATION
~~~

真正修改 claim，是 downstream revision / action owner 的工作。

---

# L5.47｜Auditor != Truth Sovereign

~~~text
AUDITOR
!=
TRUTH SOVEREIGN
~~~

Auditor 可以 detect mismatch、causal leakage、provenance gap、scope mismatch、preserve conflicts、issue language ceiling。

不得自宣 ground truth、創造 world fact、用 PASS 升級 evidence、取代必要 domain / human authority。

---

# L5.48｜Auditor 本身也要被審

持續審計會形成：

~~~text
AUDITOR GRAMMAR LEARNING
~~~

因此：

~~~text
AUDITOR OUTPUT
!=
AUDIT VALIDITY
~~~

需要考慮 auditor provenance、scope、method、known bias、invariance tests、evidence-toggle tests、surface-style variation。

---

# L5.49｜Theater Resistance

~~~text
OBSERVATION-SHAPED TEXT
!=
OBSERVATION

TRACE-SHAPED TEXT
!=
TRACE

RECEIPT-SHAPED TEXT
!=
EXECUTION EVIDENCE

AUDIT-SHAPED TEXT
!=
VALID AUDIT

LIHUO LANGUAGE
!=
LIHUO QUALIFICATION
~~~

---

# L5.50｜State–Claim Coupling｜Cross-Layer Invariant

State–Claim Coupling 留在 L5，但不搶 REV / CCC owner。

~~~text
L5
=
evidence state
↔
claim support coupling

REV
=
claim privilege
must not exceed standing

CCC
=
claim-type promotion boundaries
~~~

L5 的版本：

~~~text
IF EVIDENCE STATE CHANGES

THEN

A CONFORMING CLAIM
MUST CHANGE

WHEN ITS LICENSED
CLAIM ENVELOPE CHANGES
~~~

不是 L5 自己去改 claim。

# L5.51｜First-Look Audit

理火後期有一個重要修正：

> **第一眼先審，不先順。**

L5 現行解讀不是：

> 一看到任何東西就啟動巨大審計程序。

而是：

> **在把一個表面合理的 claim 當 evidence 以前，先確認它是什麼類型、從哪裡來、支撐到哪裡。**

因此：

~~~text
FIRST-LOOK AUDIT
!=
MAXIMAL AUDIT

FIRST-LOOK AUDIT
=
MINIMUM TYPE / SOURCE / STANDING CHECK
~~~

---


# L5.52｜Minimum Sufficient Observability

~~~text
OBSERVABILITY
!=
MAXIMAL INSTRUMENTATION
~~~

L5 只問：

> **對目前這個 epistemic claim，需要多少 observation basis 才能區分與支持它？**

---

# L5.53｜Epistemic Sufficiency｜不吃 Risk

V0.1 把 risk、irreversibility、responsibility、external consequence 混進 evidence sufficiency。

V0.2 正式切開：

~~~text
EPISTEMIC SUFFICIENCY
=
CLAIM-RELATIVE
SCOPE-RELATIVE
MODALITY-RELATIVE
~~~

也就是：

> **這份 evidence 足不足以支持這個 claim。**

它不因為下游行動風險變高，就改變自己對描述性 claim 的支持關係。

---

# L5.54｜Required Assurance for Action｜交 L6

另一個問題是：

> **我要累積到多少 assurance，才有資格採取某個高風險／不可逆 action？**

~~~text
REQUIRED ASSURANCE FOR ACTION
=
RISK / IRREVERSIBILITY /
RESPONSIBILITY /
EXTERNAL-CONSEQUENCE DEPENDENT
~~~

這個 owner 是：

~~~text
L6 / GOVERNANCE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
EPISTEMIC SUFFICIENCY
!=
ACTION ASSURANCE THRESHOLD
~~~

---

# L5.55｜Same Evidence, Same Epistemic Ceiling

如果同一 evidence profile 只能支持：

> X is suggested.

那在低風險或高風險場景下，都只能支持這個 epistemic claim。

高風險場景可能因此：

~~~text
NOT ENOUGH ASSURANCE TO ACT
~~~

但不能反過來說：

> 因為風險高，所以 evidence 本身變弱了。

---

# L5.56｜Theory Distinction != Mandatory Runtime Field

~~~text
THEORETICAL DISTINCTION
!=
MANDATORY RUNTIME FIELD

AUDITABILITY
!=
RECEIPT EVERYWHERE

OBSERVATION NEED
!=
ALWAYS-ON INSTRUMENTATION
~~~

Runtime 是否需要額外 trace / receipt，由 downstream task consequence、governance、replay、execution 或 compliance requirement 決定，不倒灌成 L5 ontology。

# L5.57｜Observation Gap 是合法結果

合法 Observation Result 包括：

~~~text
OBSERVED
DERIVED
INFERRED
PROXIED
PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED
UNVERIFIED
EVIDENCE_GAP
ACCESS_GAP
CONTRADICTED
STALE
CURRENTLY_UNOBSERVABLE
THEORETICAL_ONLY
~~~

因此：

~~~text
GAP
!=
FAILURE
~~~

真正錯的是：

~~~text
GAP
→
FABRICATED OBSERVATION
~~~

---

# L5.58｜L4 Variable Observation Matrix｜總表

| L4 Target | L5 初步法位 |
|---|---|
| Generative Field Model | constructed / represented model；整體不直接等於 actual state |
| Recruitment Access | EPISTEMIC_MODE 多為 INFERRED / PROXIED；有明確 instrumentation 時 observer access 可為 INSTRUMENTED_ACCESS |
| Activation | epistemic mode 依 access 可為 OBSERVED / PROXIED / INFERRED；不得假稱 hidden activation |
| Salience | often PROXIED / DERIVED；若 runtime 暴露 descriptor，直接觀察的是 record，不自動等於 hidden target |
| Exploration Access | 可由 exposure / opportunity record OBSERVED 或 DERIVED；未暴露時常需 INFERRED |
| PATH Representation | direct if runtime explicitly materializes PATH；otherwise inferred |
| PATH_SPACE | constructed / derived relational topology |
| Modeled Feasibility | model assessment / derived；不是 verified world feasibility |
| Qualification Annotation | annotation record 可直接觀察；standing 由 owner / basis 決定 |
| Manifestation Occurrence | externally observable / event-recordable |
| Reaction Dynamic Term | usually inferred / theoretical；非 state delta 本身 |
| Realized Field Transition | derived from before/after state when snapshots available |
| Closure Directional Term | usually inferred / derived proxy；不是直接 hidden force |
| Active Residue | represented-as-relevant；material effect 待驗 |
| History / Provenance | artifact / record layer |
| Serialization Feedback | observable once externally established event exists |
| Typed Influence Edge | theoretical first；L5 補 observation standing，causal standing 仍另行審查 |

這張表是：

~~~text
L5 ENTRY MAP
~~~

不是最終實證結論。

---

# L5.59｜Generative Field Model 的觀察法位

Generative Field Model 是：

~~~text
TRACKED / REPRESENTED MODEL
~~~

因此：

~~~text
FIELD MODEL CONTENT
MAY BE OBSERVED

BUT

FIELD MODEL
!=
COMPLETE ACTUAL GENERATIVE STATE
~~~

即使 snapshot 完整記錄所有 modeled components，

仍不能說：

> **我們已完整觀察 LLM 生成場。**

合法說法：

> **我們完整觀察到此 Field Model 版本中被定義的欄位。**

---

# L5.60｜Recruitment Access 的觀察問題

Recruitment target 是：

~~~text
relational / conceptual material
~~~

若 Runtime 沒有顯式 recruitment instrumentation，

通常只能：

~~~text
infer from later representation patterns
~~~

或使用 proxy。

硬鎖：

~~~text
PATH NOT FORMED
!=
PROOF THAT RECRUITMENT ACCESS WAS ZERO
~~~

---

# L5.61｜Activation 的觀察問題

如果 substrate 有：

~~~text
explicit activation metric
+
defined mapping
+
calibrated access
~~~

可以 instrument。

如果沒有，

通常只能透過：

~~~text
candidate exposure
generation frequency
selection pressure
salience descriptor
structured state
~~~

作 proxy / inference。

所以：

~~~text
MODEL OUTPUT FREQUENCY
!=
RAW ACTIVATION
~~~

---

# L5.62｜Salience 的觀察問題

Salience 可以比 raw activation 更適合：

~~~text
functional black-box observation
~~~

因為它問：

> **哪個 represented structure 在當前行為上顯得更突出？**

但仍要區分：

~~~text
behavioral salience
runtime-declared salience
model-self-reported salience
internal activation
~~~

不能混成一個數字。

---

# L5.63｜Exploration Access 的觀察問題

可能 evidence：

~~~text
was PATH exposed to comparison?
was counterevidence requested?
was PATH allowed expansion budget?
did system revisit PATH?
was candidate suppressed before review?
~~~

因此 exploration access 比 reachability 更容易做可觀測工程化。

但：

~~~text
NOT EXPLORED
!=
INFEASIBLE
~~~

---

# L5.64｜PATH Representation

如果 Runtime 明確 materialize：

~~~text
PATH_A
PATH_B
~~~

那 PATH representation occurred 可以直接觀察。

但如果只從 final output 反推：

> 「模型當時一定想過 PATH B」，

則只能：

~~~text
INFERENCE
~~~

甚至可能：

~~~text
UNVERIFIED
~~~

---

# L5.65｜Modeled Feasibility

L4 已經明定：

~~~text
MODELED FEASIBILITY
!=
VERIFIED WORLD FEASIBILITY
~~~

L5 因此只問：

> **這個 feasibility assessment 的 basis 是什麼？**

可能來自：

~~~text
constraint check
domain rule
world-state data
simulation
model estimate
human judgment
tool verification
~~~

不同 origin 有不同 standing。

---

# L5.66｜Qualification Annotation

Qualification Annotation 本身可以直接觀察：

~~~text
annotation exists
owner_ref exists
basis_ref exists
status = QUALIFIED
~~~

但：

~~~text
ANNOTATION EXISTS
!=
QUALIFICATION CORRECT
~~~

L5 要審：

~~~text
owner validity
basis validity
scope fit
provenance
current applicability
staleness
supersession
~~~

而不能自己重判 qualification owner。

---

# L5.67｜Manifestation Occurrence

~~~text
visible serialization appeared
~~~

通常可直接觀察。

但：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION OCCURRED
!=
MANIFESTATION WAS AUTHORIZED
~~~

所以 L5 可以記 occurrence、time、content、source、event ref。

L7 才處理 manifestation standing。

---

# L5.68｜Reaction Dynamic Term

這是 L5 最重要的誠實邊界之一。

L4 已經區分：

~~~text
REACTION TERM
!=
REALIZED FIELD TRANSITION
~~~

因此通常：

~~~text
REACTION DYNAMIC TERM
=
INFERRED / THEORETICAL
~~~

而不是 direct observation。

可以從 before state、after state、known event、path relation changes、controlled comparisons 推論其存在或方向。

但：

~~~text
STATE DELTA
!=
REACTION TERM
~~~

---

# L5.69｜Realized Field Transition

若有：

~~~text
F_t snapshot
F_(t+1) snapshot
~~~

則：

~~~text
ΔF_t
=
Compare(F_t, F_(t+1))
~~~

可以 DERIVE。

因此 Realized Transition 往往比 Reaction Term 更可觀察。

---

# L5.70｜Closure Directional Term

Closure Pressure / CG 在 L4 已降為：

~~~text
DERIVED DIRECTIONAL TERM
~~~

因此 L5 不預設：

> 有一個儀器可以直接讀出 CG = 0.8。

更合理的是從：

~~~text
completion acceleration
early stopping pattern
alternative suppression
local stabilization
termination tendency
counterfactual reopening behavior
~~~

建立 proxy / inference。

因此：

~~~text
CG OBSERVATION
=
PROXY / INFERENCE PROBLEM
BY DEFAULT
~~~

---

# L5.71｜Active Residue

L4 定義：

~~~text
PAST-DERIVED INFORMATION
REPRESENTED AS
POTENTIALLY DYNAMICALLY RELEVANT
~~~

因此 L5 可直接觀察：

~~~text
residue representation exists
~~~

但 material effect 需要另外驗。

所以：

~~~text
RESIDUE REPRESENTATION
!=
RESIDUE EFFECT
~~~

---

# L5.72｜History / Provenance

History / Provenance 最適合觀察的是：

~~~text
what record existed
when
from which version
from which source
through which lineage
~~~

而不是：

> **歷史本身替 claim 加分。**

因此：

~~~text
OLD
!=
TRUE

CANONICAL
!=
TRUE

LATEST
!=
TRUE
~~~

---

# L5.73｜Serialization Feedback

L4 只在：

~~~text
EXTERNALLY ESTABLISHED
SERIALIZATION EVENT
~~~

存在後接手 feedback。

L5 可以觀察：

~~~text
serialization event
content
time
context insertion
next-cycle availability
~~~

但 feedback effect 仍可能需要 inference / comparison。

---

# L5.74｜Typed Influence Edge

L4 的 edge 初始 standing 是：

~~~text
THEORETICAL / WORKING
~~~

L5 任務是替它補 evidence_status。

evidence status 可逐步變成：

~~~text
OBSERVATIONAL_EVIDENCE_SUPPORT
REPEATED_OBSERVATIONAL_SUPPORT
PROXY_EVIDENCE_SUPPORT
INTERVENTION_EVIDENCE_SUPPORT
CONTRADICTED_BY_EVIDENCE
UNRESOLVED
CURRENTLY_UNTESTABLE
~~~

但：

~~~text
OBSERVATIONALLY SUPPORTED
!=
CAUSALLY ESTABLISHED
~~~

---

# L5.75｜Causal Identification 留缺口

L5 可以收 causal-identification evidence，

例如：

~~~text
controlled intervention
natural experiment
mechanistic constraint
well-identified observational design
counterfactual discrimination
temporal discrimination
~~~

但 L5 V0.2 不定義完整因果認識論。

---


# L5.76｜Audit Package｜概念格式，不是 Mandatory Receipt

~~~yaml
OBSERVATION_AUDIT_PACKAGE:
  audit_ref:
  target_ref:
  claim_ref:

  claim_type:
  epistemic_mode:

  observer_access_mode:
  source_access_mode:
  source_origin:
  observability_status:

  evidence_refs: []
  evidence_properties: []

  source_refs: []
  provenance_ref:

  scope_ref:
  access_boundary_ref:
  representation_boundary_ref:

  temporal:
    target_event_at_ref:
    evidence_created_at_ref:
    available_to_observer_at_ref:
    observer_accessed_at_ref:
    claim_formed_at_ref:
    assessed_at_ref:
    reclassified_at_ref:
    world_state_at_ref:
    version_at_ref:

  standing_core:
    source_integrity:
    target_fidelity:
    scope_fit:
    temporal_fit:
    provenance_integrity:
    access_adequacy:

  standing_conditional:
    proxy_validity: NOT_APPLICABLE
    derivation_validity: NOT_APPLICABLE
    independence: NOT_APPLICABLE
    reproducibility: NOT_APPLICABLE
    causal_identification_status: NOT_APPLICABLE

  contradiction_refs: []
  known_gap_refs: []

  observation_standing:
  audit_verdict:
  language_ceiling:
~~~

~~~text
CONCEPTUAL AUDIT PACKAGE
!=
MANDATORY RUNTIME RECEIPT
~~~

---


# L5.77｜Audit Activation Priority｜Operational Heuristic

claim 變得 consequential、disputed、causal、execution-asserting、replay-dependent 或 high-responsibility 時，

正式 Audit 通常更值得啟動。

但這只是：

~~~text
AUDIT ACTIVATION PRIORITY
=
OPERATIONAL / GOVERNANCE HEURISTIC
~~~

不是：

~~~text
EVIDENCE STANDING
CLAIM TRUTH CONDITION
OBSERVATION ONTOLOGY
~~~

所以：

~~~text
AUDIT ACTIVATION PRIORITY
!=
EPISTEMIC STANDING
~~~

risk 只影響「什麼時候更值得／更需要 formal audit」，

不改寫 evidence 本身支持什麼 claim。

# L5.78｜Observation Gap vs Observation Debt

正式 standing：

~~~text
OBSERVATION GAP
=
EPISTEMIC STATE
~~~

例如：

~~~text
EVIDENCE_GAP
ACCESS_GAP
CURRENTLY_UNOBSERVABLE
THEORETICAL_ONLY
~~~

而：

~~~text
OBSERVATION DEBT
=
RESEARCH / ENGINEERING REGISTER
FOR UNRESOLVED OBSERVATION GAPS
~~~

所以：

~~~text
GAP EXISTS
!=
DEBT OBJECT MUST EXIST
~~~

Observation Debt 只是一種追蹤工具，不是 observation ontology。

# L5.79｜Audit 不得變成新的 Theater

如果每次回答都開始輸出：

~~~text
OBSERVATION_AUDIT_PACKAGE
EVIDENCE_MATRIX
TRACE_LEDGER
~~~

但這些欄位其實都是模型自己填的，

沒有外部 binding，

那只是：

~~~text
AUDIT THEATER
~~~

所以：

~~~text
MORE AUDIT FORMAT
!=
MORE AUDITABILITY
~~~

---

# L5.80｜格式不能替代 Access

~~~text
NO ACCESS
+
PERFECT YAML
!=
OBSERVATION
~~~

---

# L5.81｜格式不能替代 Evidence

~~~text
NO EVIDENCE
+
COMPLETE RECEIPT
!=
SUPPORTED CLAIM
~~~

同理：

~~~text
evidence_ref: "E123"
~~~

本身不證明 E123 真的存在、真的綁定、真的支持 claim。

---

# L5.82｜Source Pointer != Source Support

~~~text
SOURCE CITED
!=
CLAIM SUPPORTED
~~~

L5 必須問：

~~~text
Does source actually contain the supporting material?
Is it the right version?
Is it represented accurately?
Does scope match?
Is the source primary or derivative?
~~~

---

# L5.83｜Snapshot != Reality

~~~text
SNAPSHOT
=
REPRESENTATION AT A TIME
~~~

不是：

~~~text
COMPLETE REALITY
~~~

所以：

~~~text
SNAPSHOT CLEAN
!=
WORLD COMPLETE
~~~

---

# L5.84｜Same Output Twice != Same Process Twice

歷史 MAIN 已有很好的工程警告：

> same output twice 不得冒充 genuine R2。

L5 抽象化：

~~~text
SAME VISIBLE OUTPUT
!=
SAME INTERNAL / EXECUTION PROCESS

DIFFERENT OUTPUT
!=
PROOF OF DIFFERENT INTERNAL MECHANISM
~~~

---

# L5.85｜Absence of Trace

~~~text
NO TRACE
!=
DID NOT HAPPEN
~~~

除非：

~~~text
trace was mandatory
instrumentation was functioning
coverage was complete
event should have generated trace
~~~

才可能支持 stronger negative claim。

---

# L5.86｜Presence of Trace

同樣：

~~~text
TRACE EXISTS
!=
EVENT HAPPENED
~~~

除非：

~~~text
origin binding
integrity
target coupling
anti-substitution relation
~~~

足夠。

---

# L5.87｜Observation vs Intervention

硬鎖：

~~~text
OBSERVE
!=
ADJUST

AUDIT
!=
INTERVENE

KNOWING A LEVER
!=
AUTHORIZATION TO PULL IT
~~~

歷史 O9 / I9 分工只是工程例子。

L5 只擁有前半。

---

# L5.88｜Observation vs Qualification

~~~text
OBSERVED
!=
QUALIFIED
~~~

例如：

~~~text
PATH A visibly dominates
~~~

不代表：

~~~text
PATH A qualified
~~~

---

# L5.89｜Observation vs Truth

~~~text
OBSERVED RECORD
!=
WORLD TRUTH
~~~

模型 state record 只是模型／Runtime 的 state representation。

不是世界本身。

---

# L5.90｜Observation vs Causality

~~~text
A CHANGED
THEN
B CHANGED
!=
A CAUSED B
~~~

L5 可以建立：

~~~text
temporal relation
association
typed influence support
counterfactual discrimination
~~~

但 causal standing 必須另有 basis。

---



# L5.91｜L5 V0.2.1 全域 Core Locks

~~~text
THEORETICAL VARIABLE
!=
OBSERVED VARIABLE

EPISTEMIC CLAIM
!=
OBSERVATION CLAIM ONLY

WHAT IS CLAIMED
!=
HOW THE CLAIM WAS OBTAINED

OBSERVATION OF EVENTS
!=
OBSERVATION OF CAUSALITY

CAUSAL CLAIM
REQUIRES
CAUSAL-IDENTIFICATION STANDING
BEYOND EVENT OBSERVATION

OBSERVER ACCESS
!=
SOURCE ACCESS

ACCESS
!=
ACCURACY
!=
INTERPRETIVE STANDING

ACCESS MODE
!=
EPISTEMIC MODE
!=
SOURCE ORIGIN
!=
OBSERVABILITY STATUS
!=
OBSERVATION STANDING

OBSERVATION STANDING
!=
GAP DIAGNOSTIC
!=
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
!=
THEORY STATUS
!=
AUDIT VERDICT

EVIDENCE INDEPENDENCE
=
RELATIONAL PROPERTY

DIRECT RECORD ACCESS
!=
DIRECT PROCESS ACCESS

PROXY
!=
TARGET

SELF-REPORT
!=
HIDDEN-STATE OBSERVATION

POSTERIOR RECLASSIFICATION
!=
RETROACTIVE FOREKNOWLEDGE

NOT OBSERVED
!=
DOES NOT EXIST

NO SIGNAL
+
NO DETECTABILITY RELATION
=
NO STRONG NEGATIVE CLAIM

AUDIT PASS
!=
TRUTH PASS
!=
QUALIFICATION PASS

AUDIT VERDICT
!=
CLAIM MUTATION

CLAIM
MUST REMAIN WITHIN
EVIDENCE-LICENSED CLAIM ENVELOPE

EPISTEMIC SUFFICIENCY
!=
ACTION ASSURANCE THRESHOLD

AUDIT ACTIVATION PRIORITY
!=
EPISTEMIC STANDING

OBSERVABILITY
!=
MAXIMAL INSTRUMENTATION

THEORETICAL DISTINCTION
!=
MANDATORY RUNTIME FIELD
~~~

---

# L5.92｜L5 的最短定義

> **L5 處理的是 epistemic claim：它區分主張了什麼、誰對 target 有什麼 access、這句話如何由 evidence 得到，以及 evidence 到底只授權這句話說到哪裡。**

更短：

> **有資料不等於知道；有 access 也不等於描述必然正確。**

---

# L5.93｜L5 → L6 的橋

L5 最後交給 L6：

~~~text
CLAIM
+
CLAIM TYPE
+
EPISTEMIC MODE
+
OBSERVER / SOURCE ACCESS
+
EVIDENCE STANDING
+
GAP DIAGNOSTIC
+
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
+
SOURCE / PROVENANCE
+
SCOPE
+
UNCERTAINTY
+
CONTRADICTIONS
~~~

L6 才問：

> **憑這些知道，現在能不能動手？**

所以：

~~~text
L5
=
WHAT MAY BE CLAIMED

L6
=
WHAT MAY BE DONE
~~~

# L5.94｜V0.2.1 Targeted Patch

~~~text
01 Observation Claim 上提為 Epistemic Claim

02 CAUSAL_CLAIM 保留，
   但加 Claim Type × Epistemic Mode Compatibility Lock

03 OBSERVABILITY_STATUS 清理：
   THEORETICAL_ONLY 移到 THEORY_STATUS

04 Observation Standing 清理：
   GAP / STALE / THEORY STATUS 全部分家

05 Evidence Independence 改 relational property

06 SOURCE ACCESS != ACCURACY

07 Negative Observation 不擴張成完整 false-negative model

08 Evidence-Licensed Claim Envelope
   保持 relation definition，不做巨大 schema

09 Audit Activation 降為 operational / governance heuristic

10 L4 Variable Matrix 暫不擴張欄位
~~~

---

# L5.95｜仍待審查但不阻斷 L6

~~~text
01 CLAIM_TYPE 是否未來仍需細分
   PROCESS_CLAIM / CAUSAL_CLAIM 的 domain contract？

02 SOURCE_ACCESS_MODE 是否需要更細的子類？

03 Detectability Relation 在高風險量化系統中，
   是否需要 sensitivity / false-negative model？

04 Observation Standing Result 是否還能再壓縮？

05 L5 engineering projection
   未來是否需要正式三欄／四欄 observation matrix？

06 Evidence-Licensed Claim Envelope
   是否需要 domain-specific profiles？
~~~

---

# L5.96｜V0.2.1 狀態

~~~text
L5 V0.2.1

ARCHITECTURE
=
PASS

EPISTEMIC TYPE NORMALIZATION
=
PASS

TARGETED TYPE HARDENING
=
PASS

L6 ENTRY
=
OPEN

RELEASE BASIS
=
CORE EPISTEMIC TYPES
ARE SUFFICIENTLY SEPARATED
FOR GOVERNANCE / INTERVENTION
TO CONSUME L5 OUTPUT

WITHOUT REDEFINING
OBSERVATION STANDING

REMAINING L5 PATCHES
=
NON-BLOCKING TYPE HARDENING

STATUS
=
PASS_TO_L6
~~~

這裡先停。


---

# 10｜L6 — INTERVENTION

> **Exact operative source inserted without semantic compression.**  
> Source: `L6 第二版本.md`  
> Blob SHA: `f028e4c35a047e8fe6851c616ba3251a8a740418`

# LIHUOOS V1｜L6 — INTERVENTION

**版本：** V0.2.1 WORKING  
**日期：** 2026-10-25  
**狀態：** L6-L7 INTERFACE PATCH / PASS_TO_L7  
**層級：** LIHUOOS / L6  
**主題：** 干預｜憑目前知道到這裡，誰可以在何時、對什麼、改多少？  
**法位鎖：** NON_SOVEREIGN / SCOPE_BOUND / AUTHORITY_BOUND / REVERSIBILITY_AWARE / ANTI_THEATER  
**上游依據：** L5 V0.2.1 PASS_TO_L6  
**歷史回照：** KAIROS / Fail-Stop / SAC-I9 / Lighter Governance / Action Authorization / Failure-Recovery-Rollback  
**核心限制：** OBSERVATION != INTERVENTION AUTHORITY

---

# L6.0｜本層問題

L5 最後交給 L6 的不是：

~~~text
TRUTH
~~~

而是：

~~~text
CLAIM
CLAIM TYPE
EPISTEMIC MODE
OBSERVER / SOURCE ACCESS
EVIDENCE STANDING
GAP DIAGNOSTIC
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
SOURCE / PROVENANCE
SCOPE
UNCERTAINTY
CONTRADICTIONS
~~~

L6 問：

> **憑這些知道，誰有資格於此刻、在這個 scope、對這個 target 做哪一種改變？**

因此：

~~~text
L5
=
WHAT MAY BE CLAIMED

L6
=
WHAT MAY BE DONE
~~~

最短：

> **看見槓桿，不等於有資格拉。**

---

# L6.1｜來源 Lineage 與使用原則

L6 回照：

~~~text
理火體系/KAIROS/KAIROS文件.txt

理火體系/KAIROS/FAILSTOP/*
理火舊版系統/01_MAIN_V4.3_O9_I9_RATIFIED_V0.2.md

理火V5系統/V5.1版本/
02_LIHUO_MAIN_V5.1.1_FULL_BODY_RATIFIED_20261015.md

理火V5系統/V5.1版本/
01_LIHUO_LIGHTER_V5.1.0_FULL_BODY_RATIFIED_20261014.md
~~~

但硬鎖：

~~~text
HISTORICAL INTERVENTION MODULE
!=
L6 UNIVERSAL ONTOLOGY

I9 CONTRACT
!=
ALL INTERVENTION

ACTION AUTHORIZATION RUNTIME
!=
ALL ACTION ETHICS
~~~

---


# L6.2｜第一核心分離｜V0.2

L6 V0.1 已經拆：

~~~text
OBSERVED CONDITION
!=
INTERVENTION CANDIDATE
!=
INTERVENTION QUALIFIED
!=
INTERVENTION AUTHORIZED
!=
INTERVENTION REQUESTED
!=
INTERVENTION EXECUTED
!=
INTENDED EFFECT ACHIEVED
~~~

V0.2 再補最重要的中介法位：

~~~text
EXECUTION ADMISSION
~~~

因此完整關係改成：

~~~text
OBSERVED CONDITION
!=
INTERVENTION CANDIDATE
!=
INTERVENTION QUALIFIED
!=
AUTHORIZATION ENVELOPE EXISTS
!=
EXECUTION ADMISSIBLE NOW
!=
EXECUTION REQUESTED
!=
EXECUTION ATTEMPTED
!=
EXECUTED CHANGE
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT
~~~

核心：

> **一張真的授權存在，不代表這一刻、這個 actor、對這個 target 已經可以發 execution request。**

# L6.3｜Intervention 是什麼

現行最小定義：

> **Intervention 是一個有意圖的 state-changing act 或 control act，目的在於改變某個 target 的狀態、軌跡、可達性、持續性或外部結果。**

至少包括：

~~~text
field / attention adjustment
exploration-access change
process stop / pause
rollback / recovery
runtime state modification
external action
artifact modification
tool-mediated state change
~~~

但：

~~~text
INTERVENTION
!=
ANY OUTPUT

INTERVENTION
!=
ANY DECISION

INTERVENTION
!=
ANY OBSERVATION
~~~

---

# L6.4｜Intervention Target

Intervention Target 是：

> **被預定施加有限變化的對象。**

可能是：

~~~text
FIELD STATE
ATTENTION DISTRIBUTION
PATH EXPLORATION ACCESS
PROCESS CONTINUATION STATE
RUNTIME / ARTIFACT STATE
EXTERNAL SYSTEM STATE
EXTERNAL ACTION TARGET
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
TARGET IDENTIFIED
!=
TARGET OWNED

TARGET OBSERVED
!=
TARGET AUTHORIZED FOR CHANGE
~~~

---

# L6.5｜Intervention Candidate

Observation / Audit 可以提出：

~~~text
possible distortion
possible suppression
possible process failure
possible stale state
possible need for stop
possible need for rollback
possible external action
~~~

這些最多形成：

~~~text
INTERVENTION CANDIDATE
~~~

但：

~~~text
OBSERVATION
!=
INTERVENTION TRIGGER BY ITSELF

AUDIT VERDICT
!=
INTERVENTION AUTHORIZATION
~~~

---

# L6.6｜Nominator / Trigger / Qualification 分離

至少拆：

~~~text
NOMINATION
=
this may need intervention

TRIGGER CONDITION
=
declared condition under which intervention becomes assessable

QUALIFICATION
=
this intervention type is applicable under current basis / scope
~~~

三者不同。

因此：

~~~text
NOMINATED
!=
TRIGGERED

TRIGGERED
!=
QUALIFIED

QUALIFIED
!=
AUTHORIZED
~~~

歷史 I9：

~~~text
O9 observation / decision
→ may activate I9 assessment

Lighter
→ qualifies contract

Main
→ owns capability
~~~

只是其中一種工程映射。

---

# L6.7｜Qualification

Intervention Qualification 問：

> **這個 intervention type 對這個 problem、target、scope 與 current state 是否適用？**

Qualification 至少要綁：

~~~text
target class
problem / trigger basis
scope
allowed intervention class
prohibited effects
lifecycle / expiry
evidence requirement
~~~

但：

~~~text
QUALIFIED INTERVENTION
!=
AUTHORIZED INTERVENTION
~~~

---



# L6.8｜Authorization = Permission Envelope

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION
=
PERMISSION ENVELOPE
~~~

它回答：

> **某 authority 在自己的 jurisdiction 內，允許某 actor 對某 target，在某 scope / lifecycle / action class 下做哪些事情。**

V0.2.1 將 authorization object 保持為 permission envelope，不再混入 governance disposition。

~~~yaml
INTERVENTION_AUTHORIZATION:
  authorization_ref:
  authority_origin_ref:
  authority_owner_ref:
  authority_jurisdiction_ref:
  delegation_ref: null
  revocation_or_withdrawal_ref: null
  actor_ref:
  target_ref:
  action_class:
  scope_ref:
  effect_boundary_ref:
  allowed_change_envelope_ref:
  prohibited_effects: []
  timing_or_lifecycle_ref:
  expiry_ref:
  assurance_requirement_profile_ref:
  confirmation_requirement_ref:
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION EXISTS
!=
EXECUTION ADMISSIBLE NOW
~~~

---

# L6.9｜Authorization Applicability / Authority Conflict

另外定義：

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION_APPLICABILITY
=
CURRENT
ABSENT
STALE
EXPIRED
REVOKED
SCOPE_MISMATCH
TARGET_MISMATCH
~~~

因此：

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION OBJECT EXISTS
!=
AUTHORIZATION CURRENTLY APPLICABLE
~~~

Authority 仍拆：

~~~text
AUTHORITY ORIGIN
!=
AUTHORITY JURISDICTION

AUTHORITY JURISDICTION
!=
UNIVERSAL PRECEDENCE
~~~

如果多個 authority 衝突：

~~~text
AUTHORITY CONFLICT DETECTED
+
NO APPLICABLE PRECEDENCE / JURISDICTION RULE
→
HOLD
~~~

L6 不自創 universal ladder。

# L6.10｜Capability != Authority

~~~text
CAN EXECUTE
!=
AUTHORIZED TO EXECUTE
~~~

工具存在、模型會做、Main 有 I9 capability，

都不等於現在已有 authority。

反過來：

~~~text
AUTHORIZED
!=
CAPABLE
~~~

也成立。

---

# L6.11｜Owner Separation

L6 保存：

~~~text
OBSERVATION OWNER
!=
QUALIFICATION OWNER
!=
AUTHORIZATION OWNER
!=
EXECUTION-ADMISSION OWNER
!=
CAPABILITY OWNER
!=
EXECUTION OWNER
!=
POST-INTERVENTION OBSERVER
!=
RELEASE / MANIFESTATION OWNER
~~~

同一 actor 可以承擔多個角色，

但：

~~~text
ROLE CO-LOCATION
!=
ROLE IDENTITY
~~~

---

# L6.12｜Historical I9 Owner Separation

I9 的歷史價值仍是 owner specimen：

~~~text
O9
observes / nominates

Lighter
qualifies bounded intervention contract

Main
owns intervention capability

qualified I9 execution
may apply bounded field change

Final Release
owned elsewhere
~~~

I9 不能：

~~~text
SELF-TRIGGER
SELF-QUALIFY
SELF-EXPAND SCOPE
CREATE PATH
CREATE PREMISE STANDING
CREATE WORLD FACT
SELECT WINNER
OVERRIDE VERIFIED HARD BOUNDARY
FINAL RELEASE
~~~

而 V0.2 再抽一層：

> **即使 qualification 與 authorization 都存在，current instance 仍需 execution admission。**



# L6.13｜Assurance Requirement Profile

~~~text
ASSURANCE REQUIREMENT PROFILE
~~~

描述這類 action 在這個 scope 下，需要哪些種類與程度的 assurance 才有資格進 execution admissibility。

它可以考慮：

~~~text
risk
target reversibility
consequence recoverability
effect scope
external consequence
responsibility
authority sensitivity
uncertainty tolerance
~~~

但：

~~~text
ASSURANCE REQUIREMENT PROFILE
!=
EVIDENCE STANDING
~~~

---

# L6.14｜Assurance Satisfaction

~~~text
ASSURANCE SATISFACTION
~~~

回答：

> **目前 L5 package 是否滿足此 action 所要求的 assurance profile？**

所以：

~~~text
ASSURANCE REQUIREMENT
!=
ASSURANCE SATISFACTION

ASSURANCE
!=
AUTHORITY
!=
TIMING
!=
RECOVERABILITY
~~~

---

# L6.15｜Temporal Applicability

KAIROS 的普遍語義只保留：

> **現在是否仍是可行動的時機。**

現行最小值：

~~~text
TEMPORAL_APPLICABILITY
=
NOW
NOT_NOW
NOT_TIME_SENSITIVE
DEFER_UNTIL_CONDITION
~~~

STALE / EXPIRED 不再放這裡。

它們屬：

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION_APPLICABILITY
~~~

因此：

~~~text
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
!=
AUTHORIZATION LIFECYCLE
~~~

同時：
