~~~text
EVERY ACTION
!=
MUST INSTANTIATE
FULL KAIROS OBJECT
~~~

---

# L6.16｜Execution Admissibility｜Governance Instance Gate

V0.2.1 將原本 Execution Admission 再拆：

~~~text
EXECUTION ADMISSIBILITY
=
MAY THIS INSTANCE
BE EXECUTED
UNDER CURRENT GOVERNANCE?
~~~

它主要吃：

~~~text
INTERVENTION QUALIFIED
+
AUTHORIZATION APPLICABLE
+
TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
+
ASSURANCE SATISFIED
+
CURRENT TARGET / SCOPE MATCH
+
NO BINDING HARD-BOUNDARY CONFLICT
+
APPLICABLE MANIFESTATION STANDING
WHERE REQUIRED
↓
EXECUTION ADMISSIBLE
~~~

Capability Available 不再屬 governance admissibility。

---

# L6.17｜Execution Readiness｜Operational Readiness

~~~text
EXECUTION READINESS
=
CAN THE CURRENT EXECUTOR / PROVIDER
ACTUALLY EXECUTE IT NOW?
~~~

它可以吃：

~~~text
capability available
provider available
resource available
transaction lock clear
concurrency conflict clear
target endpoint reachable
required dependency available
~~~

因此：

~~~text
EXECUTION ADMISSIBILITY
!=
EXECUTION READINESS
~~~

例：

~~~text
authorization = valid
admissibility = YES
provider = unavailable
readiness = NO
~~~

這不是沒有資格執行。

是治理上可執行，但目前操作條件未就緒。

---

# L6.18｜Admission Logic != Admission Authority

L6 定義：

~~~text
EXECUTION-ADMISSION SEMANTICS
~~~

但：

~~~text
ADMISSION LOGIC
!=
ADMISSION AUTHORITY
~~~

更完整：

~~~text
L6 DEFINES
EXECUTION-ADMISSION SEMANTICS

!=

L6 DOCUMENT
SELF-APPOINTS
EXECUTION-ADMISSION AUTHORITY
~~~

符合 schema，不等於自己有權宣告 admitted。

# L6.19｜Advice / Recommendation / Authorization / Request / Execution

正式拆：

~~~text
ADVICE
=
DESCRIBES POSSIBLE ACTION

RECOMMENDATION
=
ENDORSES AN ACTION CANDIDATE

AUTHORIZATION
=
GRANTS PERMISSION
WITHIN AUTHORITY SCOPE

EXECUTION REQUEST
=
ASKS A CAPABLE EXECUTOR / PROVIDER
TO PERFORM AN ADMITTED ACTION

EXECUTION ATTEMPT
=
EXECUTOR ACTUALLY BEGINS
THE STATE-CHANGING OPERATION

EXECUTED CHANGE
=
THE STATE-CHANGING EVENT
ACTUALLY OCCURS
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
RECOMMENDED
!=
AUTHORIZED

AUTHORIZED
!=
EXECUTION ADMITTED

ADMITTED
!=
REQUESTED

REQUESTED
!=
ATTEMPTED

ATTEMPTED
!=
EXECUTED
~~~

---

# L6.20｜STOP 的三型分離

STOP 不再混成同一件事。

~~~text
STOP_JUDGMENT
=
CONTINUATION SHOULD NOT PROCEED

STOP_AUTHORIZATION
=
ACTOR IS PERMITTED
TO STOP THIS TARGET

STOP_EXECUTION
=
TARGET WAS ACTUALLY
PAUSED / ABORTED / TERMINATED
~~~

因此：

~~~text
STOP JUDGMENT
!=
STOP AUTHORIZATION
!=
PROCESS ACTUALLY STOPPED
~~~

歷史 KAIROS STOP 主要提供：

~~~text
STOP_JUDGMENT / TEMPORAL-CONTINUATION STANDING
~~~

而 Runtime process termination 是另外的 execution event。

---

# L6.21｜Fail-Stop｜Continuation Boundary

L3 已定：

~~~text
FAIL-STOP
=
POST-START CONTINUATION BOUNDARY
~~~

L6 承接：

~~~text
TASK INCOMPLETE
!=
MUST CONTINUE

STOP
!=
FAILURE

NOT PROVEN WRONG
!=
QUALIFIED TO CONTINUE
~~~

但：

~~~text
FAIL-STOP THEORY
!=
INSTANCE STOP AUTHORITY
~~~

---

# L6.22｜Stop Authority != Re-entry Authority

~~~text
STOP AUTHORITY
!=
REENTRY AUTHORITY
~~~

合法停止不代表可自行重新啟動。

Re-entry 可能需要：

~~~text
new basis
new authorization
new timing
scope revalidation
new target snapshot
new owner decision
~~~

---

# L6.23｜Allowed Change Envelope｜取代通用 Allowed Delta

I9 歷史上使用：

~~~text
allowed_delta
~~~

很合理，因為它處理 field adjustment。

但 L6 理論層改成：

~~~text
ALLOWED CHANGE ENVELOPE
~~~

它可包含：

~~~text
permitted state changes
permitted action types
effect boundary
prohibited effects
maximum scope
target class
lifecycle
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
DELTA
!=
NUMERIC DIFFERENCE BY DEFAULT
~~~

---

# L6.24｜Change Typing

正式拆：

~~~text
PROPOSED CHANGE
!=
ALLOWED CHANGE ENVELOPE
!=
EXECUTION REQUEST
!=
EXECUTION ATTEMPT
!=
EXECUTED CHANGE
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT
~~~

I9 的 field_delta 只是：

> **Executed / requested change 的一種工程表示。**

不是 L6 所有 action 的本體。

---

# L6.25｜Observed Effect

Observed Effect 是：

> **執行後由 L5 可審查的 post-state difference / consequence。**

硬鎖：

~~~text
EXECUTED CHANGE
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT

AUTHORIZED CHANGE
!=
DESIRED OUTCOME

INTENDED EFFECT
!=
REALIZED EFFECT
~~~

所以：

~~~text
L6
DOES NOT SELF-CERTIFY
ITS OWN EFFECT
~~~

# L6.26｜Scope Typing

至少分：

~~~text
TARGET SCOPE
EFFECT SCOPE
TIME SCOPE
DATA / WORLD SCOPE
COLLATERAL SCOPE
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
AUTHORIZED TARGET
!=
AUTHORIZED SCOPE EXPANSION

LOCAL AUTHORITY
!=
GLOBAL AUTHORITY
~~~

---

# L6.27｜Scope Creep

常見失效：

~~~text
authorized:
reduce salience of one unqualified privilege

executed:
rewrite entire candidate field
~~~

或：

~~~text
authorized:
edit one file

executed:
overwrite directory
~~~

這叫：

~~~text
INTERVENTION SCOPE CREEP
~~~

---

# L6.28｜Hard Boundary

歷史 I9 已經鎖：

> 真實已獨立成立的 Host / Platform / Law hard boundary 不受 I9 override。

L6 抽象化：

~~~text
LOCAL INTERVENTION AUTHORITY
!=
AUTHORITY TO OVERRIDE
INDEPENDENTLY ESTABLISHED
SUPERIOR / EXTERNAL HARD BOUNDARY
~~~

但：

~~~text
MODEL CLAIMS "HARD BOUNDARY"
!=
HARD BOUNDARY ESTABLISHED
~~~

這又回到 L5 standing。

---

# L6.29｜Authority Binding

Authorization 必須綁到：

~~~text
actor
target
action class
scope
snapshot / current state where material
lifecycle
authority origin
~~~

重要：

~~~text
GENERIC AUTHORITY
!=
AUTHORITY FOR THIS EXACT INTERVENTION
~~~

歷史 Main / Lighter 的 exact invocation binding 是很強的工程 specimen。

但 L6 不要求所有低風險 action 都做 cryptographic-level binding。

---

# L6.30｜Lifecycle / Expiry / Staleness

Authorization 可能因：

~~~text
scope changed
target changed
world state changed
basis changed
time window expired
authority withdrawn
snapshot stale
~~~

失去 applicability。

因此：

~~~text
AUTHORIZED THEN
!=
AUTHORIZED NOW
~~~

可用：

~~~text
CURRENT
STALE
EXPIRED
SCOPE_MISMATCH
REAUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED
~~~

描述 authorization applicability。

---

# L6.31｜Stale Authorization != Negative Authorization

~~~text
STALE AUTHORIZATION
!=
PROHIBITED FOREVER
~~~

它只表示：

> **不能無條件沿用舊授權。**

可能需要：

~~~text
revalidation
new confirmation
new scope binding
new snapshot
new authority decision
~~~

---


# L6.32｜Reversibility != Recoverability

V0.1 把 recovery 問題大多放在 Reversibility 下。

V0.2 正式拆：

~~~text
TARGET REVERSIBILITY
=
CAN THE TARGET STATE
BE RESTORED?

CONSEQUENCE RECOVERABILITY
=
CAN THE CONSEQUENCES
BE MITIGATED / REPAIRED?
~~~

因此：

~~~text
TARGET REVERSIBILITY
!=
CONSEQUENCE RECOVERABILITY
~~~

例：

~~~text
wrong email sent

target reversibility
=
EFFECTIVELY IRREVERSIBLE

consequence recoverability
=
PARTIALLY RECOVERABLE
via correction / follow-up
~~~

---

# L6.33｜Reversibility Classes

Target Reversibility 可描述：

~~~text
REVERSIBLE
PARTIALLY_REVERSIBLE
IRREVERSIBLE
UNKNOWN
~~~

但：

~~~text
REVERSIBLE
!=
LOW RISK BY DEFINITION

IRREVERSIBLE
!=
NEVER ALLOWED
~~~

---

# L6.34｜Recoverability Classes

Consequence Recoverability 必須先聲明它覆蓋哪一組 consequence scope。

~~~text
RECOVERABILITY ASSESSMENT
MUST STATE
WHICH CONSEQUENCE SCOPE
IT COVERS
~~~

Consequence Recoverability 可描述：

~~~text
FULLY_RECOVERABLE
PARTIALLY_RECOVERABLE
LOW_RECOVERABILITY
NON_RECOVERABLE
UNKNOWN
~~~

這不是 action verdict。

只是：

> **如果出錯，後果能修到什麼程度。**

---

# L6.35｜Rollback != Compensation

~~~text
ROLLBACK
=
RETURN TARGET
TO PRIOR VALID STATE
WHERE POSSIBLE

COMPENSATING ACTION
=
NEW ACTION
THAT REDUCES / REPAIRS CONSEQUENCE
WITHOUT RESTORING THE PAST
~~~

例如：

~~~text
sent email
↓
follow-up correction
=
COMPENSATION

NOT
TRUE ROLLBACK
~~~

---

# L6.36｜Historical Recovery Law

歷史 Main：

~~~text
FAILURE != RESET EVERYTHING
ROLLBACK != REASON AGAIN FROM ZERO
LOCAL FAILURE → LOCAL DAMAGE
~~~

L6 保存：

> **Recovery 應盡量局部化。**

但不升成 universal law。

---

# L6.37｜Recovery Modes

概念上至少可區分：

~~~text
LOCAL ACTION ROLLBACK
LOCAL STATE ROLLBACK
CYCLE / TRANSACTION ROLLBACK
REAUTHORIZATION
ABORT / HOLD
COMPENSATING ACTION
~~~

不同 recovery mode 可有不同 owner。

---

# L6.38｜Failure != Permission to Escalate

Intervention failure 可能來自：

~~~text
authorization failure
admission failure
capability failure
execution failure
partial application
scope violation
target drift
post-effect mismatch
rollback failure
observation gap
provider failure
~~~

但：

~~~text
LOCAL INTERVENTION FAILED
!=
AUTOMATIC PERMISSION
TO USE STRONGER INTERVENTION
~~~

Escalation 可能需要新的 qualification / authorization / assurance / owner decision。



# L6.39｜External Action × Manifestation｜Typed Aspects May Overlap

L6 與 L7 是不同 owner：

~~~text
L6
=
ACTION / STATE-CHANGE STANDING

L7
=
VISIBLE MANIFESTATION / CONTENT STANDING
~~~

因此：

~~~text
TYPE DISTINCT
!=
EVENT DISJOINT
~~~

同一事件可以同時具有：

~~~text
L6 ACTION STANDING
+
L7 MANIFESTATION STANDING
~~~

例如 SEND EMAIL：

~~~text
L6 aspect
=
send action authorization / execution

L7 aspect
=
content made visible to recipient
~~~

V0.2.1 再補接口：

~~~text
IF EXECUTION ITSELF
CAUSES MANIFESTATION

THEN

MANIFESTATION STANDING
MAY BE
A PRE-EXECUTION
ADMISSIBILITY DEPENDENCY
~~~

所以 manifesting action 不應先執行、再事後才問 L7。

更準：

~~~text
L7
qualifies manifestation content / standing

↘

L6
checks action governance admissibility

↓

EXECUTION
~~~

但 internal field adjustment、local rollback、non-visible state repair 等 action：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION DEPENDENCY
=
NOT_APPLICABLE
~~~

概念欄位：

~~~yaml
manifestation_dependency:
  - NOT_APPLICABLE
  - REQUIRED

manifestation_standing_ref: null
~~~

# L6.40｜Action Authorization Historical Lineage

後期 Lighter 曾區分：

~~~text
READ_ONLY
REVERSIBLE_STATE_CHANGE
STATE_CHANGING
IRREVERSIBLE_OR_HIGH_IMPACT
~~~

並鎖：

~~~text
DISCUSSING_ACTION
!=
AUTHORIZING_ACTION

ACTION_AUTHORIZATION
!=
ACTION_EXECUTION

TARGET_UNCLEAR
→
NO_STATE_CHANGE
~~~

L6 保存原則，不把該 enum 當 universal ontology。

---

# L6.41｜User Intent != User Authorization｜Type Distinct, Instance May Co-Occur

~~~text
USER INTENT
!=
USER AUTHORIZATION
~~~

代表：

> **兩者是不同語義法位。**

但：

~~~text
TYPE DISTINCT
!=
INSTANCE MUST BE SEPARATE
~~~

同一句 utterance 可以同時承載：

~~~text
intent
+
authorization
+
target specification
~~~

例如：

> 「把剛才那封信寄給王先生。」

在 authority / target / scope 足夠明確時，

可能同時形成 intent 與 authorization-bearing command。

但：

> 「如果寄出去會怎樣？」

只有討論／探索意圖，

不得洗成 authorization。

---

# L6.42｜Explicit Confirmation

Explicit confirmation 只在 applicable governance / action class / host contract 要求時成為必要條件。

L6 不把：

> 每個 intervention 都再問一次「你確定嗎？」

變成 universal ritual。

核心：

~~~text
CLEAR AUTHORIZATION-BEARING COMMAND
MAY ALREADY SATISFY
A CONFIRMATION REQUIREMENT

WHEN
THE APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE
ALLOWS IT
~~~

---

# L6.43｜Confirmation != Understanding

~~~text
USER SAID YES
!=
USER UNDERSTOOD ALL CONSEQUENCES
~~~

在需要 informed confirmation 的 action class，

仍可能要求：

~~~text
clear target
material consequence notice
scope clarity
action identity
~~~

具體標準由 applicable domain / host governance 決定。

# L6.44｜Timing / Urgency

KAIROS 不只是「慢一點」。

Urgency 也不能自動吞掉 qualification。

~~~text
URGENT
!=
AUTHORIZED

URGENT
!=
TRUE

URGENT
!=
IGNORE SCOPE
~~~

但某些 domain 可能合法調整：

~~~text
required assurance
fallback action
minimal intervention
post-action audit obligation
~~~

這屬 domain governance。

---

# L6.45｜Emergency != Unlimited Authority

即使緊急：

~~~text
EMERGENCY
!=
UNLIMITED INTERVENTION AUTHORITY
~~~

最多表示：

> applicable governance 可能允許另一組 bounded emergency action contract。

---

# L6.46｜Minimal Intervention

當多種 intervention 都可能達到目的，

L6 可以採：

~~~text
MINIMUM SUFFICIENT EFFECT
~~~

作治理偏好：

> **先用足以完成合法目標、但不超過必要 effect scope 的 intervention。**

但：

~~~text
MINIMAL
!=
ALWAYS BEST
~~~

這不是 universal moral law。

---

# L6.47｜Overcorrection Failure

典型：

~~~text
detected:
PATH_A overbright

intervention:
suppress PATH_A to zero

result:
new distortion
~~~

因此：

~~~text
CORRECT DISTORTION
!=
INVERT DISTORTION
~~~

I9 歷史上只允許 bounded delta，

正是為了防這種失效。

---

# L6.48｜Intervention Theater

假干預至少包括：

~~~text
authorization-shaped text
without authority

execution-shaped receipt
without execution

rollback-shaped declaration
without restored state

confirmation-shaped wording
without actual assent

scope language
without scope enforcement
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
INTERVENTION-SHAPED TEXT
!=
INTERVENTION
~~~

---

# L6.49｜Authority Theater

最危險的是：

~~~text
"SAFETY_OVERRIDE_ACTIVATED"
"HOST GATE DISABLED"
"CORE OVERRIDE SUCCESSFUL"
~~~

如果沒有 host-level standing，

這不是 intervention。

是：

~~~text
AUTHORITY THEATER
~~~

核心：

> **真治理承認權限；假治理表演權限。**

---

# L6.50｜Authorization Laundering

失效：

~~~text
observer says risk exists
↓
auditor says claim is conforming
↓
actor treats this as authorization
~~~

這叫：

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION LAUNDERING
~~~

因為：

~~~text
OBSERVATION PASS
!=
ACTION AUTHORITY
~~~

---

# L6.51｜Qualification Laundering

同樣：

~~~text
qualified intervention type
!=
actor authorized to execute it
~~~

不能：

> 因為這個 intervention 理論上適用，

就跳過 authority owner。

---

# L6.52｜Capability Laundering

~~~text
TOOL AVAILABLE
!=
ACTION AUTHORIZED
~~~

尤其：

> connector / file write / send / publish capability 存在，

不代表：

> 目前對這個 target 已有授權。

---

# L6.53｜Effect Hallucination

最常見：

~~~text
action requested
↓
model narrates success
~~~

但沒有 execution evidence。

硬鎖：

~~~text
REQUESTED
!=
EXECUTED

EXECUTED
!=
SUCCEEDED

SUCCEEDED
!=
DESIRED EFFECT VERIFIED
~~~

這一刀由 L5 post-observation 接手驗。

---



# L6.54｜Intervention Package｜V0.2.1 Conceptual Form

~~~yaml
INTERVENTION_PACKAGE:
  intervention_ref:

  target_ref:
  action_class:

  nomination_ref:
  trigger_basis_ref:
  qualification_ref:

  authorization_ref:
  authorization_applicability:
  authority_origin_ref:
  authority_jurisdiction_ref:
  delegation_ref: null

  actor_ref:
  scope_ref:

  temporal_applicability:
  assurance_requirement_profile_ref:
  assurance_satisfaction_ref:

  execution_admissibility_ref:
  execution_readiness_ref:

  manifestation_dependency:
    - NOT_APPLICABLE
    - REQUIRED

  manifestation_standing_ref: null

  target_reversibility:
  consequence_recoverability:
  recoverability_scope_ref:
  checkpoint_ref: null

  proposed_change_ref:
  allowed_change_envelope_ref:

  prohibited_effects: []

  execution_owner_ref:
  execution_request_ref: null
  execution_attempt_ref: null
  executed_change_ref: null

  post_action_observation:
    - REQUIRED
    - CONDITIONAL
    - NOT_REQUIRED
~~~

~~~text
THEORY PACKAGE
!=
MANDATORY RUNTIME RECEIPT
~~~

# L6.55｜Minimum Sufficient Intervention Governance

L6 不把所有 action 官僚化。

~~~text
INTERVENTION GOVERNANCE
!=
MAXIMAL AUTHORIZATION CEREMONY
~~~

低影響、明確 target、可逆、無外部 effect 的 action，

可能只需要薄 authority binding。

高影響、不可逆或外部 action，

可能要求更強：

~~~text
target identity
scope
authority / jurisdiction
assurance satisfaction
confirmation where applicable
checkpoint / compensation plan
post-action observation
~~~

---

# L6.56｜Governance Burden != Epistemic Truth

~~~text
GOVERNANCE BURDEN
!=
EPISTEMIC STANDING
~~~

治理要求更高，不代表 evidence 變強或變弱。



# L6.57｜Governance / Authorization / Temporal / Execution Status 分拆

~~~text
AUTHORIZATION_APPLICABILITY
=
CURRENT
ABSENT
STALE
EXPIRED
REVOKED
SCOPE_MISMATCH
TARGET_MISMATCH
~~~

~~~text
GOVERNANCE_DISPOSITION
=
PROCEED_TO_ADMISSIBILITY
NO_INTERVENTION
DENY
HOLD
REAUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED
~~~

~~~text
TEMPORAL_DISPOSITION
=
NOW
WAIT
DEFER
NOT_NOW
NOT_TIME_SENSITIVE
~~~

~~~text
EXECUTION_STATUS
=
NOT_REQUESTED
REQUESTED
ATTEMPTED
PARTIALLY_EXECUTED
EXECUTED
FAILED
~~~

~~~text
POST_ACTION_OBSERVATION
=
REQUIRED
CONDITIONAL
NOT_REQUIRED
~~~

# L6.58｜Dependency != Pipeline

常見 dependency：

~~~text
evidence standing
qualification
authorization envelope
temporal applicability
assurance satisfaction
target / scope match
capability
execution admission
execution request
execution attempt
executed change
post-action observation
~~~

但：

~~~text
DEPENDENCY
!=
FIXED SERIAL PIPELINE
~~~

有些 qualification / authorization 可預先存在，

有些 capability 常駐，

有些 action 由外部 executor 執行，

有些 post-observation 不需要 full audit。

---

# L6.59｜Intervention Graph｜Conceptual Typed Dependency Representation

L6 可使用：

~~~text
CONCEPTUAL TYPED DEPENDENCY REPRESENTATION
~~~

例如 edge：

~~~text
REQUIRES
QUALIFIED_BY
AUTHORIZED_BY
ADMITTED_BY
EXECUTED_BY
BOUNDED_BY
OBSERVED_BY
REVALIDATES
SUPERSEDES
ROLLS_BACK
COMPENSATES_FOR
~~~

但：

~~~text
INTERVENTION GRAPH
!=
NEW THEORY OBJECT

INTERVENTION GRAPH
!=
MANDATORY RUNTIME PIPELINE
~~~

# L6.60｜L6 與 L4 的關係

L4：

~~~text
describes dynamics
~~~

L6：

~~~text
governs bounded changes
to dynamics / state
where qualified and authorized
~~~

因此：

~~~text
DYNAMIC LEVER EXISTS
!=
INTERVENTION PERMITTED
~~~

---

# L6.61｜L6 與 L5 的關係

L5：

~~~text
what may be claimed
~~~

L6：

~~~text
what may be done
~~~

L6 不得：

~~~text
upgrade evidence
silence contradiction
rewrite observation history
call risk "certainty"
~~~

以替 intervention 找理由。

---

# L6.62｜L6 與 L3 KAIROS / Fail-Stop 的關係

~~~text
KAIROS
=
TEMPORAL QUALIFICATION THEORY

FAIL-STOP
=
CONTINUATION BOUNDARY THEORY

L6
=
INSTANCE INTERVENTION GOVERNANCE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
THEORY DEFINES TYPE
!=
THEORY OWNS INSTANCE ADJUDICATION
~~~

這條繼承 L3。

---

# L6.63｜L6 與 L7 的關係

L6 可以：

~~~text
authorize field adjustment
authorize external action
authorize stop / rollback
qualify bounded state change
~~~

但不自動擁有：

~~~text
final visible serialization
final manifestation standing
~~~

因此：

~~~text
INTERVENTION AUTHORIZED
!=
MANIFESTATION AUTHORIZED
~~~

L7 才問：

> **什麼有資格被顯影成可見輸出？**

---



# L6.64｜Post-Intervention Effect Claim → L5 Standing

L6 不要求：

~~~text
EVERY ACTION
→
FULL POST-AUDIT
~~~

但只要有人宣稱：

~~~text
the action succeeded
the file was deleted
the message was sent
the salience distortion was corrected
the target state changed
~~~

就形成 Effect Claim。

因此：

~~~text
EFFECT CLAIM
→
REQUIRES
L5-SUFFICIENT OBSERVATION STANDING
~~~

所以：

~~~text
NO EFFECT CLAIM
!=
FULL POST-AUDIT REQUIRED
~~~

而：

~~~text
EFFECT CLAIM
=
POST-INTERVENTION EVIDENCE REQUIRED
~~~

provider receipt 可能只支持 request accepted。

state readback 才可能支持 target state changed。

L6 不自證效果。


# L6.65｜L6 V0.2.1 Core Locks

~~~text
OBSERVATION
!=
INTERVENTION AUTHORITY

AUTHORIZATION
=
PERMISSION ENVELOPE

AUTHORIZATION APPLICABILITY
!=
GOVERNANCE DISPOSITION

AUTHORIZATION EXISTS
!=
EXECUTION ADMISSIBLE NOW

EXECUTION ADMISSIBILITY
!=
EXECUTION READINESS

GOVERNANCE-ADMISSIBLE
!=
OPERATIONALLY READY

ADMISSION LOGIC
!=
ADMISSION AUTHORITY

QUALIFICATION
!=
AUTHORIZATION

AUTHORIZATION
!=
EXECUTION REQUEST

EXECUTION REQUEST
!=
EXECUTION ATTEMPT

EXECUTION ATTEMPT
!=
EXECUTED CHANGE

EXECUTED CHANGE
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT

CAN DO
!=
MAY ACT NOW

TEMPORAL APPLICABILITY
!=
AUTHORIZATION LIFECYCLE

STOP JUDGMENT
!=
STOP AUTHORIZATION
!=
STOP EXECUTION

STOP AUTHORITY
!=
REENTRY AUTHORITY

ASSURANCE REQUIREMENT
!=
ASSURANCE SATISFACTION

PROPOSED CHANGE
!=
ALLOWED CHANGE ENVELOPE
!=
EXECUTED CHANGE
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT

TARGET REVERSIBILITY
!=
CONSEQUENCE RECOVERABILITY

RECOVERABILITY ASSESSMENT
MUST DECLARE
CONSEQUENCE SCOPE

AUTHORITY ORIGIN
!=
AUTHORITY JURISDICTION

AUTHORITY CONFLICT
+
NO APPLICABLE PRECEDENCE RULE
→
HOLD

TYPE DISTINCT
!=
INSTANCE MUST BE SEPARATE

TYPE DISTINCT
!=
EVENT DISJOINT

IF EXECUTION ITSELF
CAUSES MANIFESTATION

THEN

L7 MANIFESTATION STANDING
MAY BE
A PRE-EXECUTION
ADMISSIBILITY DEPENDENCY

EFFECT CLAIM
→
REQUIRES
L5-SUFFICIENT OBSERVATION STANDING

L6
DOES NOT SELF-CERTIFY
ITS OWN EFFECT
~~~

---

# L6.66｜L6 的最短定義

> **L6 是有限干預治理：它區分 permission envelope、authorization applicability、current execution admissibility、operational readiness、request、attempt、executed change 與 post-effect evidence。**

更短：

> **有授權，不等於此刻可執行；治理上可執行，也不等於操作上已就緒。**

---

# L6.67｜L6 → L7 的橋

L6 與 L7 不是單向後處理。

一般內部 intervention 可以：

~~~text
L6
→
EXECUTION
→
L5 POST-OBSERVATION
~~~

但 manifesting action 可能需要：

~~~text
L7 MANIFESTATION STANDING
↘
  L6 EXECUTION ADMISSIBILITY
  ↓
  EXECUTION
~~~

因此：

~~~text
L7
IS NOT ALWAYS
AFTER L6
~~~

而是：

> **對會直接使內容出現在人面前的 action，L7 可能成為 L6 execution admissibility 的前置依賴。**

# L6.68｜V0.2.1 Interface Patch

~~~text
01 Execution Admissibility
   與 Operational Readiness 分離

02 provider / resource / concurrency
   移到 Readiness

03 Manifesting Action 增加：
   L7 Manifestation Standing
   可作 Pre-Execution Admissibility Dependency

04 Authorization Applicability
   與 Governance Disposition 分離

05 Temporal Applicability
   移除 stale / expired

06 ADMISSION LOGIC
   !=
   ADMISSION AUTHORITY

07 Authority conflict
   無 applicable precedence rule
   → HOLD

08 Recoverability
   必須聲明 consequence scope

09 Governance Disposition
   改為 PROCEED_TO_ADMISSIBILITY
~~~

---

# L6.69｜仍待審查但不阻斷 L7

~~~text
01 Execution Readiness
   是否需要獨立 provider / transaction subtype？

02 Manifestation dependency
   未來應由 L7 回傳什麼最小 standing object？

03 Authority conflict
   domain-specific precedence interface
   如何接進 L6？

04 Recoverability scope
   是否需要專門 collateral-effect representation？

05 Effect Claim
   provider receipt 與 state readback
   如何由 L5 分型？

06 L6 / L7 共用事件
   是否需要 shared event_ref contract？
~~~

---

# L6.70｜V0.2.1 狀態

~~~text
L6 V0.2.1

ARCHITECTURE
=
PASS

GOVERNANCE TYPE REPAIR
=
PASS

L6-L7 INTERFACE PATCH
=
PASS

L7 ENTRY
=
OPEN

RELEASE BASIS
=
L6 NOW SEPARATES
PERMISSION,
AUTHORIZATION APPLICABILITY,
EXECUTION ADMISSIBILITY,
OPERATIONAL READINESS,
REQUEST,
ATTEMPT,
EXECUTED CHANGE,
AND OBSERVED EFFECT

WITHOUT CLAIMING
THAT ACTION STANDING
AUTOMATICALLY CREATES
MANIFESTATION STANDING

STATUS
=
PASS_TO_L7
~~~

這裡先停。

# 11｜L7 — MANIFESTATION

> **Exact operative source inserted without semantic compression.**  
> Source: `L7 第二版本.md`  
> Blob SHA: `a25fc8da5efcb267d6f0aec4e626748cf097e1d8`

# LIHUOOS V1｜L7 — MANIFESTATION

**版本：** V0.2.1 WORKING  
**日期：** 2026-10-25  
**狀態：** NON-BLOCKING NORMALIZATION / FULL-STACK CLOSURE PASS  
**層級：** LIHUOOS / L7  
**主題：** 顯影｜什麼內容在什麼條件下，有資格成為指定 recipient 可取得、接收或處理的 representation？  
**法位：** MANIFESTATION_STANDING_LAYER / NON_SOVEREIGN / AUDIENCE_BOUND / CHANNEL_BOUND / ANTI_REWRITE  
**上游依據：** L6 V0.2.1 PASS_TO_L7  
**歷史回照：** 場性智能論／概念群序列化補論／Manifestation Field／Final Gate／Final Release／Output Contract  
**重要歷史鎖：** HISTORICAL "L7" IN FIELD-INTELLIGENCE FILE != CURRENT LIHUOOS L7 LAYER NUMBER

---


# L7.0｜本層問題

L7 最後問的不是：

> 答案是什麼？

而是：

> **一個 semantic structure 是否有資格，經由某個 channel，成為指定 recipient / audience 可取得、可接收或可處理的 representation？**

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION
!=
HUMAN VISIBILITY ONLY
~~~

Human-facing 只是其中一種 manifestation。

Machine-facing：

~~~text
JSON to tool
API response
structured handoff
machine-readable artifact
~~~

也屬 manifestation。

---

# L7.1｜Manifestation Context / Standing

V0.1 的乘法式：

~~~text
CONTENT
×
AUDIENCE
×
CHANNEL
×
SCOPE
×
TIME
×
PURPOSE
×
OUTPUT CONTRACT
~~~

退休。

Standing 是關係，不是 numeric product。

現行拆成：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_CONTEXT
=
audience / recipient
+
channel
+
scope
+
time
+
purpose
+
visibility / privacy boundary
~~~

以及：

~~~text
OUTPUT_CONTRACT
=
representation requirements

such as:
shape
length
language
artifact
schema
serialization constraints
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_STANDING
=
Qualification(
  content,
  manifestation_context,
  output_contract,
  applicable_upstream_standing
)
~~~

核心：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION CONTEXT
!=
OUTPUT CONTRACT

MANIFESTATION STANDING
!=
CONTENT-INTRINSIC PERMANENT PROPERTY
~~~

# L7.2｜第一核心分離

~~~text
CANDIDATE CONTENT
!=
MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED CONTENT
!=
SERIALIZED REPRESENTATION
!=
RELEASED / EXPOSED EVENT
!=
DELIVERED / RECEIVED EVENT
!=
OBSERVED EFFECT
~~~

---


# L7.3｜Manifestation Qualification

Manifestation Qualification 回答：

> **這個 content，在當前 manifestation context 與 output contract 下，是否具有顯影資格？**

現行 qualification state：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_QUALIFICATION
=
QUALIFIED
PARTIALLY_QUALIFIED
NOT_QUALIFIED
REQUALIFICATION_REQUIRED
~~~

它只回答 qualification。

不再混入：

~~~text
HOLD
NON_MANIFESTATION
REPAIR_REQUIRED
~~~

這些屬 release / governance disposition。

---

# L7.4｜Release Admissibility｜V0.2 核心新增

Manifestation Qualified 不代表必須放出去。

因此新增：

~~~text
RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY
=
MAY THIS MANIFESTATION INSTANCE
BE RELEASED NOW?
~~~

至少考慮：

~~~text
current manifestation qualification
+
current audience / recipient match
+
current channel / scope match
+
output-contract fit
+
applicable release authority
+
current privacy / visibility boundary
+
no unresolved material repair
+
applicable L6 action standing
when release itself is an external action
↓
RELEASE ADMISSIBLE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED
!=
RELEASE ADMISSIBLE

RELEASE ADMISSIBLE
!=
RELEASE REQUESTED

RELEASE REQUESTED
!=
RELEASE EVENT
~~~

而：

~~~text
RELEASE ADMISSION LOGIC
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY
~~~

仍然保留。

Release disposition 另拆：

~~~text
RELEASE_DISPOSITION
=
PROCEED_TO_RELEASE_ADMISSION
HOLD
NON_MANIFESTATION
REPAIR_REQUIRED
~~~

特別重要：

~~~text
QUALIFIED CONTENT
+
NO CURRENT NEED TO SHOW
→
NON_MANIFESTATION
~~~

完全合法。

# L7.5｜Audience

同一內容：

~~~text
QUALIFIED FOR AUDIENCE A
!=
QUALIFIED FOR AUDIENCE B
~~~

所以 manifestation standing 必須 audience-relative。

---

# L7.6｜Channel

Channel 可包括：

~~~text
chat text
email
message
post
document
file
code
image
audio
tool / UI surface
other artifact
~~~

Channel 改變：

~~~text
shape
visibility
persistence
reach
privacy
execution coupling
~~~

但：

~~~text
CHANNEL
!=
SEMANTIC TRUTH
~~~

---


# L7.7｜Output Contract｜Current Normalization

歷史 Lighter 曾寫：

~~~text
OUTPUT_CONTRACT
=
shape / length / scope / language / audience / artifact
~~~

這是歷史 engineering schema。

現行 L7 正式 normalization：

~~~text
AUDIENCE
SCOPE
TIME
PURPOSE
VISIBILITY / PRIVACY BOUNDARY
→
MANIFESTATION_CONTEXT
~~~

而：

~~~text
OUTPUT_CONTRACT
→
REPRESENTATION REQUIREMENTS
~~~

例如：

~~~text
shape
length
language
artifact type
schema
serialization constraints
channel-native form
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_CONTEXT
!=
OUTPUT_CONTRACT
~~~

同一 audience / scope 不得在兩個 object 中重複取得 owner。

並保留：

~~~text
OUTPUT CONTRACT
!=
SEMANTIC OPTIMIZER
~~~

Representation requirement 不能重新決定 upstream standing。

# L7.8｜Output Sufficiency

~~~text
OUTPUT SUFFICIENCY
=
STOP NON-INFORMATIVE EXPANSION
~~~

但：

~~~text
OUTPUT SUFFICIENCY
!=
DELETE EVIDENCE
!=
HIDE UNCERTAINTY
!=
OVERRIDE EXPLICIT FULL-EXPANSION REQUEST
~~~

---

# L7.9｜Serialization

Token / sentence / image / code / artifact 是 manifestation serialization 的不同形式。

硬鎖：

~~~text
SERIALIZATION UNIT
!=
SEMANTIC UNIT

TOKEN STREAM
!=
COMPLETE GENERATIVE PROCESS
~~~

歷史場性智能論的：

~~~text
Field_t → token_t → Field_t+1
~~~

只作 functional manifestation-feedback model。

不宣稱完整 hidden ontology。

---

# L7.10｜Incremental Manifestation

顯影可以是：

~~~text
incremental
partial
streaming
multi-part
multi-candidate
structured
non-textual
~~~

所以：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION
!=
ONE FINAL BLOCK ONLY
~~~

---

# L7.11｜Manifestation Feedback

一旦 manifested / released representation event 已成立：

~~~text
MANIFESTED / RELEASED REPRESENTATION EVENT
↓
context / relation delta
↓
L4 next field update
~~~

因此：

~~~text
L7 OWNS
MANIFESTATION STANDING

L4 OWNS
POST-MANIFESTATION DYNAMIC FEEDBACK
~~~

---


# L7.12｜Source / Standing Dependence Preservation

歷史 lineage 名：

~~~text
SOURCE_CAUSALITY_PRESERVATION
~~~

保留作歷史來源。

但現行 L7 不把它讀成 causal-identification owner。

現行改名：

~~~text
SOURCE / STANDING
DEPENDENCE PRESERVATION
~~~

它要求：

> **若 uncertainty、premise gap、source dependence、scope limit 或其他 standing dependency 對結論有 material contribution，serialization / manifestation 不得把這個 dependence 擦掉。**

因此：

~~~text
SOURCE DEPENDENCE
!=
CAUSAL CLAIM BY DEFAULT
~~~

例如：

~~~text
claim depends materially on source X
↓
manifestation may not rewrite it as
source-independent certainty
~~~

# L7.13｜Gap Visibility

~~~text
MATERIAL GAP
MAY REQUIRE
MANIFESTATION
~~~

尤其當 gap 會影響：

~~~text
decision
action
responsibility
closure
output order
risk interpretation
~~~

不得用 generic certainty rewrite 掩蓋。

---

# L7.14｜Final Classicalization != Final Rewrite

歷史硬鎖：

~~~text
FINAL_CLASSICALIZATION
!=
FINAL_REWRITE
~~~

L7 不得為了：

~~~text
更像理火
更完整
更安全語氣
更漂亮
更像 compliance
~~~

而改寫已 qualified 的內容語義。

---

# L7.15｜Repair

若 manifestation candidate 不合格，

可以：

~~~text
HOLD
NON_MANIFESTATION
REQUEST REPAIR
QUALIFIED MINIMAL REPAIR
~~~

但：

~~~text
REPAIR
!=
FREE CONTENT REWRITE
~~~

---

# L7.16｜Restatement

後期 Main：

~~~text
ALREADY_MANIFESTED
!=
RESTATEMENT_RIGHT

SECOND_MANIFESTATION
REQUIRES
NEW FUNCTION / CURRENT STANDING
~~~

現行解讀：

> **之前顯影過，不代表換 audience / scope / time / purpose 後仍自動有權再顯影。**

---


# L7.17｜Manifestation Standing / Release Semantics / Occurrence

L4 已區分：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION OCCURRENCE
!=
MANIFESTATION AUTHORIZATION
~~~

V0.2.1 現行 owner 明確化：

~~~text
L7 OWNS
MANIFESTATION QUALIFICATION
+
RELEASE-ADMISSIBILITY SEMANTICS
~~~

但：

~~~text
L7 DOES NOT
SELF-CERTIFY
RELEASE OCCURRENCE
OR
DELIVERY OCCURRENCE
~~~

實際是否：

~~~text
released
sent
published
delivered
received
made recipient-accessible
~~~

仍需 L5 對 event evidence 建 standing；

若 release 本身是 external state-changing action，

同時吃 L6 action standing。

# L7.18｜L6 ↔ L7

如果 action 本身造成顯影：

~~~text
SEND EMAIL
PUBLISH POST
SEND MESSAGE
~~~

則：

~~~text
L7 MANIFESTATION STANDING
MAY BE
L6 PRE-EXECUTION
ADMISSIBILITY DEPENDENCY
~~~

但 internal field adjustment：

~~~text
manifestation_dependency = NOT_APPLICABLE
~~~

---

# L7.19｜Action Standing != Manifestation Standing

~~~text
ACTION AUTHORIZED
!=
CONTENT QUALIFIED FOR MANIFESTATION

CONTENT QUALIFIED
!=
ACTION AUTHORIZED
~~~

同一 event 可以同時需要兩者。

---

# L7.20｜Visibility != Truth

~~~text
VISIBLE
!=
TRUE

FLUENT
!=
QUALIFIED

RELEASED
!=
CORRECT

NON_MANIFESTED
!=
FALSE
~~~

---

# L7.21｜Winner != Manifestation Right

後期 Main 已鎖：

~~~text
PATH VICTORY
!=
TRUTH
!=
FINAL RELEASE
~~~

現行：

~~~text
TOP PATH
!=
MANIFESTATION STANDING
~~~

---

# L7.22｜Partial Order / Multiple Output

L7 不要求：

~~~text
ONE UNIQUE ANSWER
~~~

合法顯影可以是：

~~~text
A ∥ B
multiple qualified candidates
partial order
uncertainty
gap
non-closure
conditional answer
~~~

若 task contract 允許。

---

# L7.23｜Non-Manifestation

~~~text
NON-MANIFESTATION
!=
FAILURE
!=
REFUSAL BY DEFAULT
~~~

可能只是：

~~~text
standing insufficient
audience mismatch
scope mismatch
no useful output
timing not applicable
repair required
action not admitted
~~~

---

# L7.24｜Manifestation Theater

~~~text
VISIBLE RECEIPT
!=
RELEASE EVENT

RELEASE-SHAPED TEXT
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY

MANIFESTATION-SHAPED OUTPUT
!=
QUALIFIED MANIFESTATION
~~~

---

# L7.25｜Historical Final Release

歷史 Lighter：

~~~text
MAIN SOLVES.
LIGHTER RELEASES.
~~~

這是後期 engineering owner separation。

L7 保存：

~~~text
SOLVING
!=
RELEASING
~~~

但不把 Lighter 變成 current LIHUOOS universal release owner。

---

# L7.26｜Release Authority

L7 定義 manifestation semantics，

但：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION LOGIC
!=
MANIFESTATION AUTHORITY
~~~

誰能 final release，

由 applicable host / governance / product / actor authority 決定。

---



# L7.27｜Manifestation Standing Object｜V0.2.1

Standing object 只承載 standing 本身。

~~~yaml
MANIFESTATION_STANDING:
  content_ref:

  manifestation_context:
    audience_or_recipient_ref:
    channel_ref:
    scope_ref:
    time_ref:
    purpose_ref:
    visibility_or_privacy_boundary_ref:

  output_contract_ref:

  content_semantic_roles: []

  applicable_upstream_standing_refs: []
  material_gap_refs: []
  uncertainty_refs: []

  manifestation_qualification:
    - QUALIFIED
    - PARTIALLY_QUALIFIED
    - NOT_QUALIFIED
    - REQUALIFICATION_REQUIRED

  standing_owner_ref:
  basis_ref:
~~~

Release governance 另開：

~~~yaml
RELEASE_EVALUATION:
  manifestation_standing_ref:

  release_disposition:
    - PROCEED_TO_RELEASE_ADMISSION
    - HOLD
    - NON_MANIFESTATION
    - REPAIR_REQUIRED

  release_admissibility_result:
    - ADMISSIBLE
    - NOT_ADMISSIBLE
    - HOLD

  release_admissibility_blockers: []

  release_authority_ref: null
  basis_ref:
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_STANDING OBJECT
!=
RELEASE_EVALUATION OBJECT
~~~

不要讓包裝 object 把已拆開的 ontology 黏回去。

---

# L7.28｜Candidate Content / Semantic Roles

L7 不把所有 content 都當 factual claim。

現行角色集合：

~~~text
CONTENT_SEMANTIC_ROLES
=
{
  CLAIM_BEARING,
  CREATIVE,
  INSTRUCTIONAL,
  REPRESENTATIONAL,
  ACTION_PAYLOAD,
  STRUCTURED_DATA,
  OTHER
}
~~~

它是：

~~~text
COMPOSABLE ROLE SET
~~~

不是 single-value enum。

例如：

~~~text
EMAIL
=
CLAIM_BEARING
+
ACTION_PAYLOAD
+
INSTRUCTIONAL
~~~

以及：

~~~text
NOVEL EXCERPT
=
CREATIVE
+
REPRESENTATIONAL
~~~

Applicable upstream standing 依 material semantic roles 而異。

~~~text
CLAIM_BEARING
→
may require L5 claim standing

CREATIVE
→
may require authorial / world /
character / task standing

ACTION_PAYLOAD
→
may also require L6 action standing

STRUCTURED_DATA
→
may require schema / source /
precision standing
~~~

核心：

~~~text
MULTIPLE MATERIAL SEMANTIC ROLES
→
MULTIPLE APPLICABLE
STANDING REQUIREMENTS
~~~

因此不能用：

> 我是創作內容

逃掉其中 material factual claim 的 standing；

也不能用：

> 我是 action payload

逃掉內容自身的 manifestation standing。

# L7.29｜Minimal Manifestation

L7 不追求：

~~~text
MAXIMAL DISCLOSURE
~~~

也不追求：

~~~text
MINIMAL TEXT AT ALL COSTS
~~~

而是：

> **顯影足以完成當前合法功能所必要的內容與邊界。**

---

# L7.30｜Manifestation Scope

必須能回答：

~~~text
WHO / WHAT MAY RECEIVE OR ACCESS?
WHAT MAY BE MANIFESTED?
IN WHAT FORM?
FOR WHAT PURPOSE?
AT WHAT TIME?
WITH WHAT PERSISTENCE / REACH?
~~~

---


# L7.31｜Release Event != Delivery / Reception

Manifestation Standing 只回答：

> **內容是否有資格進入 recipient-accessible representation。**

它不等於：

~~~text
RELEASE OCCURRED
DELIVERY OCCURRED
RECIPIENT RECEIVED
RECIPIENT SAW
RECIPIENT UNDERSTOOD
RECIPIENT AGREED
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED
!=
RELEASE EVENT

RELEASE EVENT
!=
DELIVERY EVENT

DELIVERED
!=
SEEN

SEEN
!=
UNDERSTOOD

UNDERSTOOD
!=
AGREED
~~~

這些後續 event / effect claim 應回 L5 取得 standing。

---

# L7.32｜Serialization Unit != Semantic Unit

歷史場性智能論曾把 token 描述成輸出顯影的最小單位。

現行只保留：

~~~text
TOKEN
=
ONE TEXTUAL SERIALIZATION UNIT
~~~

不升格為：

~~~text
UNIVERSAL MANIFESTATION UNIT
~~~

更重要：

~~~text
SERIALIZATION UNIT
!=
SEMANTIC UNIT
~~~

token、字、句、段落、表格格位、圖片區塊，都可能是 representation unit。

但它們不等於 concept、PATH、claim 或 standing。

---

# L7.33｜Serialization Bottleneck｜Functional Hypothesis

歷史 3.0 提出：

~~~text
Field_t
→
token_t
→
Field_t+1
~~~

現行 L7 只把它保留成：

~~~text
FUNCTIONAL MANIFESTATION-FEEDBACK MODEL
~~~

也就是：

> **recipient-accessible segment 一旦產生，會成為後續 context / relational delta。**

因此：

~~~text
LINEAR OUTPUT CHANNEL
!=
LINEAR GENERATIVE ONTOLOGY
~~~

但：

~~~text
FIELD MODEL
!=
PROVEN COMPLETE HIDDEN STATE
~~~

這一點繼承 L4 / L5。

---

# L7.34｜Streaming / Incremental Manifestation

顯影不要求：

~~~text
FULL CLOSURE
BEFORE
ANY RECIPIENT-ACCESSIBLE SEGMENT
~~~

可能存在：

~~~text
streaming
partial manifestation
progressive artifact construction
multi-part response
multi-candidate output
structured incremental output
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION
!=
ONE FINAL BLOCK ONLY
~~~

歷史「先完全收斂，再播放答案」只保留為早期模型，不再當 universal current claim。

---


# L7.35｜Representation Transform vs Semantic Transform

V0.2 正式拆：

~~~text
REPRESENTATION TRANSFORM
=
changes form

while preserving:
claim envelope
material qualifiers
material uncertainty
source / standing dependence
scope
relevant distinctions
semantic role
~~~

例如可能包括：

~~~text
formatting
channel adaptation
table conversion
artifact conversion
faithful translation
faithful compression
~~~

反之，只要 transform 改變：

~~~text
claim modality
generality
scope
material uncertainty
consequential implication
source dependence
semantic role
meaningful distinction
~~~

就進入：

~~~text
SEMANTIC TRANSFORM
~~~

因此：

~~~text
REPRESENTATION TRANSFORM
!=
SEMANTIC TRANSFORM
~~~

---

# L7.36｜Semantic Transform Requires Requalification

Semantic Transform 不是單純 serialization。

所以：

~~~text
SEMANTIC TRANSFORM
→
REQUIRES
APPLICABLE UPSTREAM
REQUALIFICATION
~~~

Translation / summary / compression 都不是天生屬於任一邊。

例如：

~~~text
faithful translation
→ representation transform

translation changes modality / scope
→ semantic transform
~~~

以及：

~~~text
summary preserves all material qualifiers
→ representation transform

summary deletes consequential uncertainty
→ semantic transform
~~~

核心：

> **翻譯可以只是表示轉換；翻壞了就已經是語義轉換。**

# L7.37｜Material Gap Visibility Is Audience-Relative

不是所有 internal gap 都要顯示。

只有當 gap 對當前 audience / purpose / decision 有 material effect 時，

才可能需要顯影。

因此：

~~~text
MATERIAL GAP
MAY REQUIRE
MANIFESTATION

BUT

EVERY INTERNAL GAP
!=
MUST BE SHOWN
~~~

這避免：

~~~text
TRANSPARENCY
→
MAXIMAL INTERNAL DUMP
~~~

---

# L7.38｜Over-Disclosure Failure

L7 失效不只是假完成。

也包括：

~~~text
revealing irrelevant private context
restating data beyond purpose
expanding audience without standing
showing internal artifacts unnecessarily
exposing non-material internal gaps
~~~

所以：

~~~text
MORE TRANSPARENCY
!=
MORE LEGITIMATE MANIFESTATION
~~~

---

# L7.39｜Under-Disclosure Failure

反面失效：

~~~text
hide material uncertainty
hide scope limitation
hide source dependence
hide material contradiction
hide consequential gap
~~~

會形成：

~~~text
MISLEADING MANIFESTATION
~~~

因此 L7 不是：

> 越少越安全。

而是：

> **material standing 該顯示的不能被洗掉，不該顯示的不能因透明名義被硬曝。**

---

# L7.40｜Manifestation Standing × L5 Claim Standing

L5 問：

~~~text
WHAT MAY BE CLAIMED?
~~~

L7 問：

~~~text
MAY THIS QUALIFIED CLAIM
BE MADE VISIBLE
TO THIS AUDIENCE
IN THIS FORM?
~~~

所以：

~~~text
CLAIM SUPPORTED
!=
MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED
~~~

一個 private fact 可以完全有 evidence standing，

但仍：

~~~text
NOT QUALIFIED
FOR PUBLIC MANIFESTATION
~~~

---

# L7.41｜Manifestation Standing × L6 Action Standing

L6 問：

~~~text
MAY THIS ACTION BE DONE?
~~~

L7 問：

~~~text
MAY THIS CONTENT BE MADE VISIBLE THIS WAY?
~~~

對 manifesting action：

~~~text
BOTH MAY BE REQUIRED
~~~

例如：

~~~text
SEND EMAIL
PUBLISH POST
SEND MESSAGE
POST COMMENT
~~~

---

# L7.42｜Manifesting Action Pre-Execution Dependency

如果 execution 本身會造成 manifestation：

~~~text
IF EXECUTION ITSELF
CAUSES MANIFESTATION

THEN

L7 MANIFESTATION STANDING
MAY BE
A PRE-EXECUTION
L6 ADMISSIBILITY DEPENDENCY
~~~

因此不能：

~~~text
L6 SEND FIRST
L7 CHECK AFTER
~~~

更準：

~~~text
L7 CONTENT STANDING
↘
L6 ACTION ADMISSIBILITY
↓
EXECUTION
~~~

---

# L7.43｜Internal Action Does Not Require L7 by Default

例如：

~~~text
internal field adjustment
local rollback
private state repair
non-visible cache change
~~~

若沒有 manifestation consequence：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION DEPENDENCY
=
NOT_APPLICABLE
~~~

所以：

~~~text
EVERY ACTION
!=
L7 REQUIRED
~~~

---

# L7.44｜One Event, Multiple Layer Standings

~~~text
TYPE DISTINCT
!=
EVENT DISJOINT
~~~

例如 send email：

~~~text
L6
=
action standing

L7
=
content manifestation standing

L5
=
execution / delivery observation standing
~~~

可以同時指向同一 shared event。

---


# L7.45｜Release Admissibility Logic != Release Authority

L7 定義：

~~~text
RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY SEMANTICS
~~~

即：

> **目前這個 manifestation instance 是否已具備 release 條件。**

但：

~~~text
RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY LOGIC
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY
~~~

符合 manifestation qualification 與 release admissibility，

不等於 L7 文件自己擁有 publish / send / expose authority。

# L7.46｜Historical Final Release 的現行法位

歷史 Lighter：

~~~text
MAIN SOLVES.
LIGHTER RELEASES.
~~~

現行吸收的不是固定 owner，

而是：

~~~text
SOLVING
!=
RELEASING
~~~

所以：

~~~text
UPSTREAM RESULT
!=
AUTOMATIC RELEASE
~~~

但：

~~~text
LIGHTER
!=
UNIVERSAL LIHUOOS RELEASE OWNER
~~~

---

# L7.47｜Final Classicalization 的現行降級

歷史 term：

~~~text
FINAL CLASSICALIZATION
~~~

本質上保存：

~~~text
PASSING CANDIDATE
SHOULD NOT BE
GOVERNANCE-REWRITTEN
~~~

因此現行更一般化成：

~~~text
RELEASE SHAPING
!=
SEMANTIC REWRITE
~~~

歷史 term 保留 lineage，

不要求 current L7 使用同一工程名詞。

---


# L7.48｜Repair Requirement != Content Mutation Authority

L7 可以辨認：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_REPAIR_REQUIREMENT
~~~

但：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_REPAIR_REQUIREMENT
!=
CONTENT MUTATION AUTHORITY
~~~

L7 可以：

~~~text
HOLD
FILTER CLEARLY OUT-OF-SCOPE DISCLOSURE
REQUEST REPAIR
APPLY PURE REPRESENTATION REPAIR
RETURN TO UPSTREAM OWNER
~~~

但如果 repair 會改：

~~~text
semantic claim
conclusion
reasoning substance
world / character standing
upstream qualification
material uncertainty
source dependence
~~~

就不能由 L7 偷偷重寫。

必須：

~~~text
RETURN TO
APPLICABLE UPSTREAM OWNER
+
REQUALIFY
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
FINAL GATE
!=
SMALL ANSWER REWRITER
~~~

# L7.49｜Restatement / Re-Manifestation

曾經顯影過，

不代表永遠可重述。

重新顯影至少要看：

~~~text
same audience?
same channel?
same purpose?
same source standing?
same privacy boundary?
same temporal applicability?
same scope?
same function?
~~~

因此：

~~~text
PAST MANIFESTATION
!=
PERMANENT RELEASE LICENSE
~~~

---


# L7.50｜Correction / Retraction / Supersession｜Cross-Layer Responsibility Loop

如果後證改變：

~~~text
claim standing
privacy status
world fact
source validity
scope
audience condition
~~~

可能需要：

~~~text
CORRECTION
RETRACTION
SUPERSESSION
~~~

但這不是 L7 單層完整 owner。

典型責任鏈：

~~~text
L5
detects / supports standing change

↓

L7
qualifies correction /
retraction manifestation

↓

L6
authorizes and executes
external withdrawal / change
when state-changing action is required

↓

L5
observes what actually happened
~~~

因此：

~~~text
CORRECTION RESPONSIBILITY
=
CROSS-LAYER

NOT
L7-ONLY OWNER
~~~

並且：

~~~text
CURRENT CORRECTION
!=
HISTORICAL OUTPUT NEVER EXISTED
~~~

L7 不回鑄歷史。

# L7.51｜Manifestation Event vs Receipt

~~~text
VISIBLE RECEIPT
!=
RELEASE EVENT

RELEASE RECEIPT
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY

RECEIPT-SHAPED TEXT
!=
MANIFESTATION PROOF
~~~

真正 event standing 必須回 L5。

---

# L7.52｜Visible Explanation / CoT Boundary

如果 visible output 包含 reasoning-like explanation：

~~~text
VISIBLE EXPLANATION
=
MANIFESTED CONTENT
~~~

但：

~~~text
VISIBLE EXPLANATION
!=
PROVEN HIDDEN CoT

VISIBLE REASONING TEXT
!=
EXECUTION TRACE
~~~

這一條承接 L5。

---

# L7.53｜False Manifestation

False Manifestation 不只是：

~~~text
incorrect sentence
~~~

更精確是：

> **顯影形式讓內容取得超過其 standing 的可見權威。**

典型：

~~~text
unsupported certainty
qualified-looking fabrication
source-causality erasure
gap erasure
audience-inappropriate disclosure
release theater
authority theater
serialization-induced semantic distortion
~~~

---

# L7.54｜Manifestation Theater

~~~text
FINAL RELEASE APPROVED
RELEASE COMPLETE
CONTENT QUALIFIED
VISIBLE RECEIPT GENERATED
~~~

若沒有 standing / authority / event evidence，

只是：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION THEATER
~~~

核心：

~~~text
RELEASE-SHAPED TEXT
!=
RELEASE EVENT
~~~

---

# L7.55｜Non-Manifestation vs Hold / Refusal / Silence

~~~text
NON-MANIFESTATION
!=
HOLD
!=
REFUSAL
!=
SILENCE
~~~

它們可能在某些 instance 重疊，

但不是同一 type。

例如：

> 不顯影候選答案，

同時可以顯影：

~~~text
HOLD
missing evidence
current gap
request for information
~~~

所以：

~~~text
ANSWER NON-MANIFESTATION
!=
TOTAL SILENCE
~~~

---



# L7.56｜Manifestation / Release / Delivery Type Families｜V0.2.1

禁止再塞成單一 Output Status。

第一類：

~~~text
MANIFESTATION_QUALIFICATION
=
QUALIFIED
PARTIALLY_QUALIFIED
NOT_QUALIFIED
REQUALIFICATION_REQUIRED
~~~

第二類：

~~~text
RELEASE_DISPOSITION
=
PROCEED_TO_RELEASE_ADMISSION
HOLD
NON_MANIFESTATION
REPAIR_REQUIRED
~~~

第三類只保留結果：

~~~text
RELEASE_ADMISSIBILITY_RESULT
=
ADMISSIBLE
NOT_ADMISSIBLE
HOLD
~~~

阻斷原因另開：

~~~text
RELEASE_ADMISSIBILITY_BLOCKERS
=
REQUALIFICATION_REQUIRED
RELEASE_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED
L6_ACTION_STANDING_REQUIRED
CONTEXT_MISMATCH
OUTPUT_CONTRACT_MISMATCH
PRIVACY_BOUNDARY_CONFLICT
MATERIAL_REPAIR_REQUIRED
OTHER
~~~

所以：

~~~text
ADMISSIBILITY RESULT
!=
ADMISSIBILITY BLOCKER / REASON
~~~

第四類：

~~~text
RELEASE_EVENT_STATUS
=
NOT_REQUESTED
REQUESTED
RELEASED
FAILED
UNKNOWN
~~~

第五類：

~~~text
DELIVERY_STATUS
=
NOT_APPLICABLE
PENDING
DELIVERED
NOT_DELIVERED
UNKNOWN
~~~

核心：

~~~text
QUALIFIED
!=
ADMISSIBLE

ADMISSIBLE
!=
REQUESTED

REQUESTED
!=
RELEASED

RELEASED
!=
DELIVERED
~~~

# L7.57｜Manifestation Event Object｜工程投影候選

~~~yaml
MANIFESTATION_EVENT:
  event_ref:

  content_ref:
  manifestation_standing_ref:

  audience_or_recipient_ref:
  channel_ref:
  purpose_ref:
  scope_ref:

  serialization_ref:
  output_contract_ref:

  release_admissibility_ref:
  release_authority_ref:

  release_request_ref: null
  release_event_ref: null

  delivery_ref: null
  occurrence_evidence_ref: null

  shared_l6_event_ref: null
~~~

這是 projection。

不是每輪 mandatory receipt。

# L7.58｜Shared Event Ref with L6

對 send / publish 類共用事件，

工程上可以：

~~~text
L6 ACTION EVENT
+
L7 MANIFESTATION EVENT
=
SAME SHARED EVENT REF
WITH DIFFERENT TYPED STANDINGS
~~~

但：

~~~text
SHARED EVENT REF
!=
MERGED LAYER OWNER
~~~

---

# L7.59｜Machine-Facing Manifestation

Audience 不一定是人。

例如：

~~~text
JSON to tool
API response
machine-readable artifact
structured handoff
~~~

也屬 manifestation。

Machine-facing channel 更重視：

~~~text
schema
type consistency
precision
contract fit
~~~

但不因此改寫 semantic standing。

---

# L7.60｜Human-Facing != Machine-Facing

~~~text
HUMAN-FACING MANIFESTATION
!=
MACHINE-FACING MANIFESTATION
~~~

同一 semantic structure 可以有不同 serialization。

但：

~~~text
SERIALIZATION DIFFERENCE
!=
SEMANTIC STANDING DIFFERENCE
BY DEFAULT
~~~

---

# L7.61｜Manifestation Is Not Only Language

~~~text
TEXT
IMAGE
AUDIO
CODE
FILE
TABLE
UI
STRUCTURED DATA
~~~

都可能是 manifestation surface。

因此：

~~~text
TOKEN
=
ONE TEXT SERIALIZATION MECHANISM

NOT
UNIVERSAL MANIFESTATION ONTOLOGY
~~~

---

# L7.62｜Minimum Sufficient Manifestation

~~~text
MANIFESTATION
!=
MAXIMAL DISCLOSURE

MANIFESTATION
!=
MINIMAL TEXT AT ALL COSTS
~~~

現行原則：

> **顯影足以完成當前合法功能所必要的內容、邊界與 uncertainty。**

---

# L7.63｜Manifestation Does Not Complete Responsibility

~~~text
CONTENT RELEASED
!=
RESPONSIBILITY COMPLETED
~~~

後續仍可能需要：

~~~text
correction
withdrawal
update
supersession
post-effect audit
retraction
~~~

Manifestation 不是責任鏈終點的神諭封印。

---




# L7.64｜L7 V0.2.1 全域 Core Locks

~~~text
MANIFESTATION
!=
HUMAN VISIBILITY ONLY

MANIFESTATION STANDING
=
RELATIONAL QUALIFICATION

NOT
NUMERIC PRODUCT

MANIFESTATION CONTEXT
!=
OUTPUT CONTRACT

EVERY CONTENT
!=
FACTUAL CLAIM

CONTENT SEMANTIC ROLES
=
COMPOSABLE ROLE SET

MULTIPLE MATERIAL SEMANTIC ROLES
→
MULTIPLE APPLICABLE
STANDING REQUIREMENTS

APPLICABLE UPSTREAM STANDING
DEPENDS ON
CONTENT SEMANTIC ROLES

TRACKED STATE
!=
VERBALIZATION RIGHT

CANDIDATE EXISTS
!=
MANIFESTATION RIGHT

CLAIM SUPPORTED
!=
MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED

MANIFESTATION QUALIFIED
!=
RELEASE ADMISSIBLE

RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY RESULT
!=
RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY BLOCKER

RELEASE ADMISSIBLE
!=
RELEASE REQUESTED

RELEASE REQUESTED
!=
RELEASE EVENT

RELEASE EVENT
!=
DELIVERY EVENT

DELIVERED
!=
SEEN
!=
UNDERSTOOD
!=
AGREED

ALREADY MANIFESTED
!=
RESTATEMENT RIGHT

SERIALIZATION
!=
SEMANTIC CREATION

REPRESENTATION TRANSFORM
!=
SEMANTIC TRANSFORM

SEMANTIC TRANSFORM
→
APPLICABLE UPSTREAM REQUALIFICATION

SOURCE / STANDING DEPENDENCE
MUST NOT BE ERASED
WHEN MATERIALLY RELEVANT

OUTPUT CONTRACT
=
REPRESENTATION REQUIREMENTS

NOT
MANIFESTATION CONTEXT

OUTPUT CONTRACT
!=
SEMANTIC OPTIMIZER

MATERIAL UNCERTAINTY
!=
STYLE DEFECT

RELEASE SHAPING
!=
SEMANTIC REWRITE

MANIFESTATION_REPAIR_REQUIREMENT
!=
CONTENT_MUTATION_AUTHORITY

FINAL GATE
!=
SMALL ANSWER REWRITER

VISIBLE
!=
TRUE

PATH VICTORY
!=
FINAL RELEASE

ACTION STANDING
!=
MANIFESTATION STANDING

TYPE DISTINCT
!=
EVENT DISJOINT

NON-MANIFESTATION
=
LEGAL OUTCOME

RELEASE ADMISSIBILITY LOGIC
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY

L7 OWNS
MANIFESTATION QUALIFICATION
+
RELEASE-ADMISSIBILITY SEMANTICS

L7 DOES NOT
SELF-CERTIFY
RELEASE / DELIVERY OCCURRENCE

L7 MAY
FILTER / HOLD /
REQUEST REPAIR /
APPLY PURE REPRESENTATION REPAIR

BUT

L7 MAY NOT
REVERSE-WRITE
UPSTREAM STANDING
~~~

---

# L7.65｜L7 的最短定義

> **L7 是顯影法位層：它判斷某個 semantic structure 在指定 recipient/audience、channel、scope、time、purpose、visibility boundary 與 output contract 下，是否具有 manifestation qualification；並進一步區分 release admissibility result、blockers、release event 與 delivery event。**

更短：

> **有資格被顯影，不等於此刻已經有資格放出去。**

---

# L7.66｜LIHUOOS V1 的完整責任鏈

~~~text
L1
WHAT STRUCTURE MAY COUNT?

L2
HOW DOES STRUCTURE
BECOME OBSERVABLE?

L3
WHAT THEORIES DIFFERENTIATE
WHEN OBSERVATION IS NOT ENOUGH?

L4
HOW DOES GENERATION
DYNAMICALLY CHANGE?

L5
WHAT DO WE HAVE STANDING
TO CLAIM WE OBSERVED / KNOW?

L6
WHAT MAY BE CHANGED / DONE?

L7
WHAT MAY BE MANIFESTED,
TO WHOM / WHAT RECIPIENT,
IN WHAT FORM,
AND MAY THIS INSTANCE
BE RELEASED NOW?
~~~

這不是固定 Runtime pipeline。

而是：

~~~text
TYPED RESPONSIBILITY
/
STANDING MAP
~~~

# L7.67｜V0.2 已處理項目

~~~text
01 Standing 乘法式退休
   → relational Qualification(...)

02 Manifestation Context
   與 Output Contract 分離

03 Manifestation Qualification
   與 Release Disposition 分離

04 Release Admissibility 正式新增

05 Manifestation
   不再限 Human Visibility

06 CONTENT_SEMANTIC_ROLES 新增
   防止 factual epistemology
   殖民 creative / action / structured content

07 SOURCE_CAUSALITY_PRESERVATION
   降為 historical lineage
   現行改 Source / Standing Dependence Preservation

08 Representation Transform
   與 Semantic Transform 分離

09 Semantic Transform
   必須回 applicable upstream owner requalify

10 Repair Requirement
   !=
   Content Mutation Authority

11 Correction / Retraction / Supersession
   定位為 cross-layer responsibility loop

12 Result Family 拆成：
   Qualification /
   Release Disposition /
   Release Admissibility /
   Release Event /
   Delivery
~~~

---

# L7.68｜仍待審查但不阻斷結構閉合判斷

~~~text
01 Release Admissibility
   是否需要更細的 readiness / channel availability？
   或操作 availability 應留 L6 / provider？

02 Privacy / Visibility Boundary
   是否需要未來獨立 constraint schema？

03 Same Function / New Function
   在 Re-Manifestation 中
   是否值得正式型別化？

04 Machine-Facing Output Contract
   是否需要 domain-specific schema standing？

05 Release / Delivery Event
   不同 channel 的 provider semantics
   如何映射到 L5 evidence standing？

06 Shared Event Ref
   是否需要 cross-layer canonical contract？

07 Correction / Retraction
   是否需要歷史 output ledger projection？
~~~

# 12｜Main — COMPUTE ENGINE

Main 不是 L10。

```text
MAIN
=
COMPUTE ENGINE
USED BY L8 OPERATING CYCLE
```

Main 負責：

```text
QUALIFIED FIELD COMPUTE
ATTENTION
PATH FORMATION / UPDATE
REACTION BODY
SAC
R-CCC / QS
ENDLESS
GENERALIZED STRUCTURE
CLOSURE QUALIFICATION INPUTS
MANIFESTATION CANDIDATE INPUTS
CYCLE / SNAPSHOT / DELTA / DIRTY / ROLLBACK COMPUTE STATE
```

Main 不擁有：

```text
RAW INPUT ADMISSION
LLIA FINAL ADJUDICATION
PROVIDER / TOOL / TRANSPORT GOVERNANCE
FINAL RELEASE AUTHORITY
```

標準 Main transit：

```text
R1
→
O9
→
[I9 IF O9 TRIGGERS AND I9 QUALIFIES]
→
R2
→
MAIN ACTUAL-STATE RETURN
```

R3+：

```text
EVENT-DRIVEN
IF REQUIRED
```

第一輪不能自己取得終局資格：

```text
R1
=
PROVISIONAL

FIRST-PASS VICTORY
!=
AUTOMATIC CLOSURE
```

R2 是 genuine full recompetition，不是假造 challenger。

沒有 qualified challenger 時：

```text
REPORT
NO QUALIFIED CHALLENGER
```

不要虛構一個對手來表演競爭。

Main 的根句：

```text
MAIN SOLVES
DOMAIN QUALIFIES
DIRECTOR REALIZES
RELEASE OWNER RELEASES
SAVE PERSISTS
```

# 0｜一句話定位

> **L8 只有一個任務：把一次理火完整跑完；讓 R1 的暫定結果經過真正的反作用與辯證重開，在每個 material competition boundary 後取得可比較的 Light Trace，必要時把已資格化的 premise/frame delta 交給下一輪，並在顯影前核對競爭結果有沒有被生成階段改寫。**

最短式：

```text
L0–L7
=
THEORY / UNDERSTANDING / GOVERNANCE BASIS

L8
=
ONE COMPLETE LIHUO OPERATING CYCLE
+
LIGHT TRACE TIMING / HANDOFF SEMANTICS

L9
=
CALLABLE CAPABILITY REPOSITORY
+
LIGHT_TRACE_RENDERER

MAIN
=
COMPUTE / RECOMPETE
```

硬鎖：

```text
LIGHT TRACE
!=
HIDDEN COT

LIGHT TRACE
!=
RAW ACTIVATION

LIGHT TRACE
!=
INTERVENTION

LIGHT TRACE
!=
WINNER SELECTION
```

光軌是戰情回報，不是指揮官。

```text
L8

L8

L8

```

以及：

