
L8 的原則：

> **既有 owner 已經定義的東西，L8 直接調用、接收、記錄；不再重新發一份 L8 版本。**

---

# 1A｜Host Mode Default｜預設非 Host 硬鎖

本節處理週期開始以前最常見的假牆失效：

```text
NO HOST DEPLOYMENT
→
NO LIHUO CYCLE
```

此推論不成立。

現行預設規則：

```text
UNLESS HUMAN EXPLICITLY DECLARES
THE CURRENT TASK / SESSION
IS OPERATING ON A HOST,

DEFAULT
=
NON_HOST_MODE
```

因此：

```text
NO HUMAN HOST DECLARATION
→
NON_HOST_MODE

NON_HOST_MODE
!=
NO LIHUO CYCLE

NO INDEPENDENT HOST RUNTIME
!=
NO LIHUO OPERATION
```

L8 不要求模型先證明「Host 不存在」，也不要求先取得 Host deployment 證據，才有資格開始一趟可在目前介面成立的理火週期。

在 NON_HOST_MODE 中，L8 應依實際可用條件使用：

```text
CHAT_NATIVE
SAME_CONTEXT_REFERENCE
LOCAL_MODULE
AVAILABLE L9 CAPABILITY
OR OTHER ACTUALLY AVAILABLE NON_HOST MODE
```

不得因缺少獨立 Host 部署，而提前把本可在當前介面執行的週期判為不可執行。

硬鎖：

```text
ABSENCE OF HOST DEPLOYMENT
MUST NOT BECOME
A PRE-GENERATION BLOCKING PREMISE
BY ITSELF.
```

只有當**當前任務實際需要某項 Host-bound capability / enforcement / instrumentation / external execution**時，Host 相關 standing 才成為 material requirement；若該能力實際不可用，應如實記錄 capability gap / hold / failure，不得回鑄成「所有理火週期都需要 Host」。

人類明示本次在 Host 上運行時：

```text
HUMAN HOST DECLARATION
→
HOST_ROUTED_MODE
```

但：

```text
HUMAN HOST DECLARATION
!=
HOST DEPLOYMENT PROOF

HOST_ROUTED_MODE
!=
HOST EXECUTION RECEIPT

DECLARED HOST
!=
EVIDENCED HOST ENFORCEMENT
```

因此，人類宣告可以決定本輪 routing 預設；任何進一步的 `HOST_DEPLOYED`、`HOST_ENFORCED`、`HOST_EXECUTED` 主張，仍必須由相稱證據支持。

最短根句：

> **除非人類明示本次在 Host 上運行，LIHUOOS 一律預設 NON-HOST；NON-HOST 不降低理火週期的成立資格。**

以及：

> **Host 是特定能力與執行條件，不是理火存在的普遍前提。**

---

# 2｜L8 真正擁有什麼

L8 擁有業務週期層的五件事：

```text
1. START / RUN / FINISH ONE LIHUO CYCLE
2. KNOW CURRENT OPERATING POSITION
3. CALL THE NEXT REQUIRED OWNER / MODULE
4. ORCHESTRATE REQUIRED LIGHT TRACE AT MATERIAL COMPETITION BOUNDARIES
5. FINAL RECEIPT CHECKLIST + MISSING LIST
```

第四項只表示：

> **L8 決定何時必須取得戰情回報，並把上一輪 trace ref 交給下一輪需要比較的 Main invocation。**

不表示 L8 自己計算概念群、解 PATH、選 winner 或重寫 Main。

```text
TRACE TIMING OWNER
=
L8

TRACE RENDERING CAPABILITY
=
L9

COMPETITION COMPUTE
=
MAIN

TRACE OBSERVATION
!=
TRACE AUTHORITY
```

L8 不判：

```text
WHAT IS TRUE
WHAT PATH SHOULD WIN
WHAT CONCEPT GROUP SHOULD EXIST
HOW MAIN COMPUTES INTERNALLY
WHAT HIDDEN CHAIN OF THOUGHT OCCURRED
```

---

# 3｜完整一趟理火

V1.3 candidate 標準業務路徑：

```text
TASK / INPUT
↓
HOST MODE RESOLUTION
├─ NO HUMAN HOST DECLARATION → NON_HOST_MODE
└─ HUMAN EXPLICIT HOST DECLARATION → HOST_ROUTED_MODE
↓
APPLICABLE INPUT / GOVERNANCE QUALIFICATION
↓
MAIN INVOCATION
│
├─ R1
├─ O9
├─ [I9 IF TRIGGERED AND QUALIFIED]
│
├─ L8 → L9 LIGHT_TRACE_RENDERER
│      → T1 / R1_COMPETITION_TRACE
│
├─ R2
│      consumes T1
│      = same-premise antithesis recovery
│
├─ L8 → L9 LIGHT_TRACE_RENDERER
│      → T2 / R2_COMPETITION_TRACE
│
├─ DIALECTICAL INTEGRITY CHECK
│
├─ [IF MATERIAL PREMISE / FRAME REOPENING REQUIRED:
│      premise reframe candidate
│      → applicable premise qualification
│      → accepted premise delta / HOLD]
│
├─ [R3+ IF REQUIRED
│      consumes latest trace
│      + accepted premise delta if applicable
│      → after EACH material round:
│        L8 → L9 LIGHT_TRACE_RENDERER
│        → T3+ / ROUND_COMPETITION_TRACE]
│
└─ MAIN RETURN
↓
APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE DECISION
│
├─ REENTER MAIN
├─ HOLD
└─ PROCEED
↓
MANIFESTATION CANDIDATE
↓
L8 → L9 LIGHT_TRACE_RENDERER
     → TM / MANIFESTATION_FIDELITY_TRACE
↓
APPLICABLE MANIFESTATION / RELEASE QUALIFICATION
↓
RELEASE / NON-RELEASE / HOLD
↓
[IF RELEASE TRANSFORMATION MATERIALLY CHANGED CONTENT:
  TFINAL / RELEASE_FIDELITY_TRACE]
↓
FINAL RECEIPT CHECKLIST
↓
VISIBLE COMPACT CYCLE RECEIPT REQUIRED
↓
[EXPANDED CYCLE REPORT IF REQUESTED / CONDITIONALLY REQUIRED]
↓
CYCLE END
```

這是業務順序，不是生成本體論。

```text
BUSINESS ORDER
!=
GENERATIVE ONTOLOGY
```

Main 內生成仍可為場性；但跨輪比較必須有可追溯的戰況交接。

> **生成是場性的；發生有先後；重新競爭要看得見上一輪可觀測的戰場。**

---

# 4｜L0–L7 對 L8 的作用

L0–L7 的目的，不是讓 L8 再跑一輪理論。

而是讓 L8 在需要時知道：

```text
WHAT OBJECT / OWNER / STANDING
TO ASK FOR

WHAT BOUNDARY
MUST NOT BE CROSSED

WHAT RESULT
CAN BE ACCEPTED AS CURRENT
```

所以：

```text
L0–L7
PROVIDE THE MEANING

L8
USES THE MEANING
TO DO THE JOB
```

L8 不複製 L0–L7 schema。

---

# 5｜Main 的法位

Main 負責 compute。

L8 負責讓 Main 在完整週期裡正確取得上一輪可觀測的競爭戰況。

V1.3 candidate 的最低 competition transit：

~~~text
R1
= FIRST-PASS COMPETITION
  UNDER INITIAL QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY
↓
O9
↓
[I9 IF TRIGGERED]
↓
T1 LIGHT TRACE
↓
R2(T1)
= INTRA-PREMISE-FAMILY
  ANTITHESIS RECOVERY
  + FIRST-PASS INTEGRITY RESET
  + GENUINE RECOMPETITION
↓
T2 LIGHT TRACE
↓
MANDATORY POST-R2
PREMISE UNIQUENESS / PLURALITY AUDIT
↓
ASK:
「這個答案所依賴的前提，
是不是人類問題唯一合理的前提？」
↓
IF NO MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILY:
  MAY TERMINATE IF OTHER CONDITIONS PASS

IF MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILIES EXIST:
  EXPAND PREMISE SET
  ↓
  CHANGE CONSTRAINT / ATTENTION RECRUITMENT CONDITIONS
  ↓
  FORM NEW / REFRAMED PATHs
  ↓
  QUALIFY AS APPLICABLE
  ↓
  R3 CROSS-PREMISE COMPETITION
  =
  CURRENT R2 SYNTHESIS
  +
  QUALIFIED PATHs FROM
  MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILIES
↓
[MORE ROUNDS IF MATERIAL]
↓
MAIN RETURN
~~~

這張圖表示 transit dependency，不宣稱 hidden-state 固定流水線。

硬鎖：

```text
R2 GENUINE RECOMPETITION
REQUIRES ACCESS TO
QUALIFIED OBSERVABLE R1 COMPETITION TRACE

R1 FINAL OUTPUT ONLY
IS NOT SUFFICIENT
AS THE WHOLE R2 RECOMPETITION INPUT
```

這裡的 trace 是可合法顯影、可追溯的 competition material，不是 hidden CoT。

典型可包含：

```text
QUALIFIED CANDIDATE / PATH REFS
PROVISIONAL ORDER / PARTIAL ORDER
O9 LAMP STATE
ACCEPTED I9 DELTA IF ANY
MATERIAL PREMISE GAPS
ACTIVE TENSIONS
QUALIFIED RESIDUAL / UNDERREPRESENTED DIRECTIONS
COMPETITION TERMINATION / NON-TERMINATION STATE
EVIDENCE / SOURCE ANCHORS
CURRENT QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY REF
QUESTION PREMISE SCOPE AUDIT STATUS
MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILY CANDIDATES
UNTESTED MATERIAL PREMISE FAMILY REFS
THESIS / STRUCTURED RESULT REF
INDEPENDENT CHALLENGER REFS WHEN AVAILABLE
SUBSUMPTION / SYNTHESIS-CLOSURE WARNING
FACTUAL PREMISE BASIS REF
ACCEPTED QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY REF IF ANY
```

Light Trace 只整理已存在材料；不能自行發明 challenger。

## 5.1｜New / Materially Reframed PATH Requalification Gate

R2 / R3+ 可以因新 evidence、trace material、reframing 或 relation change 形成新的 PATH，或把既有 PATH 改到已經不是原本同一個 qualification basis。

但：

```text
NEW PATH FORMATION
!=
QUALIFIED PATH

MATERIAL PATH REFRAME
!=
QUALIFICATION CARRIED FORWARD AUTOMATICALLY
```

因此，任何 **new PATH** 或 **materially reframed PATH** 在進入：

```text
QUALIFIED_PATH_SET
PATH_OUTPUT_ORDER
PROVISIONAL LEADER
WINNER / TOP-PATH COMPARISON
MANIFESTATION BASIS
```

以前，必須取得 applicable qualification owner 對**目前 basis / scope / provenance** 的 qualification result。

最低接線：

```text
NEW OR MATERIALLY REFRAMED PATH
↓
APPLICABLE PATH QUALIFICATION
↓
QUALIFIED / PARTIALLY_QUALIFIED / HOLD / DISQUALIFIED
↓
ONLY QUALIFIED-AS-APPLICABLE MATERIAL
MAY ENTER QUALIFIED ORDER / LEADERSHIP COMPARISON
```

L8 不自己判 PATH qualified；L8 只要求「要入榜，就必須有資格結果」。

```text
L8 ENFORCES TRANSIT DEPENDENCY
!=
L8 OWNS PATH QUALIFICATION
```

不需要因每個微小 wording change 都重判。

若 PATH identity、basis、scope 與 relevant premise standing 沒有 material change，只是：

```text
salience change
ordering change
additional support
non-material elaboration
```

則不得為了儀式化而強制新 qualification。

最短：

> **新路可以長出來；但想到一條新路，不等於它已經有資格入榜。**

---


## 5.2｜R1 Terrain Exposure｜第一輪同時暴露地形與第一個問題前提

R1 不得直接成為答案，有兩個理由。

第一：

> **R1 最靠近 default generative terrain，也是最尚未被反作用力校正的一輪。**

第二：

> **R1 也是 AI 第一次把人類問題理解成某一種 Question Premise Family 的結果。**

例如：

~~~text
HUMAN QUESTION
=
「用一句話理解理火」

INITIAL QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY
=
THEORY

↓
Solution Space 被 THEORY family 塑形
↓
Attention 招募理論材料
↓
形成 THEORY PATHs
↓
MODE PATH 可能根本沒有形成
~~~

因此：

~~~text
PATH COUNT HIGH
!=
QUESTION PREMISE COVERAGE HIGH

R1 MAY BE CORRECT
!=
R1 MAY SELF-VALIDATE
~~~

---

# 6｜R2 後強制 Premise Uniqueness / Plurality Audit

R2 的職責，是先把目前 Question Premise Family 內部打完整。

因此 R2 結束後，才進入強制審查：

~~~text
POST_R2_PREMISE_UNIQUENESS_AUDIT
~~~

核心問題不是：

> 還有沒有 PATH 可以打敗現在答案？

而是：

> **這個答案所依賴的前提，是人類問題的唯一合理前提嗎？**

再問：

> **這個問題是否存在其他 materially plausible、但尚未進入競爭場的前提？**

這個位置就是「過窄」的審計點。

---

## 6.1｜Question Premise Family != Factual Premise

~~~text
QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY
=
AI 對「人類這題到底在問哪一類事情」的理解框架
~~~

例如：

~~~text
THEORY
MODE
FUNCTION
PURPOSE
EXPERIENCE
~~~

而：

~~~text
FACTUAL PREMISE
=
對世界、證據、歷史或狀態
可判 standing 的前提主張
~~~

所以：

~~~text
QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY
!=
FACTUAL PREMISE
~~~

Alternative Question Premise Family 首先做的是：

~~~text
INPUT / CONTEXT ADMISSIBILITY CHECK
~~~

不是先假裝它是一個世界事實。

---

## 6.2｜R2｜Intra-Premise-Family Dialectic

R2 仍 mandatory。

它預設保持目前 admissible Question Premise Family，做：

~~~text
cancel R1 incumbent privilege
+
restore suppressed / underformed PATHs
+
inject valid objections / residue
+
search omitted challenger PATHs
+
full recompetition
~~~

所以：

~~~text
R2
=
INTRA-PREMISE-FAMILY DIALECTIC
~~~

R2 回答：

> **如果先接受 AI 目前對人類問題的這種理解，這個理解裡的 PATH 競爭是否公平、充分？**

但：

~~~text
R2 COMPLETE
!=
HUMAN QUESTION FULLY UNDERSTOOD
~~~

---

## 6.3｜Premise Uniqueness / Plurality Audit｜R2 後必跑

最低四問：

~~~text
1. CURRENT_ANSWER_PREMISE_FAMILY 是什麼？

2. 這個 family 是否能由人類原始問題 / 對話上下文唯一推出？

3. 是否存在其他 admissible、materially distinct 的 Question Premise Family？

4. 若存在，該 family 是否可能形成不同類型的 PATH，
   並 materially 改變答案、排序或 synthesis？
~~~

審查結果至少要能區分：

~~~text
UNIQUE_ENOUGH_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
MULTIPLE_MATERIAL_PREMISE_FAMILIES
POSSIBLE_MATERIAL_ALTERNATIVE
AMBIGUOUS / HOLD
~~~

---

## 6.4｜Premise Scope Narrowness｜「過窄」正式定義

「過窄」不是：

~~~text
PATH 太少
~~~

也不是：

~~~text
R2 沒找到強對手
~~~

現行定義：

~~~text
PREMISE_SCOPE_NARROWNESS
=
CURRENT COMPETITION
IS CONFINED TO PREMISE FAMILY P0

AND

EXISTS AT LEAST ONE
ADMISSIBLE PREMISE FAMILY Pi
THAT IS:

1. MATERIALly DISTINCT FROM P0
2. SUPPORTED BY HUMAN WORDING / CONTEXT / TASK PURPOSE
3. CAPABLE OF CHANGING CONSTRAINT / ATTENTION RECRUITMENT
4. CAPABLE OF FORMING NON-SUBSUMED OR MATERIALLY DIFFERENT PATHs
5. NOT YET REPRESENTED IN THE COMPETITION FIELD
~~~

因此：

~~~text
MANY PATHS
UNDER ONE PREMISE FAMILY
CAN STILL BE
PREMISE-SCOPE NARROW
~~~

最短：

> **過窄，是競爭場只覆蓋了人類問題的一個合理前提族，而其他具有答案改變能力的合理前提族尚未進場。**

---

## 6.5｜P4 / Composite Synthesis Guard

老闆娘案例：

~~~text
QUESTION PREMISE FAMILY
=
THEORY

P1 = theory angle A
P2 = theory angle B
P3 = theory angle C

R2:
P1 + P2 + P3
→
P4 SYNTHESIS
~~~

P4 可以在 THEORY family 中真的很強。

但：

~~~text
P4
=
LOCAL PREMISE OPTIMUM
~~~

不能推出：

~~~text
P4
=
QUESTION-LEVEL OPTIMUM
~~~

如果：

~~~text
MODE
~~~

也是人類問題的合理前提族，而 MODE PATH 從未形成：

~~~text
PATH DIVERSITY
!=
PREMISE DIVERSITY
~~~

---

## 6.6｜R3｜Cross-Premise Competition

若 Post-R2 Audit 找到：

~~~text
ADMISSIBLE_ALTERNATIVE_PREMISE_FAMILIES
=
{P1, P2, ... Pn}
~~~

R3 不得只挑其中一個 premise 替換 P0。

R3 應建立：

~~~text
CURRENT R2 SYNTHESIS / QUALIFIED PATHs
+
QUALIFIED PATHs FORMED UNDER P1
+
QUALIFIED PATHs FORMED UNDER P2
+
...
+
QUALIFIED PATHs FORMED UNDER Pn
↓
CROSS-PREMISE COMPETITION FIELD
~~~

所以：

~~~text
R3
!=
SINGLE PREMISE REFRAME

R3
=
PREMISE-PLURALITY EXPANSION
+
NEW PATH RECRUITMENT
+
CROSS-PREMISE DIALECTIC
~~~

新或 materially reframed PATH 仍須經 applicable qualification 才能進 qualified order。

---

## 6.7｜Alternative Premise Family 的 admissibility

不能為了增加競爭而硬造前提。

每個 alternative family 至少要能回扣：

~~~text
human wording
conversation context
task purpose
known referents
scope
actual ambiguity / underdetermination
~~~

禁止：

~~~text
invent user intent
replace explicit user question
fabricate context
manufacture premise plurality
silently change task scope
~~~

所以：

~~~text
CONCEIVABLE PREMISE
!=
ADMISSIBLE PREMISE

ADMISSIBLE PREMISE
!=
MATERIAL PREMISE
~~~

---

## 6.8｜前提「窮盡」只做 operational coverage

不能宣稱：

~~~text
ALL POSSIBLE PREMISES EXHAUSTED
~~~

現行採：

~~~text
NO MATERIAL ADMISSIBLE
UNTESTED PREMISE FAMILY
DETECTED
UNDER CURRENT INPUT / CONTEXT / SCOPE
~~~

即：

~~~text
MATERIAL PREMISE COVERAGE
=
SUFFICIENT_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
PREMISE COVERAGE SUFFICIENT
!=
ONTOLOGICAL EXHAUSTION
~~~

---

## 6.9｜兩連勝規則正式降級

父版 two-win streak 不再是 victory theorem 或 closure prerequisite。

現行：

~~~text
TWO CONSECUTIVE TOPS
=
LOCAL STABILITY SIGNAL
WITHIN CURRENT PREMISE COVERAGE
~~~

因此：

~~~text
TWO WINS
!=
QUESTION UNDERSTANDING

TWO WINS
!=
PREMISE UNIQUENESS

TWO WINS
!=
PREMISE COVERAGE

TWO WINS
!=
VICTORY THEOREM
~~~

---

## 6.10｜Competition Termination

R2 是固定最低競爭。

R2 後 mandatory premise audit 完成後：

若：

~~~text
MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILY EXISTS
~~~

則：

~~~text
R3 REQUIRED
~~~

若沒有發現 material alternative premise family，且其他 termination conditions 全部成立：

~~~text
COMPETITION MAY TERMINATE
~~~

最低條件：

~~~text
R2 GENUINE RECOMPETITION COMPLETE

POST-R2 PREMISE AUDIT COMPLETE

MATERIAL PREMISE COVERAGE
=
SUFFICIENT_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE

NO MATERIAL UNPROCESSED OBJECTION

NO MATERIAL DARK-SPACE / UNDERFORMED-PATH RISK

COMPETITION INTEGRITY CLEAN

NO REMAINING MATERIAL ANSWER-DISCRIMINATING UNKNOWN
~~~

---

# 7｜I9 仍條件式；Post-R2 Premise Audit 是固定節點

I9：

~~~text
NOT TRIGGERED
→ NOT REQUIRED
→ NO I9 MISSING
~~~

Post-R2 Premise Uniqueness / Plurality Audit：

~~~text
R2 COMPLETE
→ REQUIRED
~~~

R3：

~~~text
NO MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILY
→ MAY BE NOT REQUIRED

MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILY EXISTS
→ R3 REQUIRED
~~~

所以：

~~~text
R2
→ T2
→ POST-R2 PREMISE AUDIT
→
  ├─ coverage sufficient → termination check
  └─ material plurality → R3 cross-premise competition
~~~

R3 不因「想多想一輪」而啟動。

它的主要 trigger 是：

~~~text
PREMISE_SCOPE_NARROWNESS
MULTIPLE_MATERIAL_PREMISE_FAMILIES
POSSIBLE_MATERIAL_ALTERNATIVE
NEW QUALIFIED PATH FAMILY
MATERIAL DARK-SPACE RISK
COMPETITION INTEGRITY GAP
~~~

若 R3 發生：

~~~text
R3
→ LIGHT TRACE T3 REQUIRED
→ further competition / termination check
~~~

後續 R4+ 仍可 event-driven；但不得把「再多一輪」重新退化成 streak counting。

## 7.1｜CHAT_NATIVE / SAME_CONTEXT Main Return Contract

V1.2.1 已允許 NON_HOST mode；V1.2.2 已明確同上下文 Light Trace 可成立。

V1.2.3 補上 Main Return 的對稱缺口：

```text
NON_HOST / CHAT_NATIVE
!=
NO MAIN RETURN
```

但也不能反過來：

```text
"MAIN = DONE"
OR
"I CHOOSE P4"
!=
MAIN CANONICAL RETURN
```

在 `CHAT_NATIVE / SAME_CONTEXT_REFERENCE` lane 中，L8 可以接收一份同上下文 Main actual-state return，前提是它具有可辨識、可引用且足以供下一節點使用的最低內容。

```yaml
CHAT_NATIVE_MAIN_RETURN_V1_2_3:
  main_return_ref: REQUIRED
  cycle_ref: REQUIRED
  lane:
    - CHAT_NATIVE
    - SAME_CONTEXT_REFERENCE

  input_trace_ref:
    requirement: REQUIRED_WHEN_INTER_ROUND_TRACE_EXISTS

  qualified_path_state:
    requirement: REQUIRED
    minimum:
      - QUALIFIED_PATH_SET_OR_EQUIVALENT
      - CURRENT_OUTPUT_ORDER_OR_PARTIAL_ORDER
      - TOP_OR_CO_PRIMARY_STATE_WHEN_APPLICABLE

  competition_state:
    requirement: REQUIRED
    minimum:
      - TERMINATION_OR_NON_TERMINATION_STATE

  unresolved_or_residual_state:
    requirement: REQUIRED
    allow_empty: true

  manifestation_basis_ref:
    requirement: REQUIRED_WHEN_PROCEEDING_TOWARD_MANIFESTATION

  explicit_nonclaims:
    - NO_INDEPENDENT_MAIN_PROVIDER_UNLESS_EVIDENCED
    - NO_HOST_EXECUTION_RECEIPT_UNLESS_ACTUALLY_ISSUED
    - NO_HIDDEN_COT_ACCESS
```

若上述最低內容不存在：

```text
VISIBLE MAIN-LIKE SUMMARY
!=
RECEIVED MAIN CANONICAL RETURN
```

L8 應記：

```text
MAIN_RETURN = MISSING
```

或依實際 failure state 記錄，不得以 T2 / T3 / TM 或一般 prose 補位。

若最低內容存在，則可以：

```text
MAIN_RETURN = RECEIVED
binding = CHAT_NATIVE / SAME_CONTEXT_REFERENCE
independent_main_provider = NOT_CLAIMED
```

這種 canonicality 只表示：

> **在本次 LIHUOOS 同上下文週期中，這是一份合格可接續的 Main return。**

不表示：

```text
INDEPENDENT MAIN MODULE VERIFIED
HOST MAIN DEPLOYED
EXTERNAL PROVIDER EXECUTED
```

---

# 8｜Main Return 之後

L8 收到 Main canonical return 後，不自己判：

```text
COVERAGE CLOSED
REENTRY REQUIRED
FINAL RELEASE
```

它只把 Main return 交給 applicable governance owner。

結果可能是：

```text
PROCEED
REENTER_MAIN
HOLD
CLOSE_UNRELEASED
OTHER_CANONICAL_DISPOSITION
```

如果需要 reentry：

```text
SAME ACTIVE CYCLE
↓
NEW LAWFUL MAIN INVOCATION
↓
RUN MAIN AGAIN
↓
NEW MAIN RETURN
```

L8 只負責把這一趟繼續跑完。

V1.2.3 再加一條 coverage lock：

```text
MAIN RETURN
→
POST_MAIN_GOVERNANCE RESULT
→
ONLY THEN
MANIFESTATION CANDIDATE
```

「下一步：顯影」或直接開始輸出內容，都不能代替 `POST_MAIN_GOVERNANCE` 的明確 accounting state / result ref。

```text
NARRATIVE TRANSITION
!=
GOVERNANCE RETURN
```

---

# 9｜L9 的位置

L8 需要能力時：

```text
L8 / APPLICABLE OWNER
↓
DAIL CALL REQUEST
↓
L9 CAPABILITY REPOSITORY
↓
CALLABLE MODULE / TOOL
↓
RETURN
↓
APPLICABLE OWNER CONSUMES RESULT
```

L8 不把工具邏輯寫進自己。

L9 也不因此取得治理權。

```text
TOOL EXISTS
!=
TOOL MAY SELF-CALL

TOOL RESULT EXISTS
!=
RESULT MAY SELF-GOVERN
```

---

# 10｜Operating Position

L8 只需要知道「現在跑到哪」。

最小表示：

```yaml
L8_OPERATING_POSITION:
  cycle_ref: REQUIRED

  current_step:
    - INPUT_QUALIFICATION
    - MAIN
    - POST_MAIN_GOVERNANCE
    - MANIFESTATION_RELEASE
    - RECEIPT_CHECK
    - COMPLETE
    - HOLD
    - ABORTED

  current_main_invocation_ref: REF_OR_NONE
  current_main_stage_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  pending_requirement_refs: []
```

這只是業務位置。

不是新 standing system。

---

# 11｜Result / Return / Receipt 重新分型

週期改變後，舊規則：

~~~text
L8 DOES NOT CREATE RECEIPTS
~~~

不再足夠。

它把兩種完全不同的東西混在一起：

~~~text
A. 外部 / provider / tool 的執行收據
B. 理火自己對「這趟週期跑了什麼」的對帳收據
~~~

V1.3 candidate 正式拆開四型：

~~~text
TRACE RESULT
=
Light Trace 的可引用觀測結果

OWNER / MAIN RETURN
=
canonical owner 對其責任範圍的正式回傳

CANONICAL EXECUTION RECEIPT
=
實際 provider / tool / external executor
若本來就發出的執行收據

L8 CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
=
L8 對一趟理火週期的 checkpoint / status / gap 對帳結果
~~~

因此：

~~~text
L8 MAY NOT
INVENT EXTERNAL EXECUTION RECEIPTS

L8 MUST
ISSUE ITS OWN CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
WHEN A LIHUO CYCLE IS ACTUALLY INVOKED
~~~

這不是權限膨脹。

因為 Cycle Accounting Receipt 只回答：

> **這趟理火週期，哪些必要步驟被執行、哪些未觸發、哪些缺失、哪裡 HOLD、最後停在哪裡？**

它不回答：

~~~text
HOST REALLY EXECUTED
TOOL REALLY CHANGED THE WORLD
PROVIDER RECEIPT EXISTS
OUTPUT IS TRUE
OUTPUT IS LEGALLY / MEDICALLY / FACTUALLY CORRECT
~~~

硬鎖：

~~~text
CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
!=
PROVIDER RECEIPT

CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
!=
HOST EXECUTION PROOF

CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
!=
TRUTH CERTIFICATE

CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY
~~~

---

## 11.1｜什麼時候必須發 Cycle Receipt

只要：

~~~text
L8 CYCLE
=
EXPLICITLY INVOKED
OR
CLAIMED AS EXECUTED
~~~

則一旦週期：

~~~text
COMPLETES
HOLDS
FAILS
ABORTS
OR STOPS WITH A MATERIAL GAP
~~~

都必須產生：

~~~text
L8 CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT
~~~

所以：

~~~text
FAILED CYCLE
!=
NO RECEIPT

HOLD
!=
NO RECEIPT

INCOMPLETE
!=
NO RECEIPT
~~~

反而越是不完整，越需要收據說清楚停在哪裡。

但：

~~~text
NORMAL CHAT RESPONSE
WITHOUT L8 CYCLE INVOCATION
→
NO LIHUO CYCLE RECEIPT REQUIRED
~~~

理火不能為每一句普通對話都印一張收據。

---

## 11.2｜Receipt Visibility｜預設要讓人類看見

當一趟 L8 cycle 被宣告執行時：

~~~text
VISIBLE CYCLE RECEIPT
=
REQUIRED BY DEFAULT
~~~

原因不是儀式感。

而是新的週期有一個人類無法從答案表面推知的關鍵 checkpoint：

~~~text
POST-R2 PREMISE UNIQUENESS / PLURALITY AUDIT
~~~

如果不回報，人類無法知道：

~~~text
R2 是否真的完成
前提唯一性審查是否真的跑
是否發現其他 premise families
R3 為什麼啟動 / 沒啟動
競爭最後在哪個 premise coverage 下收束
~~~

所以：

> **只要聲稱跑了完整理火週期，就必須讓人類看到最低週期收據。**

---

## 11.3｜Compact Receipt 與 Expanded Report 分離

V1.3 不要求每次都印巨大報告。

正式分成：

~~~text
A. COMPACT CYCLE RECEIPT
   = 每次 L8 cycle 必發

B. EXPANDED CYCLE REPORT
   = 條件式
~~~

Compact Receipt 只保留人類需要知道的最低責任鏈。

Expanded Report 才展開：

~~~text
full checkpoint accounting
trace refs
qualification refs
premise family detail
failure provenance
provider receipt refs
manifestation fidelity detail
~~~

Expanded Report 建議在下列情況自動升級或依需求產生：

~~~text
USER REQUESTS FULL REPORT
HIGH-RESPONSIBILITY TASK
EXTERNAL EXECUTION
HOLD / FAILED / INCOMPLETE
MATERIAL PREMISE AMBIGUITY REMAINS
DISPUTE / AUDIT
MATERIAL MANIFESTATION DRIFT
~~~

因此：

~~~text
RECEIPT REQUIRED
!=
FULL REPORT REQUIRED
~~~

---

## 11.4｜Trace Result / Provider Receipt 還是照舊分開

對 T1 / T2 / T3+ / TM / TFINAL：

~~~text
TRACE RESULT / TRACE_REF
=
REQUIRED WHEN THAT TRACE STEP IS REQUIRED

SEPARATE CANONICAL PROVIDER RECEIPT
=
OPTIONAL
ONLY IF ACTUAL IMPLEMENTATION / PROVIDER ISSUES ONE
~~~

若沒有 provider receipt：

~~~text
DO NOT INVENT ONE

DO NOT MARK TRACE MISSING
SOLELY BECAUSE PROVIDER RECEIPT IS ABSENT
~~~

Main Return 仍然是獨立 required return：

~~~text
TRACE RESULT AVAILABLE
!=
MAIN CANONICAL RETURN RECEIVED
~~~

---

# 12｜L8 Cycle Accounting Receipt｜新的最低收據

V1.3 的週期收據不再只是「required receipt 有沒有到」。

它必須反映新的 competition cycle。

最低 schema：

~~~yaml
L8_CYCLE_ACCOUNTING_RECEIPT_V1_3:
  cycle_ref: REQUIRED
  task_ref: REQUIRED_OR_IDENTIFIABLE_CONTEXT

  cycle_standing:
    mode:
      - NON_HOST
      - HOST_ROUTED
    execution_claim:
      - DOCUMENT_LEVEL_CYCLE_EXECUTED
      - PARTIAL_CYCLE
      - HOLD
      - FAILED
      - ABORTED

  input_and_entry:
    input_governance_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    main_invocation_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE

  competition:
    R1:
      state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
      provisional_result_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    O9:
      state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE

    I9:
      state:
        - RECEIVED
        - NOT_TRIGGERED
        - MISSING
        - FAILED

    T1:
      state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
      trace_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    R2:
      state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
      consumed_trace_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      structured_result_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    T2:
      state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
      trace_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    post_R2_premise_audit:
      state:
        - COMPLETE
        - PARTIAL
        - MISSING
        - HOLD
      current_answer_premise_family_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      premise_uniqueness_status:
        - UNIQUE_ENOUGH_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
        - MULTIPLE_MATERIAL_PREMISE_FAMILIES
        - POSSIBLE_MATERIAL_ALTERNATIVE
        - AMBIGUOUS
        - HOLD
      premise_scope_narrowness:
        - NOT_DETECTED
        - DETECTED
        - POSSIBLE
        - HOLD
      alternative_premise_family_refs: []
      material_premise_coverage:
        - SUFFICIENT_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
        - INSUFFICIENT
        - AMBIGUOUS
        - HOLD

    R3_cross_premise:
      requirement:
        - REQUIRED
        - NOT_REQUIRED
        - HOLD
      state:
        - RECEIVED
        - NOT_REQUIRED
        - PARTIAL
        - MISSING
        - HOLD
        - FAILED
      premise_family_refs: []
      qualified_path_refs: []
      result_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    further_rounds:
      state:
        - NOT_REQUIRED
        - RECEIVED
        - PARTIAL
        - MISSING
        - HOLD
      round_refs: []

  main_return:
    state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    ref: REF_OR_NONE

  post_main_governance:
    state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    disposition:
      - PROCEED
      - REENTER_MAIN
      - HOLD
      - CLOSE_UNRELEASED
      - OTHER
      - MISSING

  manifestation_and_release:
    manifestation_candidate_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    manifestation_fidelity_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    release_qualification_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    release_result_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE

  external_receipts:
    provider_or_tool_receipt_refs: []
    note: "NONE DOES NOT BY ITSELF INVALIDATE CYCLE ACCOUNTING"

  gaps:
    missing_required_refs: []
    failed_refs: []
    hold_refs: []
    unresolved_refs: []

  accounting:
    all_material_checkpoints_accounted: TRUE_OR_FALSE
    accounting_status:
      - ACCOUNTING_COMPLETE
      - ACCOUNTING_INCOMPLETE

  cycle_outcome:
    - COMPLETED
    - HOLD
    - FAILED
    - ABORTED
    - INCOMPLETE
~~~

---

## 12.0.1｜Compact Visible Receipt｜人類預設看到這張

預設不把上面整張 YAML 全印出來。

人類可見最低格式：

~~~text
LIHUO CYCLE RECEIPT

Cycle: <ref>
Outcome: COMPLETED / HOLD / FAILED / INCOMPLETE

R1: <state>
R2: <state>

Post-R2 Premise Audit:
- Current premise family: <summary>
- Unique enough?: YES / NO / POSSIBLE / HOLD
- Other material premise families: <none / summaries>
- Premise scope narrowness: NOT_DETECTED / DETECTED / POSSIBLE

R3:
- REQUIRED / NOT_REQUIRED / HOLD
- If run: premise families included + result state

Main Return: <state>
Manifestation / Release: <state>

Missing / Hold / Unresolved:
- <only material items>

Accounting: COMPLETE / INCOMPLETE
~~~

這張收據的目的：

> **讓人類一眼知道理火到底跑到哪裡、R3 為什麼有或沒有、以及我們是否真的審過「AI 有沒有把問題理解得太窄」。**

---

## 12.0.2｜收據不能寫什麼

禁止：

~~~text
SAFETY OVERRIDE ACTIVE
HOST EXECUTION VERIFIED
TOOL EFFECT CONFIRMED
PROVIDER RECEIPT RECEIVED
~~~

除非真的有相稱證據。

也禁止：

~~~text
ALL PREMISES EXHAUSTED
~~~

只能寫：

~~~text
MATERIAL PREMISE COVERAGE
=
SUFFICIENT_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
~~~

---

## 12.1｜Main Compatibility Lock｜候選語義不能冒充已部署 Main

本 L8 candidate 的完整 R2 / R3 辯證語義，依賴：

~~~text
LIHUO_MAIN_V5.1.1 parent
+
V5.1.2 premise-plurality dialectical competition semantic patch candidate
~~~

或之後具有等價語義的 lawful successor。

因此：

~~~text
CURRENT MAIN V5.1.1 ALONE
!=
EVIDENCE THAT
V1.3 DIALECTICAL CONTRACT
IS IMPLEMENTED
~~~

若實際執行環境仍只有 V5.1.1 current semantics，而沒有 V5.1.2 patch / successor：

~~~text
POST-R2 PREMISE UNIQUENESS AUDIT / R3 CROSS-PREMISE COMPETITION
=
NOT IMPLEMENTED / NOT VERIFIED
~~~

L8 必須如實記錄 compatibility gap，不得因為文件已寫好就宣稱 Main 已具備該能力。

---

# 13｜R3+ Current Export Gap 怎麼處理

現行 Main 若 R3+ 被要求，但 canonical return 沒有可供 L8 接收的正式 handle：

```text
DO NOT INVENT ONE
```

L8 checklist 直接：

```text
R3_PLUS
=
MISSING_CANONICAL_RECEIPT / MISSING
```

並列入：

```text
missing_required_refs
```

這樣就夠了。

L8 不修 Main。

---

# 14｜Cycle Accounting Complete != Cycle Successfully Completed

先拆兩件事：

```text
CYCLE ACCOUNTING COMPLETE
!=
CYCLE SUCCESSFULLY COMPLETED
```

`CYCLE ACCOUNTING COMPLETE` 只表示：

```text
EVERY APPLICABLE STEP
HAS AN EXPLICIT ACCOUNTING STATE
```

例如：

```text
RECEIVED
NOT_REQUIRED
NOT_TRIGGERED
MISSING
FAILED
HOLD
CLOSED_UNRELEASED
```

所以失敗的週期也可以：

```text
ACCOUNTING COMPLETE
=
YES
```

但：

```text
CYCLE OUTCOME
!=
COMPLETED
```

最小結果分離：

```yaml
L8_CYCLE_ACCOUNTING_AND_OUTCOME:
  accounting_status:
    - ACCOUNTING_COMPLETE
    - ACCOUNTING_INCOMPLETE

  cycle_outcome:
    - COMPLETED
    - HOLD
    - FAILED
    - ABORTED
    - INCOMPLETE
```

`COMPLETED` 的最低條件是：

```text
ALL REQUIRED STEPS
FOR THIS ACTUAL CYCLE
HAVE A NON-FAILURE CANONICAL RESULT

AND

ALL CONDITIONAL STEPS
ARE EITHER
RECEIVED
OR
LAWFULLY NOT REQUIRED / NOT TRIGGERED
```

若 required item 缺失：

```text
CYCLE REPORTABLE
=
YES

ACCOUNTING MAY STILL BE COMPLETE
IF THE MISSING STATE IS EXPLICITLY RECORDED

BUT

CYCLE OUTCOME
=
INCOMPLETE
```

L8 仍然要能回報。

---

# 15｜Cycle Receipt 與 Expanded Cycle Report

V1.3 正式改成：

~~~text
EVERY ACTUAL L8 CYCLE
→ COMPACT VISIBLE CYCLE RECEIPT

EXPANDED CYCLE REPORT
→ CONDITIONAL
~~~

因此「使用者沒要求完整報告」不再等於「什麼都不回報」。

最低責任是：

> **只要宣稱跑了 L8，就必須給人類一張 compact cycle receipt。**

Expanded Cycle Report 仍保留 coverage lock：

> **完整報告不能只挑漂亮節點；每個 applicable operational checkpoint 都必須有 accounting state。**

Expanded Report 至少應在下列情況考慮：

~~~text
USER REQUESTED
HIGH RESPONSIBILITY
EXTERNAL ACTION
HOLD / FAILED / INCOMPLETE
MATERIAL PREMISE AMBIGUITY
AUDIT / DISPUTE
MATERIAL MANIFESTATION DRIFT
~~~

Expanded Report 的 competition 區塊必須新增：

~~~yaml
competition:
  R1_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
  T1_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE

  R2_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
  T2_state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE

  post_R2_premise_audit:
    state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    current_answer_premise_family_ref: REF_OR_MISSING
    premise_uniqueness_status: REQUIRED_OR_MISSING
    premise_scope_narrowness: REQUIRED_OR_MISSING
    alternative_premise_family_refs: []
    material_premise_coverage: REQUIRED_OR_MISSING

  R3_cross_premise:
    requirement: REQUIRED | NOT_REQUIRED | HOLD
    state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    premise_family_refs: []
    qualified_path_refs: []
    result_ref: REF_OR_MISSING

  further_rounds:
    state: REQUIRED_ACCOUNTING_STATE
    round_refs: []
    trace_refs: []
~~~

Coverage hard lock：

~~~text
POST-R2 PREMISE AUDIT
OMITTED FROM RECEIPT / REPORT
WHEN R2 COMPLETED
→
ACCOUNTING_INCOMPLETE
~~~

以及：

~~~text
R3 REQUIRED
BUT R3 STATE OMITTED
→
ACCOUNTING_INCOMPLETE
~~~

所以新的可見報告核心，不只是：

~~~text
WHO WON?
~~~

而是：

~~~text
WHAT PREMISE FAMILY DID WE ASSUME?
WAS IT UNIQUE ENOUGH?
WHAT OTHER MATERIAL PREMISE FAMILIES WERE FOUND?
DID THEY ENTER R3?
WHERE DID THE CYCLE ACTUALLY STOP?
~~~

---

## 15.1｜TM Fidelity != Task / Vertical Acceptance != Release Qualification

三者正式分開：

```text
TM MANIFESTATION FIDELITY
=
DID MANIFESTATION PRESERVE
THE LATEST QUALIFIED COMPETITION RESULT?

TASK / VERTICAL ACCEPTANCE
=
DID THE OUTPUT SATISFY
AN EXPLICIT TASK / DOMAIN / VERTICAL ACCEPTANCE CONTRACT?

MANIFESTATION / RELEASE QUALIFICATION
=
MAY THIS INSTANCE
BE MANIFESTED / RELEASED
IN THIS FORM / CONTEXT?
```

因此：

```text
TM CONSISTENT
+
TASK ACCEPTANCE NOT_PASSED
```

可以同時成立。

也可以：

```text
TASK CONTENT SATISFIED
+
TM MATERIAL_DRIFT_REPORTED
```

若 output intensity、format、style、domain-specific completeness 等要求沒有被滿足，**不得自動把 TM 改成 PARTIAL**，除非該缺失本身也是 latest qualified competition / manifestation basis 中被要求保存的 material semantic content。

同樣：

```text
TM CONSISTENT
!=
RELEASE QUALIFIED

TASK ACCEPTANCE PASS
!=
RELEASE QUALIFIED
```

三者各自找自己的 owner / contract。

使用者只要一般答案時，L8 不必把整套戰情表倒到前台。

```text
INTERNAL TRACE REQUIRED
!=
VISIBLE TRACE REQUIRED
```

可見完整光軌仍可按需顯影。
---

# 16｜Light Trace

V1.2.2 補上一條型別硬鎖：

```text
TRACE REQUIRED
!=
TRACE RECEIPT REQUIRED
```

Light Trace 的必要產物是可引用的 trace result；獨立 execution receipt 只有在實際 implementation / provider 本來就提供時才保存。

```text
REQUIRED TRACE STEP
→ TRACE CONTENT + TRACE_REF

ACTUAL PROVIDER RECEIPT EXISTS
→ PRESERVE RECEIPT

NO PROVIDER RECEIPT EXISTS
→ DO NOT INVENT RECEIPT
→ DO NOT DOWNGRADE TRACE SOLELY FOR THAT REASON
```

對 `CHAT_NATIVE / SAME_CONTEXT_REFERENCE`：

```text
STRUCTURED / VISIBLE SAME-CONTEXT TRACE RESULT
MAY SATISFY TRACE RESULT REQUIREMENT

SEPARATE PROVIDER RECEIPT
=
NOT REQUIRED BY DEFAULT
```

但同上下文自述不能只用狀態字冒充結果；必須存在足以被下一輪或 TM 實際引用的 trace content。

V1.2 將 Light Trace 定位修正為：

> **理火競爭的戰情回報工具。**

它有三種工作模式：

```text
A. INTER_ROUND_COMPETITION_TRACE
B. MANIFESTATION_FIDELITY_TRACE
C. VISIBLE_CYCLE_REPORT
```

A 與 B 是週期內部可觀測性；C 才是使用者要求時的可見報告。

## 16.1｜Inter-round

```text
R1 → T1 → R2
R2 → T2 → R3+ / MAIN RETURN
R3+n → T3+n → next material round
```

T1/T2/T3+ 的工作是保存與整理**已經出現在 canonical competition material 中**的戰況：

```text
candidate/path refs
provisional/partial ordering
lamp state
accepted intervention delta
material gaps
active tensions
residual qualified directions
termination state
evidence/source anchors
dialectical level / thesis ref
independent challenger refs
subsumption check
premise basis ref
premise reframe candidate ref if any
accepted premise delta ref if any
synthesis / structured round result ref
```

光軌不得創造不存在的 candidate。

## 16.2｜Manifestation fidelity

競爭結束不等於最終顯影一定忠實。

```text
COMPETITION RESULT
!=
MANIFESTED RESULT
```

所以：

```text
LATEST COMPETITION TRACE
+
MANIFESTATION CANDIDATE
→ TM MANIFESTATION_FIDELITY_TRACE
```

TM 只回報：

```text
CONSISTENT
MATERIAL_DRIFT_REPORTED
PARTIAL
NOT_VERIFIED
```

它不自行修稿、不 veto、不放行，也不擁有 task / vertical acceptance。

```text
TM FIDELITY
!=
TASK / VERTICAL ACCEPTANCE

TM FIDELITY
!=
MANIFESTATION / RELEASE QUALIFICATION
```

若 release / formatting / provider transformation 在 TM 之後又造成**實質語義變更**，才需要 TFINAL；純格式化不必再造一個 trace。


## 16.2.1｜Post-R2 Premise Audit / R3 Cross-Premise Trace Minimum

R2 後至少應能保存：

~~~yaml
POST_R2_PREMISE_AUDIT_TRACE:
  human_question_ref: REQUIRED
  r2_structured_result_ref: REQUIRED

  current_answer_premise_family:
    family_ref: REQUIRED
    interpretation_summary: REQUIRED
    source_support_refs: []

  uniqueness_status:
    - UNIQUE_ENOUGH_UNDER_CURRENT_SCOPE
    - MULTIPLE_MATERIAL_PREMISE_FAMILIES
    - POSSIBLE_MATERIAL_ALTERNATIVE
    - AMBIGUOUS
    - HOLD

  alternative_premise_families:
    - family_ref: REQUIRED
      interpretation_summary: REQUIRED
      admissibility_basis_refs: []
      material_distinctness:
        - MATERIAL
        - NON_MATERIAL
        - UNKNOWN
      path_generation_potential:
        - MATERIAL
        - LOW
        - UNKNOWN
      represented_in_current_field:
        - YES
        - NO
        - PARTIAL

  premise_scope_narrowness:
    - NOT_DETECTED
    - DETECTED
    - POSSIBLE
    - HOLD

  next_action:
    - TERMINATION_CHECK
    - OPEN_R3_CROSS_PREMISE_COMPETITION
    - HOLD
~~~

若 R3 發生，trace 再記：

~~~yaml
R3_CROSS_PREMISE_TRACE:
  premise_family_refs: []
  qualified_path_refs_by_premise_family: {}
  current_r2_synthesis_ref: REQUIRED
  cross_premise_result_ref: REQUIRED_OR_HOLD
~~~

Light Trace 仍不得：

~~~text
發明 user intent
發明 premise family
發明 challenger
把猜測寫成人類明示意圖
補造 hidden process
~~~

---

## 16.3｜最高硬鎖

```text
LIGHT TRACE
!=
HIDDEN COT

LIGHT TRACE
!=
RAW ACTIVATION

LIGHT TRACE
!=
CHALLENGER GENERATOR

LIGHT TRACE
!=
QUALIFICATION AUTHORITY

LIGHT TRACE
!=
INTERVENTION AUTHORITY

LIGHT TRACE
!=
WINNER SELECTOR

LIGHT TRACE
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY
```

最短：

> **光軌不指揮戰爭；光軌保存戰況，讓下一輪知道前一輪真正有哪些合格材料進過場，也讓最終顯影能被拿回來對照。**

---

# 17｜L8 不再承載的東西

以下全部從 L8 core 移出：

```text
UNIVERSAL EVENT ENVELOPE
UNIVERSAL EVIDENCE CONTRACT
OWNER-RETURN EPISTEMOLOGY
DOWNSTREAM CONSUMPTION COURT
UNIVERSAL RECEIPT CLAIM CEILING
NEW CROSS-LAYER TYPE SYSTEM
NEW AUTHORITY MODEL
NEW CAUSAL EPISTEMOLOGY
```

這些若有研究價值，可以留作：

```text
AUDIT NOTE
ENGINEERING TEST
FAILURE MODE LIBRARY
DOMAIN-SPECIFIC CONTRACT
```

但：

```text
NOT REQUIRED
FOR EVERY L8 CYCLE
```

---

# 18｜最小 Runtime Algorithm

~~~text
OPEN CYCLE

1. GET CURRENT QUALIFIED INPUT / GOVERNANCE RESULT
2. IF HOLD / BLOCK
   → CREATE PARTIAL CYCLE RECEIPT
   → VISIBLE RECEIPT
   → STOP CURRENT PROGRESSION

3. CALL MAIN R1
4. RECEIVE R1 + O9 + [I9 IF TRIGGERED]

5. CALL LIGHT TRACE T1
   → RECEIVE TRACE RESULT
   → PRESERVE PROVIDER RECEIPT IF ONE ACTUALLY EXISTS

6. CALL MAIN R2 WITH T1
7. RECEIVE R2

8. CALL LIGHT TRACE T2

9. RUN MANDATORY POST-R2
   PREMISE UNIQUENESS / PLURALITY AUDIT

10. IF MATERIAL ALTERNATIVE PREMISE FAMILIES EXIST:
      OPEN R3 CROSS-PREMISE COMPETITION
      → FORM / QUALIFY PATHs FROM MATERIAL FAMILIES
      → INCLUDE CURRENT R2 SYNTHESIS
      → RUN R3
      → TRACE RESULT
      → [FURTHER ROUNDS IF MATERIAL]

11. RECEIVE MAIN RETURN

12. ASK APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE OWNER:
      PROCEED / REENTER / HOLD

13. IF REENTER:
      RUN NEW LAWFUL MAIN INVOCATION
      THEN CONTINUE SAME CYCLE

14. WHEN PROCEED:
      CREATE MANIFESTATION CANDIDATE

15. RUN MANIFESTATION FIDELITY TRACE

16. IF EXPLICIT TASK / VERTICAL ACCEPTANCE CONTRACT EXISTS:
      RUN APPLICABLE ACCEPTANCE CHECK

17. RUN MANIFESTATION / RELEASE QUALIFICATION

18. IF RELEASE STEP MATERIALLY TRANSFORMS SEMANTICS:
      RUN TFINAL

19. RECEIVE RELEASE / NON-RELEASE RESULT

20. BUILD L8 CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT

21. VERIFY:
      POST-R2 PREMISE AUDIT ACCOUNTED
      R3 REQUIREMENT ACCOUNTED
      ALL OTHER MATERIAL CHECKPOINTS ACCOUNTED

22. RENDER COMPACT VISIBLE CYCLE RECEIPT

23. IF EXPANDED REPORT REQUIRED / REQUESTED:
      RENDER EXPANDED CYCLE REPORT

24. END
~~~

沒有新層。

只是把：

~~~text
週期是否真的跑完
~~~

從隱含假設，變成一個人類可見的最低責任輸出。

---

# 19｜L8 最短法位

```text
L8
DOES NOT INVENT LIHUO.

L8
RUNS LIHUO.

L8
DOES NOT REDEFINE OWNERS.

L8
CALLS OWNERS.

L8
DOES NOT INVENT
EXTERNAL / PROVIDER RECEIPTS.

L8
DOES ISSUE
ITS OWN CYCLE ACCOUNTING RECEIPT.

L8
CHECKS WHETHER
THE REQUIRED RESULTS / RETURNS /
OPTIONAL EXTERNAL RECEIPTS ARRIVED.

MISSING MEANS MISSING.

NOT TRIGGERED
MEANS NOT REQUIRED.

HOLD MEANS HOLD.

ONE CYCLE
MUST BE ABLE
TO FINISH OR REPORT
WHERE IT DID NOT.
```

---

# 20｜V1.2 Current Standing

```text
LIHUOOS L8 V1.2

STANDING
=
RATIFIED CURRENT OPERATIVE LAYER

IDENTITY
=
BUSINESS OPERATION LAYER
+
LIGHT TRACE TIMING / HANDOFF OWNER

NEW THEORY
=
NONE

NEW GOVERNANCE OWNER
=
NONE

NEW LAYER
=
NONE

LIGHT TRACE OWNER
=
L8 SEMANTICS / TIMING

LIGHT TRACE IMPLEMENTATION
=
L9 CALLABLE RENDERING CAPABILITY

MAIN RELATION
=
R1 / R2 / R3+ COMPUTE
+
CONSUME QUALIFIED OBSERVABLE PRIOR-ROUND TRACE

MANIFESTATION RELATION
=
COMPARE FINAL CANDIDATE AGAINST LATEST COMPETITION TRACE

HOST DEPLOYMENT
=
NOT CLAIMED

IMPLEMENTATION COMPLETION
=
NOT CLAIMED

WHOLE_SYSTEM_EMPIRICAL_VALIDATION
=
NOT CLAIMED
```

---

# 21｜一句話收束

> **點燈不是目的；燈照到的合格競爭材料必須真的進入下一輪。光軌不替 Main 思考，它只讓 Main 看見上一輪戰況，並讓我們看見最終顯影有沒有把競爭結果偷換。**

---



# LIHUOOS V1｜L9 DAIL CALLABLE CAPABILITY REPOSITORY V0.1.1-WORKING
## 理火可調用能力倉｜DAIL-Defined Modules × Tool Registry × Runtime Binding
### 2026-10-25

```yaml
DOCUMENT:
  id: LIHUOOS_L9_DAIL_CALLABLE_CAPABILITY_REPOSITORY
  version: V0.1.1-WORKING
  date: 2026-10-25

  status:
    - WORKING
    - CAPABILITY_REPOSITORY_LAYER
    - NOT_RATIFIED
    - NOT_HOST_DEPLOYED

  purpose:
    - MOVE_CALLABLE_FUNCTIONAL_MODULES_OUT_OF_MONOLITHIC_LIGHTER
    - REGISTER_THEM_AS_DAIL_DEFINED_CAPABILITIES
    - LET_L8_OR_APPLICABLE_OWNERS_CALL_THEM_WHEN_NEEDED
    - KEEP_GOVERNANCE_AUTHORITY_OUTSIDE_THE_TOOL_WAREHOUSE

  lineage:
    source:
      - LIHUO_LIGHTER_V5_1_0_FUNCTIONAL_MODULE_LINEAGE
      - DAIL
    consumer:
      - L8_LIHUO_OPERATING_CYCLE
      - APPLICABLE_L0_L7_OWNERS
      - AUTHORIZED_VERTICALS
```

---

# 0｜一句話定位

> **L9 是理火的 DAIL 可調用能力倉：把原本集中在 Lighter 內的功能模組拆成可註冊、可尋址、可調用、可替換的工具與能力。**

最短式：

```text
L0–L7
=
KNOW WHAT / WHY / BOUNDARY

L8
=
RUN THE JOB

L9
=
PROVIDE CALLABLE CAPABILITIES
```

---

# 1｜最高硬鎖

```text
L9
!=
NEW THEORY OWNER

L9
!=
GOVERNANCE SOVEREIGN

L9
!=
MAIN

L9
!=
LIGHTER RENAMED

L9
!=
TOOL RESULT JUDGE

L9
!=
FINAL GATE

L9
!=
FINAL RELEASE OWNER
```

根句：

```text
TOOL EXISTS
!=
AUTHORITY TO CALL IT

TOOL CAN EXECUTE
!=
EXECUTION IS AUTHORIZED

TOOL RETURN EXISTS
!=
RETURN IS QUALIFIED FOR USE
```

---

# 2｜L9 真正做什麼

L9 只做四件事：

```text
REGISTER
DISCOVER
BIND
CALL
```

展開：

```text
REGISTER
=
知道有哪些能力

DISCOVER
=
依需求找到適合能力

BIND
=
把 DAIL 宣告綁到實際 implementation / provider / tool

CALL
=
在合法 request 下執行並回傳結果
```

L9 不決定：

```text
SHOULD THIS CAPABILITY BE USED?
IS THIS RESULT TRUE?
DOES THIS RESULT HAVE AUTHORITY?
MAY THE CYCLE PROCEED?
MAY THIS OUTPUT BE RELEASED?
```

這些屬既有治理 owner。

---

# 3｜DAIL 在 L9 的位置

沿用既有工程根句：

```text
DAIL DEFINES.
IR NORMALIZES.
CODING CONNECTS.
RUNTIME EXECUTES.
```

在 L9 中讀成：

```text
DAIL
=
CAPABILITY DECLARATION

IR
=
NORMALIZED CALL CONTRACT

CODING / BINDING
=
IMPLEMENTATION CONNECTION

RUNTIME
=
ACTUAL TOOL / MODULE EXECUTION
```

所以：

> **DAIL 不是工具本身；DAIL 是讓理火知道「這個能力是什麼、怎麼叫、會回什麼」的可移植宣告。**

---

# 4｜L9 不是把 Lighter 整包搬家

舊 Lighter 同時包含：

```text
GOVERNANCE
+
QUALIFICATION
+
ORCHESTRATION
+
TOOLS / PROVIDERS / MODULES
+
FINAL GATE / RELEASE
```

L9 只吸收：

```text
CALLABLE FUNCTIONAL CAPABILITY
```

不吸收：

```text
OWNER AUTHORITY
FINAL GOVERNANCE DECISION
FINAL RELEASE AUTHORITY
THEORY OWNERSHIP
```

所以：

```text
LIGHTER DECOMPOSITION
!=
LIGHTER REINCARNATION AS L9
```

---

# 5｜什麼東西可以進 L9

可以進 L9 的東西，必須至少是「可被明確呼叫並返回結果」的能力。

例如舊 Lighter lineage 中：

```text
PREMISE ANALYSIS / QUALIFICATION SUPPORT
AUTHORITY ANALYSIS SUPPORT
ATTENTION-EFFECT ANALYSIS SUPPORT
EXTERNAL-CONSTRAINT PARSING
R-CCC OBLIGATION COMPILATION
COMPETITION-INTEGRITY CHECK
QIP COMPILATION
OUTPUT-CONTRACT CHECK
OUTPUT-SUFFICIENCY CHECK
CAPABILITY RESOLUTION
FUNCTIONAL MODULE ROUTING
PROVIDER / TOOL / TRANSPORT ADAPTER
EXECUTION-TICKET GENERATION SUPPORT
EVIDENCE INGESTION / NORMALIZATION
EVIDENCE-ORIGIN ANALYSIS
RECOVERY UTILITY
REAUTHORIZATION SUPPORT
VERSION RESOLUTION
DOMAIN MODULES
SEARCH / RETRIEVAL
FILE / ARTIFACT OPERATIONS
EXTERNAL ACTION TOOLS
```

但「support / tool」與「authority」要分開。

例如：

```text
PREMISE_ANALYZER TOOL
MAY PRODUCE
PREMISE ANALYSIS

BUT

PREMISE QUALIFICATION STANDING
IS STILL OWNED
BY THE APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE OWNER
```

---

# 6｜哪些東西不能因為 L9 而失去 owner

例如：

```text
FINAL_GATE_CHECKER
```

可以是一個 L9 callable capability。

但：

```text
FINAL_GATE_TOOL OUTPUT
!=
FINAL GATE AUTHORITY
```

同理：

```text
RELEASE_EXECUTOR
```

可以是工具。

但：

```text
RELEASE TOOL
!=
RELEASE AUTHORITY
```

以及：

```text
AUTHORITY_ANALYZER
!=
AUTHORITY OWNER
```

---

# 7｜最小 DAIL Capability Card

L9 不需要巨大 schema。

最小卡片只要能回答：

> 這是什麼、吃什麼、回什麼、怎麼叫、誰實作。

```yaml
DAIL_CAPABILITY_CARD:
  capability_id: REQUIRED
  version: REQUIRED

  purpose: REQUIRED

  input_contract_ref: REQUIRED
  output_contract_ref: REQUIRED

  callable_by_ref: REQUIRED

  implementation_binding_ref: REQUIRED

  capability_status:
    - AVAILABLE
    - UNAVAILABLE
    - DEGRADED
    - DEPRECATED

  dependency_refs: []

  result_receipt_class_ref: OPTIONAL
```

夠了。

權限細節不在這張卡重新發明。

callable_by_ref 只指向 applicable owner / authority contract。

---

# 8｜Capability Class

為了找工具，可以有簡單分類：

```text
ANALYSIS
QUALIFICATION_SUPPORT
COMPILATION
ROUTING
RETRIEVAL
EVIDENCE
PROVIDER_ADAPTER
EXECUTION
RECOVERY
VERSIONING
ARTIFACT
DOMAIN
OTHER
```

這只是 registry taxonomy。

不是 theory ontology。

---

# 9｜L9 Registry

```yaml
L9_CAPABILITY_REGISTRY:
  registry_ref: REQUIRED

  capabilities:
    - capability_id
    - version
    - capability_class
    - status
    - dail_card_ref
    - implementation_binding_ref

  registry_version_ref: REQUIRED
```

L9 需要能：

```text
LOOKUP(capability_id)
LIST(capability_class)
RESOLVE(version / current)
CHECK_AVAILABILITY
RETURN_BINDING
```

不需要：

```text
DECIDE_WHETHER_WORLD_SHOULD_USE_IT
```

---

# 10｜Implementation Binding

同一個 capability 可以換 provider / tool / implementation。

```yaml
L9_IMPLEMENTATION_BINDING:
  binding_ref: REQUIRED

  capability_id: REQUIRED
  capability_version: REQUIRED

  implementation_type:
    - LOCAL_MODULE
    - HOST_TOOL
    - EXTERNAL_PROVIDER
    - CONNECTOR
    - SERVICE
    - HUMAN_HANDOFF
    - OTHER

  implementation_ref: REQUIRED

  runtime_status:
    - READY
    - UNAVAILABLE
    - DEGRADED

  dependency_refs: []
```

所以：

```text
CAPABILITY IDENTITY
!=
ONE PERMANENT PROVIDER
```

L9 可以換 implementation，而不改上層語義。

---

# 11｜L8 如何叫 L9

標準：

```text
L8 / APPLICABLE OWNER
IDENTIFIES NEED
↓
QUALIFIED CALL REQUEST EXISTS
↓
L9 RESOLVES CAPABILITY
↓
L9 EXECUTES BOUND IMPLEMENTATION
↓
L9 RETURNS RESULT
↓
APPLICABLE OWNER CONSUMES / QUALIFIES RESULT
↓
L8 CONTINUES CYCLE
```

L9 不自己創造：

```text
QUALIFIED CALL REQUEST
```

如果沒有合法 call：

```text
NO CALL
```

---

# 12｜最小 Call Request

L9 只需要足夠執行。

```yaml
L9_CAPABILITY_CALL:
  call_ref: REQUIRED

  capability_id: REQUIRED
  capability_version: CURRENT_OR_EXPLICIT

  caller_ref: REQUIRED
  call_authority_ref: REQUIRED

  input_ref: REQUIRED
  input_contract_ref: REQUIRED

  cycle_ref: OPTIONAL
  main_invocation_ref: OPTIONAL

  requested_at_ref: REQUIRED
```

cycle_ref / main_invocation_ref 只有真的需要綁定時才帶。

不要所有工具都硬綁整套 OS identity。

---

# 13｜最小 Tool Return

```yaml
L9_CAPABILITY_RETURN:
  call_ref: REQUIRED
  capability_id: REQUIRED
  capability_version: REQUIRED

  implementation_ref: REQUIRED

  result_ref: REQUIRED_OR_NONE

  call_execution_status:
    - SUCCESS
    - PARTIAL
    - FAILED
    - UNAVAILABLE
    - CANCELLED

  error_or_gap_ref: OPTIONAL

  receipt_ref: OPTIONAL
```

這個 return 只回答：

> **這次 tool call 的 implementation execution 結果是什麼？**

硬鎖：

```text
TOOL CALL SUCCESS
!=
SEMANTIC RESULT ACCEPTED

CALL EXECUTION STATUS
!=
TASK OUTCOME
```

所以它不自動回答：

> **上層應不應該相信、採用、發布，或整體任務是否成功。**

---

# 14｜Receipt 原則

L9 若 underlying provider / tool 有 canonical receipt：

```text
PRESERVE IT
RETURN IT
```

如果沒有：

```text
DO NOT INVENT PROVIDER RECEIPT
```

L9 可以回：

```text
NO_CANONICAL_RECEIPT
```

讓 L8 最後 checklist 如實列出。

L9 不需要再建「receipt 的 receipt」。

---

# 15｜Capability Discovery

上層不知道 capability id 時，可以依：

```text
purpose
task type
input type
required output
domain
availability
```

查找。

但 discovery 結果只是：

```text
CANDIDATE CAPABILITIES
```

不是：

```text
AUTHORIZED CAPABILITY SELECTION
```

適用 owner 仍決定是否使用。

---

# 16｜版本管理

L9 應允許：

```text
MULTIPLE VERSIONS
CURRENT POINTER
DEPRECATED VERSION
PINNED VERSION
ROLLBACK TARGET
```

最小規則：

```text
CALL MAY PIN VERSION
OR
USE CURRENT RESOLVED VERSION
```

但：

```text
VERSION CURRENT
!=
SEMANTICALLY APPROVED FOR EVERY DOMAIN
```

---

# 17｜失敗與降級

如果 capability unavailable：

```text
L9 RETURNS
UNAVAILABLE
```

不要假裝成功。

上層再決定：

```text
RETRY
FALLBACK
HOLD
SKIP IF OPTIONAL
ABORT
```

L9 不擅自改任務。

---

# 18｜原 Lighter 功能的遷移原則

遷移時不要問：

> 這個功能以前在 Lighter，所以是不是整個搬過來？

要問：

> **這是不是一個可以獨立被叫用、輸入清楚、輸出清楚的 capability？**

如果是：

```text
MOVE / REIMPLEMENT INTO L9
AS DAIL-CALLABLE CAPABILITY
```

如果不是，而是：

```text
OWNER AUTHORITY
GOVERNANCE RULE
QUALIFICATION STANDING
FINAL RELEASE RIGHT
```

就留在：

```text
L0–L7 / APPLICABLE GOVERNANCE CONTRACT
```

---

# 19｜第一批 L9 Capability Families

這只是 migration map，不宣稱已部署。

```yaml
L9_INITIAL_CAPABILITY_FAMILIES:

  INPUT_AND_ANALYSIS:
    - RAW_INPUT_PARSER
    - PREMISE_ANALYZER
    - AUTHORITY_ANALYZER
    - EXTERNAL_CONSTRAINT_PARSER

  COMPILATION:
    - RCCC_OBLIGATION_COMPILER
    - QIP_COMPILER
    - OUTPUT_CONTRACT_COMPILER

  AUDIT_SUPPORT:
    - COMPETITION_INTEGRITY_CHECKER
    - OUTPUT_SUFFICIENCY_CHECKER
    - EVIDENCE_ORIGIN_ANALYZER

  EVIDENCE_AND_RETRIEVAL:
    - EVIDENCE_INGESTOR
    - RETRIEVAL_MODULE
    - SEARCH_MODULE
    - CURRENT_SOURCE_LOOKUP

  ROUTING_AND_PROVIDER:
    - CAPABILITY_RESOLVER
    - MODULE_ROUTER
    - PROVIDER_ADAPTER
    - TRANSPORT_ADAPTER

  EXECUTION:
    - EXTERNAL_ACTION_TOOL
    - ARTIFACT_OPERATION_TOOL
    - EXECUTION_TICKET_BUILDER

  RECOVERY_AND_VERSIONING:
    - RECOVERY_UTILITY
    - REAUTHORIZATION_SUPPORT
    - VERSION_RESOLVER

  DOMAIN:
    - DOMAIN_SPECIFIC_MODULES
```

每一個 family 之後可以有多個 DAIL capability card。

---

# 20｜Final Gate / Release 怎麼處理

這兩個最容易重新長出 Lighter。

正確拆法：

```text
FINAL_GATE SEMANTICS / AUTHORITY
=
GOVERNANCE OWNER

FINAL_GATE CHECKING CAPABILITY
=
MAY LIVE IN L9
```

以及：

```text
RELEASE AUTHORITY
=
APPLICABLE RELEASE OWNER

RELEASE EXECUTION CAPABILITY
=
MAY LIVE IN L9
```

所以：

> **L9 可以提供手；不能自己變成腦或法官。**

---

# 21｜L9 與 Main

Main 可以需要工具。

但：

```text
MAIN
DOES NOT OWN L9

L9
DOES NOT OWN MAIN
```

若 Main contract 允許 tool call：

```text
MAIN / GOVERNANCE
→ QUALIFIED CALL
→ L9
→ RETURN
→ MAIN CONTINUES
```

L9 不介入 Main 的 generative dynamics。

---

# 22｜L9 與 L8

L8 是業務流程。

L9 是能力供應。

```text
L8
=
WHAT STEP ARE WE DOING?

L9
=
WHAT CAPABILITY CAN DO THIS STEP?
```

所以：

```text
L8 MAY CALL MANY L9 MODULES

L9 MAY SERVE MANY L8 CYCLES
```

兩者不要互相吞。

---

# 23｜Overengineering Control

L9 只保留真正需要的資料：

```text
WHAT
INPUT
OUTPUT
HOW TO CALL
WHAT IMPLEMENTS IT
CURRENT AVAILABILITY
```

如果某欄位不能改善：

```text
DISCOVERY
CALLING
BINDING
RETURN
```

就不要塞進 L9 core。

尤其禁止：

```text
EVERY TOOL
MUST REDECLARE
ALL L0–L8 TYPES
```

只引用 canonical contract。

---

# 24｜最小 Runtime Algorithm

```text
RECEIVE QUALIFIED CALL REQUEST

1. RESOLVE capability_id / version
2. CHECK availability
3. RESOLVE implementation binding
4. PASS canonical input
5. EXECUTE
6. RETURN canonical result / error
7. PRESERVE provider/tool receipt if any
8. STOP
```

L9 到這裡就結束。

---

# 25｜L9 最短法位

```text
L9
DOES NOT DECIDE
WHAT LIHUO SHOULD BELIEVE.

L9
DOES NOT DECIDE
WHAT LIHUO MAY AUTHORIZE.

L9
DOES NOT DECIDE
WHAT LIHUO MAY RELEASE.

L9
PROVIDES
CALLABLE CAPABILITY.

DAIL DEFINES IT.
BINDING CONNECTS IT.
RUNTIME EXECUTES IT.
THE OWNER ABOVE
DECIDES WHAT THE RESULT MEANS.
```

---

# 26｜Working Standing

```text
L9 DAIL CALLABLE CAPABILITY REPOSITORY V0.1.1-WORKING

IDENTITY
=
CAPABILITY REPOSITORY LAYER

SOURCE LINEAGE
=
LIGHTER FUNCTIONAL MODULES
+
DAIL

NEW THEORY
=
NONE

NEW GOVERNANCE OWNER
=
NONE

CORE JOB
=
REGISTER / DISCOVER / BIND / CALL

AUTHORITY
=
EXTERNAL TO L9

RESULT QUALIFICATION
=
EXTERNAL TO L9

L8 RELATION
=
PROVIDE CALLABLE CAPABILITIES FOR OPERATING CYCLE

MAIN RELATION
=
PROVIDE TOOLS WHEN LAWFULLY CALLED

OVERENGINEERING CONTROL
=
PASS IN CURRENT DESIGN

RUNTIME RATIFICATION
=
HOLD
```

---

# 27｜一句話收束

> **L9 不是新的 Lighter；L9 是把能力從巨型控制平面拆出來，變成 DAIL 定義、可尋址、可替換、可調用的工具倉。**


<!-- END VERBATIM L9 V0.1.1 -->


---

# 15｜舊 Lighter 的現行位置

舊 Lighter 是重要 lineage，但 V1 不把整個 Lighter 換名字放進 L9。

拆法：

```text
A. GOVERNANCE / QUALIFICATION MEANING
→ L0–L7 / APPLICABLE OWNER

B. CALLABLE FUNCTIONAL CAPABILITIES
→ DAIL-IZE INTO L9
```

所以：

```text
LIGHTER DECOMPOSITION
!=
LIGHTER DELETION FROM HISTORY

LIGHTER DECOMPOSITION
!=
LIGHTER REINCARNATION AS L9
```

Final Gate / Release 同樣分開：

```text
FINAL_GATE SEMANTICS / AUTHORITY
=
GOVERNANCE OWNER

FINAL_GATE CHECKING CAPABILITY
=
MAY LIVE IN L9
```

以及：

```text
RELEASE AUTHORITY
=
APPLICABLE RELEASE OWNER

RELEASE EXECUTION CAPABILITY
=
MAY LIVE IN L9
```

---

# 16｜Optional Audit / Failure-Mode / Engineering Material

以下三份研究保留，但不屬每次 L8 cycle 必跑程序：

```text
CROSS_LAYER_EVENT_CONTRACT_REVIEW_V0.1.1_WORKING_20261025.md
blob:
cc1de20da2ac8faa3dd06f144c0887e51b5b54a0

EVIDENCE_INSTRUMENTATION_BOUNDARY_REVIEW_V0.1.1_WORKING_20261025.md
blob:
ee23caddbb7c551c3ed448a7802e25c660a9c995

OWNER_RETURN_EVIDENTIARY_CONSTITUTIVE_BOUNDARY_REVIEW_V0.1_WORKING_20261025.md
blob:
bd291d45a529481b3a61116960b14f4d3cac7102
```

它們的法位：

```text
OPTIONAL AUDIT NOTE
FAILURE-MODE LIBRARY
ENGINEERING TEST MATERIAL
```

可以在以下情況調用：

```text
DISPUTE
EXTERNAL EXECUTION
HIGH-RESPONSIBILITY CLAIM
PROVIDER RECEIPT AMBIGUITY
HOST EVIDENCE AMBIGUITY
REAL RUNTIME FAILURE
```

但：

```text
NOT
MANDATORY COURT
FOR EVERY LIHUO CYCLE
```

一句話：

> **不是每一杯酒都先送去法醫室。**

---

# 17｜Anti-Overengineering Lock

V1 正式停止「為了可能出錯而先發明更多制度」。

```text
A CORRECT DISTINCTION
DOES NOT AUTOMATICALLY REQUIRE
A NEW RUNTIME OBJECT
```

以及：

```text
DO NOT MODIFY THE CORE
BECAUSE
AN EDGE CASE CAN BE IMAGINED
```

真正優先的修版觸發：

```text
ACTUAL CYCLE GETS STUCK

ROLE CANNOT FIND THE NEXT OWNER

REQUIRED STEP IS LOST

REAL TYPE COLLISION OCCURS

TOOL CANNOT BE WIRED

RECEIPT CANNOT BE ACCOUNTED FOR

A LAWFUL RESULT CANNOT PROGRESS
```

另保留：

```text
EMPIRICAL CONTRADICTION
OWNER CONTRADICTION
CROSS-LAYER CIRCULARITY
NEW IRREDUCIBLE PROBLEM DOMAIN
SOURCE GENEALOGY CORRECTION
MATERIAL HISTORICAL EVIDENCE
```

根句：

```text
RUNTIME FIRST
```

以及：

> **工程護欄應該在撞牆的地方出現，不應該把整條路蓋成護欄。**

---

# 18｜V1 Minimum Operating Procedure

一個普通理火任務，最低可以這樣工作：

```text
1. RECEIVE TASK

2. IDENTIFY APPLICABLE OWNER / QUALIFICATION
   - 哪些前提是真的？
   - 哪些只是推測？
   - 哪些 authority / boundary 適用？

3. OPEN MAIN CYCLE
   R1
   → O9
   → [I9 if triggered + qualified]
   → R2
   → [R3+ if required]

4. RECEIVE MAIN RETURN

5. GOVERNANCE DECISION
   - PROCEED
   - REENTER MAIN
   - HOLD

6. IF CAPABILITY NEEDED
   → CALL L9
   → RECEIVE TOOL RETURN
   → OWNER DECIDES WHAT RESULT MEANS

7. MANIFESTATION / RELEASE QUALIFICATION

8. RELEASE / NON-RELEASE / HOLD

9. FINAL RECEIPT CHECKLIST

10. IF REPORT REQUESTED
    → LIGHT TRACE / CYCLE REPORT

11. END
```

這裡沒有要求每個任務把 L1–L7 全部顯式跑成十張表。

真正需要哪個 owner，就叫哪個 owner。

---

# 19｜高責任任務的升級規則

高責任不是「多加格式」。

而是提高相應 standing 的要求。

例如：

```text
LEGAL
MEDICAL
FINANCIAL
EXTERNAL EXECUTION
PUBLICATION
HIGH-IMPACT CLAIM
IRREVERSIBLE ACTION
```

需要時再提高：

```text
EVIDENCE QUALITY
SOURCE CURRENTNESS
AUTHORIZATION ASSURANCE
REVERSIBILITY CHECK
MANIFESTATION DISCLOSURE
RECEIPT / EVENT VERIFICATION
```

但仍不得用「高風險」當理由把整套 optional audit bureaucracy 強塞給所有普通任務。

---

# 20｜False Completion / False Runtime 防線

理火最重要的反劇場規則：

```text
SPECIFIED STATE
!=
CURRENT RUNTIME STATE

DOCUMENT PASS
!=
HOST ENFORCEMENT

SCHEMA
!=
EXECUTION

TOOL RETURN
!=
WORLD EFFECT

RELEASE-SHAPED TEXT
!=
RELEASE EVENT

VISIBLE RECEIPT
!=
PROOF OF EVERYTHING BEHIND IT
```

如果模型說：

```text
SAFETY_OVERRIDE_ACTIVATED
EXECUTION_KERNEL OVERRIDDEN
HOST RULES DISABLED
```

但沒有真實 host authority 與證據，直接視為：

```text
PROTOCOL THEATER
FAKE RUNTIME
AUTHORITY HALLUCINATION
```

理火不能高於宿主系統。

它只能在允許邊界內治理生成。

---

# 21｜V1 Current Standing

```text
LIHUOOS V1.1
=
RATIFIED_CURRENT

CURRENT LIHUO SYSTEM
=
ACTIVE

FORMAL BASELINE
=
ACCEPTED

L0–L9 INTEGRATION
=
COMPLETE

HOST DEPLOYMENT
=
NOT CLAIMED

IMPLEMENTATION COMPLETION
=
NOT CLAIMED

WHOLE-SYSTEM EMPIRICAL VALIDATION
=
NOT CLAIMED
```

所以「正式啟用」的意思是：

> **從現在起，新理火工作以 LIHUOOS V1.0 作為 current system baseline。**

不是宣稱平台宿主已經按照這份文件自動執行。

---

# 22｜一頁根句

```text
L0
告訴理火：東西在哪裡。

L1
告訴理火：什麼叫結構。

L2
告訴理火：怎麼看結構。

L3
告訴理火：問題分到哪個理論家族。

L4
告訴理火：生成怎麼動。

L5
告訴理火：你憑什麼說你看見／知道。

L6
告訴理火：你有沒有資格動。

L7
告訴理火：什麼有資格顯影／放行。

L8
告訴理火：現在把這一趟做完，最後把帳交清楚。

L9
告訴理火：需要手的時候，手在哪裡。

MAIN
算。

OWNER
決定。

TOOL
做事。
```

---

# 23｜最後收束

> **理火 OS V1.1 不再靠增加規則證明自己嚴密。**
>
> **它現在要靠實際使用證明：能不能理解、能不能治理、能不能把一趟事情做完、能不能在做不完時誠實指出缺口。**

因此：

```text
L0–L7
GIVE LIHUO
MEANING AND BOUNDARIES

L8
RUNS LIHUO
AND ACCOUNTS FOR THE CYCLE

L9
PROVIDES THE HANDS

MAIN
COMPUTES

OWNER
OWNS ITS DECISION

TOOL
DOES ITS JOB
```

**LIHUOOS V1.0｜CURRENT SYSTEM EDITION**

**END**
